import { defineConfig } from "drizzle-kit";

if (!process.env.DATABASE_URL) {
  throw new Error("DATABASE_URL, ensure the database is provisioned");
}

export default defineConfig({
  out: "./migrations",
  schema: "./shared/schema.ts",
  dialect: "postgresql",
  dbCredentials: {
    url: process.env.DATABASE_URL,
  },
});

# [NOTE: POTENTIAL SENSITIVE DATA REDACTED BY USER BEFORE UPLOAD]
# CryptoPulse Live - Predictive Signal Terminal

## Overview

CryptoPulse Live is a "Predictive Signal Terminal" designed for Twitch streaming overlays, offering real-time cryptocurrency data and advanced analytics. It displays live price data, sentiment analysis, whale transaction alerts, prediction market odds, liquidation data, AI market insights, and advanced signal detection for the top 15 cryptocurrencies by 24h trading volume (refreshed every 4 hours). Only these top 15 are eligible for trading. The project aims to provide comprehensive, actionable insights with a cyberpunk-themed visual design, leveraging multiple external APIs and AI-powered analysis to identify market opportunities and risks.

## User Preferences

Preferred communication style: Simple, everyday language.

## System Architecture

### Core Design Principles
The system prioritizes real-time data delivery, robust caching, and a modular architecture. Sentiment insights are data-driven, displaying "No Current Signal" if data is unavailable. A composite sentiment algorithm is used for non-BTC cryptocurrencies, incorporating price momentum, volume, and BTC sentiment as a market proxy.

### Frontend
- **Framework**: React 18 with TypeScript
- **State Management**: TanStack React Query for server state, React hooks for local state
- **Styling**: Tailwind CSS with a cyberpunk neon theme, utilizing shadcn/ui and Radix UI components
- **Animations**: Framer Motion
- **Build Tool**: Vite

### Backend
- **Runtime**: Node.js with Express 5 (TypeScript, ESM modules)
- **Real-time Communication**: Native WebSocket (`ws` library) for live data updates
- **API Pattern**: REST endpoints for initial data, WebSocket for continuous updates
- **Data Services**: Separate, cached modules for each external API to manage rate limits and optimize data fetching. Caching is in-memory with configurable TTLs (e.g., CoinGecko: 30s, SentiCrypt: 120s, AI Insights: 300s).

### Data Flow
Backend services fetch and cache data from external APIs, then broadcast updates to connected frontend clients via WebSocket. The frontend processes these updates reactively, including client-side composite sentiment calculation for non-BTC assets.

### Database
- **ORM**: Drizzle ORM configured for PostgreSQL
- **Schema**: Defined using Drizzle ORM and Zod for runtime validation, primarily for defining data types.

### Key Features

**Predictive Signal Terminal Features**:
- **Divergence Detector**: Identifies potential reversals by comparing price action against sentiment.
- **Whale Net Flow Gauge**: Visualizes whale accumulation/distribution patterns.
- **BTC Correlation Delta Tracker**: Monitors changes in altcoin correlation with Bitcoin.
- **AI Market Analysis**: Generates periodic market insights using Google Gemini 2.0 Flash.
- **AI Price Predictions**: Provides multi-timeframe price forecasts using quantitative indicators (RSI, MACD, Bollinger Bands).
- **Prediction Markets**: Displays odds from Polymarket.
- **Liquidation Walls**: Highlights significant liquidation clusters on Binance Futures.
- **Capital Flow Delta**: Tracks stablecoin supply changes and cross-chain bridge volumes to gauge market capital movement.
- **On-Chain Valuation**: Approximates MVRV Z-Score and NUPL metrics.

**AI Paper Trading System**:
- Autonomous trading system using real price feeds with quantitative strategies.

**3-Mode Adaptive Strategy** (switches based on Mike1 metric every 4 hours):
1. **Mean Reversion Mode** - Choppy/Sideways markets (70% of the time)
   - Makes small consistent profits when the market is doing "nothing"
   - Trades extended prices expecting snap-back to mean
   - Entry requires |Z-Score| > 1.5 (extended price), target is mean (SMA100)
   
2. **Volatility Expansion Mode** - Strong Trend/Breakout markets
   - Captures the massive 10-20% moves when the market "explodes" out of a range
   - Entry: Price > Upper Keltner Channel AND Volatility Rising (ATR expanding)
   - Keltner Channel: EMA(20) ± ATR(10) × 2.0 multiplier
   
3. **Pairs Trading Mode** - Bear/Uncertain markets (hedged)
   - Hedges market risk via statistical arbitrage
   - Makes money when market crashes but Long asset crashes less than Short asset
   - Uses OLS Hedge Ratio (β) for proper position sizing
   - Cointegration test (p-value < 0.05) for pair validation
   - "Broken Leash Kill Switch" exits when Z-score > 3.5 (correlation breakdown)

**Money Management**:
- **Mike1 Metric**: Sharpe Ratio / (Ulcer Index + 0.001) calculated on rolling 50 trades
- **Kelly Criterion**: Position sizing capped at max margin (25% fractional Kelly)
- **Fixed 1:1.5 R:R Ratio**: Stop at Risk=1 Unit, Take Profit at Reward=1.5 Units

**Weighted Conviction Allocation Algorithm**:
- Calculate Mike1 for all 3 modes over last 50 trades
- Filter: Discard strategies with Mike1 < 0 (never allocate to losers)
- Dynamic Weighting based on Dominance Ratio:
  - High Conviction (2x better): 100% to winner ("Go for the kill")
  - Strong Winner (1.5x better): 80/20 split
  - Close Race (<1.5x): 60/40 split (hedges regime transition risk)

**4-Hour Adaptive Lookback**: Every 4 hours, recalculates Mike1 scores and adjusts allocation weights.

## External Dependencies

### APIs
- **CoinGecko API**: Cryptocurrency price and market data.
- **SentiCrypt API**: Bitcoin sentiment analysis.
- **Helius API**: Solana blockchain whale transaction tracking.
- **Polymarket Gamma API**: Prediction market data.
- **Binance Futures API**: Liquidation data.
- **DeFiLlama API**: Stablecoin supply and bridge volume data.
- **OpenAI API**: AI market analysis (via Replit AI Integrations).
- **Google Gemini API**: For AI market analysis.

### Database
- **PostgreSQL**: Used with Drizzle ORM for schema definition.

### Key NPM Packages
- `drizzle-orm`, `drizzle-zod`: ORM and schema validation.
- `ws`: WebSocket server.
- `@tanstack/react-query`: Server state management.
- `framer-motion`: Animations.

### Environment Variables
- `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string.
- `COINGECKO_API_KEY`: Required for cryptocurrency price data (Demo or Pro key).
- `COINGECKO_IS_PRO`: Set to "true" if using a Pro API key (uses pro-api.coingecko.com).
- `HELIUS_API_KEY`: Required for whale transaction tracking.
- `GEMINI_API_KEY`: Required for Google Gemini AI.
- `AI_INTEGRATIONS_OPENAI_API_KEY`: For OpenAI API access.

### Data Source Notes
- **CoinGecko Demo API**: Uses 30-day OHLC range (provides 4H candles). 90-day returns only daily candles.
- **Binance API**: Blocked from Replit locations (error 451 geographic restriction).
- **Synthetic Warmup**: Runs 30-day backtest on startup to calculate Mike1 scores for strategy allocation.

### Crypto Selection (Updated Feb 7, 2026)
**Shared Source of Truth**: `getTop12TradableCryptos()` in `server/services/coingecko.ts` returns top 15 by volume. Used by:
- Synthetic warmup (backtest)
- `/api/warmup-status` endpoint (UI display)
- AI Trader signal generation and trade execution (only top 15 are eligible)

**Exclusion Lists**:
- Stablecoins: usdt, usdc, dai, busd, tusd, usdp, fdusd, usd1
- Wrapped/Derivative tokens: wrapped-solana, weth, wbnb, wrapped-bitcoin, l2-standard-bridged-weth-base
- Other excluded: binance-bridged-usdt-bnb-smart-chain, tether-gold, pax-gold, usd1-wlfi

**Deduplication**: By symbol to prevent showing both SOL and Wrapped SOL.

**PnL Display**: `formatPnl()` applies $0.02 minimum threshold across all P&L displays (total, unrealized, positions, trade history, stats).

### Entry Gate Logic (Updated Jan 30, 2026)

**Mean Reversion "Falling Knife Protocol":**
- Normal entry: ADX < 30 AND Z-Score > 1.5 (extended price in ranging market)
- Panic crash bypass: Z-Score > 3.5 ignores ADX filter entirely (pure statistical extremity)

**Vol Expansion "Rocket Protocol":**
- Z-Score cap raised from 2.0 to 5.0 (allows entries during strong trends)
- Volatility check: accepts rising vol (10%+ increase) OR sustained high vol (1.5x average)
- Keltner breakout + vol condition required for entry

**Momentum Fallback Safety Net:**
- Triggers when primary strategies generate no signals
- Entry condition: 4%+ candle body AND (3x volatility surge OR 8%+ 24h change)
- Catches obvious moves that complex filters might block

### Trade Accounting (Fixed Jan 31, 2026)

**PnL Percent Base Consistency:**
All pnlPercent calculations use entry-price base (quantity × entryPrice) for consistency:
- Unrealized: `currentPositionMargin = position.quantity * position.entryPrice`
- Partial close chunk: `chunkMargin = quantityToClose * position.entryPrice`
- Partial close cumulative: `originalMargin = trade.quantity * trade.entryPrice`
- Full close (remaining): `remainingMargin = position.quantity * position.entryPrice`
- Full close (total): `originalMargin = trade.quantity * trade.entryPrice`

**Zero Guards:** All five pnlPercent calculations include zero guards to prevent NaN/divide-by-zero.

**Partial Close Accumulation:**
- Each partial close adds to cumulative pnl, fees, slippage, and pnlPercent in trade record
- Final close adds remaining pnl to accumulated totals (not overwrite)
- Rolling metrics use chunk pnlPercent (per-close datapoints)
- Learning samples use total pnlPercent (whole-trade performance)

**Fee Accounting:**
- Entry fees: Deducted from balance at open, stored in `trade.entryFees`
- Exit fees: Deducted from PnL at each close (partial and final)
- `trade.fees`: Total fees (entry + all exit fees)
- `trade.pnl`: Net PnL after ALL fees (entry + exit) - calculated at final close as `grossPnl - entryFees`
- Slippage: Accumulated across all closes in `trade.slippage`

### Mode Stats Panel (Added Feb 4, 2026)

**Dashboard Component**: `ModeStatsPanel.tsx` displays live strategy performance metrics.
- Shows Sharpe Ratio, Ulcer Index, and Mike1 score for each strategy mode
- Displays allocation status (Active/Runner Up/Blocked) with percentages
- Market regime interpretation based on active strategy
- Updates every 30 seconds via `/api/mode-stats` endpoint

**API Endpoint**: `GET /api/mode-stats` returns:
- Strategy stats: trades, wins, winRate, sharpe, ulcer, mike1, warmupMike1
- Allocation: primary/secondary strategies with weights
- Regime interpretation text

### Execution Cost Optimizations (Added Feb 4, 2026)

**Per-Symbol Trade Cooldown**:
- 5-minute (300s) minimum between trades on same symbol
- Prevents "machine gun" re-entry loops after closes
- Tracked in `symbolCooldownMap` in paperTrading.ts

**Minimum Profit Target Filter**:
- Skips trades where target distance < 0.8%
- Ensures potential profit exceeds execution costs (fees + slippage)
- Logged as "Target distance too small" when filtered

**Limit Orders for Take-Profit**:
- Take-profit exits use limit orders (maker fee 0.02%)
- Stop-loss exits use market orders (taker fee 0.04%)
- Reduces exit costs by ~50% on profitable trades

### Execution Bug Fixes (Updated Feb 5, 2026)

**Machine Gun Bug Prevention (Dual-Layer Lock System)**:
- **In-Memory Cache** (`cooldownCache` Map): Catches same-millisecond calls within process
- **File-Based Lock** (`/tmp/trade_cooldown_lock.json`): Persists across restarts
- **Atomic File Writes**: Uses temp file + rename pattern to prevent corruption
- **Error Recovery**: Corrupted lock files are automatically reset with logging
- Cooldown is SET at the very start of openPosition() - BEFORE any async operations
- 5-minute cooldown per symbol after successful trade open
- Dual-layer approach ensures reliability in high-speed async trading

**Cooldown Clear on Validation Failure**:
- If trade fails validation (margin, exposure, notional, etc.), cooldown is CLEARED
- This allows retry with next valid opportunity instead of blocking for 5 minutes
- Machine-gun prevention still works because same-millisecond calls are blocked

**Minimum Profit Gate (0.8%)**:
- Rejects trades where `abs(takeProfit - targetEntry) / targetEntry < 0.008`
- Ensures potential profit exceeds execution costs (fees ~0.1% + slippage ~0.1%)
- Logged as `[0.8% GATE] REJECTED` when filtered

**Minimum Notional Value ($10)**:
- Broker minimum trade size enforced at $10
- Prevents undersized orders that brokers would reject
- Logged as `[MIN NOTIONAL] PASSED/REJECTED`

**Initial Balance**:
- Increased from $100 to $500 for realistic paper trading with multiple positions

/**
 * Reusable AudioWorklet for streaming PCM16 audio playback.
 * Place in public/ folder and load via audioContext.audioWorklet.addModule()
 */
class RingBuffer {
  constructor(initialCapacity) {
    this.capacity = initialCapacity;
    this.buffer = new Float32Array(initialCapacity);
    this.readIndex = 0;
    this.writeIndex = 0;
    this.availableData = 0;
  }

  push(data) {
    const len = data.length;
    // Auto-grow if needed
    while (this.availableData + len > this.capacity) {
      this.grow();
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      this.buffer[this.writeIndex] = data[i];
      this.writeIndex = (this.writeIndex + 1) % this.capacity;
      this.availableData++;
    }
  }

  grow() {
    const newCapacity = this.capacity * 2;
    const newBuffer = new Float32Array(newCapacity);
    // Copy existing data maintaining order
    for (let i = 0; i < this.availableData; i++) {
      const srcIndex = (this.readIndex + i) % this.capacity;
      newBuffer[i] = this.buffer[srcIndex];
    }
    this.buffer = newBuffer;
    this.readIndex = 0;
    this.writeIndex = this.availableData;
    this.capacity = newCapacity;
  }

  pull(outputBuffer) {
    const len = outputBuffer.length;
    const available = Math.min(len, this.availableData);
    for (let i = 0; i < available; i++) {
      outputBuffer[i] = this.buffer[this.readIndex];
      this.readIndex = (this.readIndex + 1) % this.capacity;
    }
    // Pad remaining with silence
    for (let i = available; i < len; i++) {
      outputBuffer[i] = 0;
    }
    this.availableData -= available;
    return available > 0;
  }

  available() {
    return this.availableData;
  }

  clear() {
    this.readIndex = 0;
    this.writeIndex = 0;
    this.availableData = 0;
  }
}

class AudioPlaybackProcessor extends AudioWorkletProcessor {
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.ringBuffer = new RingBuffer(24000 * 30); // 30s initial capacity
    this.isPlaying = false;
    this.streamComplete = false;

    this.port.onmessage = (event) => {
      const { type, samples } = event.data;
      if (type === "audio") {
        this.ringBuffer.push(samples);
        this.isPlaying = true;
      } else if (type === "clear") {
        this.ringBuffer.clear();
        this.isPlaying = false;
        this.streamComplete = false;
      } else if (type === "streamComplete") {
        this.streamComplete = true;
      } else if (type === "stop") {
        this.isPlaying = false;
        this.streamComplete = false;
      }
    };
  }

  process(inputs, outputs) {
    const output = outputs[0];
    if (!output || output.length === 0) return true;

    const channel = output[0];
    if (this.isPlaying) {
      this.ringBuffer.pull(channel);
      if (this.streamComplete && this.ringBuffer.available() === 0) {
        this.isPlaying = false;
        this.streamComplete = false;
        this.port.postMessage({ type: "ended" });
      }
    } else {
      channel.fill(0);
    }
    return true;
  }
}

registerProcessor("audio-playback-processor", AudioPlaybackProcessor);

START OF FILE: ./client/replit_integrations/audio/audio-utils.ts

/**
 * Audio utility functions for voice chat.
 * Handles PCM16 decoding and AudioContext initialization.
 */

/**
 * Decode base64 PCM16 audio to Float32Array for Web Audio API
 */
export function decodePCM16ToFloat32(base64Audio: string): Float32Array {
  const raw = atob(base64Audio);
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(raw.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {
    bytes[i] = raw.charCodeAt(i);
  }
  const pcm16 = new Int16Array(bytes.buffer);
  const float32 = new Float32Array(pcm16.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < pcm16.length; i++) {
    float32[i] = pcm16[i] / 32768;
  }
  return float32;
}

/**
 * Create and initialize AudioContext with worklet
 */
export async function createAudioPlaybackContext(
  workletPath = "/audio-playback-worklet.js",
  sampleRate = 24000
): Promise<{ ctx: AudioContext; worklet: AudioWorkletNode }> {
  const ctx = new AudioContext({ sampleRate });
  await ctx.audioWorklet.addModule(workletPath);
  const worklet = new AudioWorkletNode(ctx, "audio-playback-processor");
  worklet.connect(ctx.destination);
  return { ctx, worklet };
}

/**
 * Voice chat client utilities for Replit AI Integrations.
 * 
 * Usage:
 * 1. Copy audio-playback-worklet.js to your public/ folder
 * 2. Import and use the React hooks in your components
 * 
 * Example:
 * ```tsx
 * import { useVoiceRecorder, useVoiceStream } from "./audio";
 * 
 * function VoiceChat() {
 *   const [transcript, setTranscript] = useState("");
 *   const recorder = useVoiceRecorder();
 *   const stream = useVoiceStream({
 *     onTranscript: (_, full) => setTranscript(full),
 *     onComplete: (text) => console.log("Done:", text),
 *   });
 * 
 *   const handleClick = async () => {
 *     if (recorder.state === "recording") {
 *       const blob = await recorder.stopRecording();
 *       await stream.streamVoiceResponse("/api/voice-conversations/1/messages", blob);
 *     } else {
 *       await recorder.startRecording();
 *     }
 *   };
 * 
 *   return (
 *     <div>
 *       <button onClick={handleClick}>
 *         {recorder.state === "recording" ? "Stop" : "Record"}
 *       </button>
 *       <p>{transcript}</p>
 *     </div>
 *   );
 * }
 * ```
 */

START OF FILE: ./client/replit_integrations/audio/useAudioPlayback.ts

/**
 * React hook for streaming audio playback using AudioWorklet.
 * Supports real-time PCM16 audio streaming from SSE responses.
 * Includes sequence buffer for reordering out-of-order chunks.
 */
import { useRef, useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { decodePCM16ToFloat32 } from "./audio-utils";

export type PlaybackState = "idle" | "playing" | "ended";

/**
 * Reorders audio chunks that may arrive out of sequence.
 * Buffers chunks until they can be played in correct order.
 *
 * Example: If chunks arrive as seq 2, seq 0, seq 1:
 * - seq 2 arrives → buffered (waiting for seq 0)
 * - seq 0 arrives → played immediately, then check buffer
 * - seq 1 arrives → played immediately (seq 0 done), seq 2 now plays
 */
class SequenceBuffer {
  private pending = new Map<number, string[]>();
  private nextSeq = 0;

  /** Add chunk with sequence number, returns chunks ready to play in order */
  push(seq: number, data: string): string[] {
    // Store the chunk under its sequence number
    if (!this.pending.has(seq)) {
      this.pending.set(seq, []);
    }
    this.pending.get(seq)!.push(data);

    // Drain consecutive ready sequences
    const ready: string[] = [];
    while (this.pending.has(this.nextSeq)) {
      ready.push(...this.pending.get(this.nextSeq)!);
      this.pending.delete(this.nextSeq);
      this.nextSeq++;
    }
    return ready;
  }

  reset() {
    this.pending.clear();
    this.nextSeq = 0;
  }
}

export function useAudioPlayback(workletPath = "/audio-playback-worklet.js") {
  const [state, setState] = useState<PlaybackState>("idle");
  const ctxRef = useRef<AudioContext | null>(null);
  const workletRef = useRef<AudioWorkletNode | null>(null);
  const readyRef = useRef(false);
  const seqBufferRef = useRef(new SequenceBuffer());

  const init = useCallback(async () => {
    if (readyRef.current) return;

    const ctx = new AudioContext({ sampleRate: 24000 });
    await ctx.audioWorklet.addModule(workletPath);
    const worklet = new AudioWorkletNode(ctx, "audio-playback-processor");
    worklet.connect(ctx.destination);

    worklet.port.onmessage = (e) => {
      if (e.data.type === "ended") setState("idle");
    };

    ctxRef.current = ctx;
    workletRef.current = worklet;
    readyRef.current = true;
  }, [workletPath]);

  /** Push audio directly (no sequencing) - for simple streaming */
  const pushAudio = useCallback((base64Audio: string) => {
    if (!workletRef.current) return;
    const samples = decodePCM16ToFloat32(base64Audio);
    workletRef.current.port.postMessage({ type: "audio", samples });
    setState("playing");
  }, []);

  /** Push audio with sequence number - reorders before playback */
  const pushSequencedAudio = useCallback((seq: number, base64Audio: string) => {
    if (!workletRef.current) return;

    const readyChunks = seqBufferRef.current.push(seq, base64Audio);
    for (const chunk of readyChunks) {
      const samples = decodePCM16ToFloat32(chunk);
      workletRef.current.port.postMessage({ type: "audio", samples });
    }
    if (readyChunks.length > 0) {
      setState("playing");
    }
  }, []);

  const signalComplete = useCallback(() => {
    workletRef.current?.port.postMessage({ type: "streamComplete" });
  }, []);

  const clear = useCallback(() => {
    workletRef.current?.port.postMessage({ type: "clear" });
    seqBufferRef.current.reset();
    setState("idle");
  }, []);

  return { state, init, pushAudio, pushSequencedAudio, signalComplete, clear };
}

START OF FILE: ./client/replit_integrations/audio/useVoiceRecorder.ts
====================
/**
 * React hook for voice recording using MediaRecorder API.
 * Records audio in WebM/Opus format for efficient streaming.
 */
import { useRef, useCallback, useState } from "react";

export type RecordingState = "idle" | "recording" | "stopped";

export function useVoiceRecorder() {
  const [state, setState] = useState<RecordingState>("idle");
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const chunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);

  const startRecording = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {
    const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
    const recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, {
      mimeType: "audio/webm;codecs=opus",
    });

    mediaRecorderRef.current = recorder;
    chunksRef.current = [];

    recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
      if (e.data.size > 0) chunksRef.current.push(e.data);
    };

    recorder.start(100); // Collect chunks every 100ms
    setState("recording");
  }, []);

  const stopRecording = useCallback((): Promise<Blob> => {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const recorder = mediaRecorderRef.current;
      if (!recorder || recorder.state !== "recording") {
        resolve(new Blob());
        return;
      }

      recorder.onstop = () => {
        const blob = new Blob(chunksRef.current, { type: "audio/webm" });
        recorder.stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
        setState("stopped");
        resolve(blob);
      };

      recorder.stop();
    });
  }, []);

  return { state, startRecording, stopRecording };
}

START OF FILE: ./client/replit_integrations/audio/useVoiceStream.ts
====================
/**
 * React hook for handling SSE voice streaming responses.
 * Converts audio blob to base64 and sends as JSON to match server expectations.
 */
import { useCallback } from "react";
import { useAudioPlayback } from "./useAudioPlayback";

interface StreamCallbacks {
  onUserTranscript?: (text: string) => void;
  onTranscript?: (text: string, full: string) => void;
  onComplete?: (transcript: string) => void;
  onError?: (error: Error) => void;
}

export function useVoiceStream(callbacks: StreamCallbacks = {}) {
  const playback = useAudioPlayback();

  const streamVoiceResponse = useCallback(
    async (url: string, audioBlob: Blob) => {
      await playback.init();
      playback.clear();

      // Convert blob to base64 for JSON body (server expects express.json())
      const base64Audio = await new Promise<string>((resolve) => {
        const fileReader = new FileReader();
        fileReader.onload = () => {
          const result = fileReader.result as string;
          resolve(result.split(",")[1]); // Remove data URL prefix
        };
        fileReader.readAsDataURL(audioBlob);
      });

      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ audio: base64Audio }),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Voice request failed");

      const streamReader = response.body?.getReader();
      if (!streamReader) throw new Error("No response body");

      const decoder = new TextDecoder();
      let buffer = "";
      let fullTranscript = "";

      while (true) {
        const { done, value } = await streamReader.read();
        if (done) break;

        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const lines = buffer.split("\n");
        buffer = lines.pop() || "";

        for (const line of lines) {
          if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;

          try {
            const event = JSON.parse(line.slice(6));

            switch (event.type) {
              case "user_transcript":
                callbacks.onUserTranscript?.(event.data);
                break;
              case "transcript":
                fullTranscript += event.data;
                callbacks.onTranscript?.(event.data, fullTranscript);
                break;
              case "audio":
                playback.pushAudio(event.data);
                break;
              case "done":
                playback.signalComplete();
                callbacks.onComplete?.(fullTranscript);
                break;
              case "error":
                throw new Error(event.error);
            }
          } catch (e) {
            if (!(e instanceof SyntaxError)) {
              callbacks.onError?.(e as Error);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    },
    [playback, callbacks]
  );

  return { streamVoiceResponse, playbackState: playback.state };
}

import { Switch, Route } from "wouter";
import { queryClient } from "./lib/queryClient";
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/ErrorBoundary";
import Dashboard from "@/pages/Dashboard";
import NotFound from "@/pages/not-found";

function Router() {
  return (
    <Switch>
      <Route path="/" component={Dashboard} />
      <Route component={NotFound} />
    </Switch>
  );
}

function App() {
  return (
    <ErrorBoundary>
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
        <TooltipProvider>
          <Toaster />
          <Router />
        </TooltipProvider>
      </QueryClientProvider>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

export default App;

START OF FILE: ./client/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
====================
import { Component, ErrorInfo, ReactNode } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

interface Props {
  children: ReactNode;
}

interface State {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
  errorInfo: ErrorInfo | null;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  constructor(props: Props) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State {
    return { hasError: true, error, errorInfo: null };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error("[ErrorBoundary] Caught error:", error, errorInfo);
    this.setState({ error, errorInfo });
  }

  handleReload = () => {
    window.location.reload();
  };

  handleReset = () => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null, errorInfo: null });
  };

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="max-w-md w-full bg-card border border-destructive/50 rounded-lg p-6 text-center">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-16 h-16 text-destructive mx-auto mb-4" />
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-foreground mb-2">
              Something went wrong
            </h1>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground mb-4">
              The application encountered an unexpected error. This is usually temporary.
            </p>
            {this.state.error && (
              <p className="text-xs text-destructive/80 font-mono bg-destructive/10 p-2 rounded mb-4 break-all">
                {this.state.error.message}
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="flex gap-2 justify-center">
              <Button onClick={this.handleReset} variant="outline">
                Try Again
              </Button>
              <Button onClick={this.handleReload}>
                <RefreshCw className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                Reload Page
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

END OF FILE: ./client/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx

START OF FILE: ./client/src/components/dashboard/AIInsightPanel.tsx
====================
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Brain, TrendingUp, TrendingDown, Minus, AlertTriangle, Sparkles, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import type { AIInsight } from "@shared/schema";
import { WidgetTooltip, widgetDescriptions } from "./WidgetTooltip";

interface AIInsightPanelProps {
  insight: AIInsight | null;
  onRefresh?: () => void;
  isLoading?: boolean;
}

export function AIInsightPanel({ insight, onRefresh, isLoading }: AIInsightPanelProps) {
  const [showRefresh, setShowRefresh] = useState(false);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    if (insight) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => setShowRefresh(true), 60000);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }, [insight]);

  if (!insight) {
    return (
      <Card className="p-4 border-border/50 bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-500/10 to-cyan-500/10" data-testid="card-ai-insight-loading">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <Brain className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-400 animate-pulse" />
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-sm text-foreground">
            AI MARKET ANALYSIS
          </h3>
          <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.aiInsight} />
        </div>
        <div className="animate-pulse space-y-2">
          <div className="h-4 bg-muted/30 rounded w-full" />
          <div className="h-4 bg-muted/30 rounded w-3/4" />
        </div>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  const TrendIcon = insight.trend === "bullish" 
    ? TrendingUp 
    : insight.trend === "bearish" 
      ? TrendingDown 
      : Minus;

  const trendColor = insight.trend === "bullish" 
    ? "text-green-400 border-green-500/50" 
    : insight.trend === "bearish" 
      ? "text-red-400 border-red-500/50"
      : "text-yellow-400 border-yellow-500/50";

  const riskColor = insight.riskLevel === "high"
    ? "text-red-400 border-red-500/50"
    : insight.riskLevel === "medium"
      ? "text-yellow-400 border-yellow-500/50"
      : "text-green-400 border-green-500/50";

  return (
    <Card className="p-4 border-border/50 bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-500/10 to-cyan-500/10" data-testid="card-ai-insight">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
        <Brain className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-400" />
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-sm text-foreground">
          AI MARKET ANALYSIS
        </h3>
        <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.aiInsight} />
        <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
          <Badge variant="outline" className={`text-xs ${trendColor}`}>
            <TrendIcon className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
            {insight.trend.toUpperCase()}
          </Badge>
          {showRefresh && onRefresh && (
            <Button
              size="icon"
              variant="ghost"
              className="h-6 w-6"
              onClick={() => {
                setShowRefresh(false);
                onRefresh();
              }}
              disabled={isLoading}
            >
              <RefreshCw className={`w-3 h-3 ${isLoading ? "animate-spin" : ""}`} />
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <p className="text-sm text-foreground mb-3">
        {insight.summary}
      </p>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mb-3">
        <div className="p-2 rounded bg-background/50 border border-border/30">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1">
            <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>Key Signal</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-foreground">
            {insight.keySignal}
          </p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="p-2 rounded bg-background/50 border border-border/30">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>Risk Level</span>
          </div>
          <Badge variant="outline" className={`text-xs ${riskColor}`}>
            {insight.riskLevel.toUpperCase()}
          </Badge>
        </div>
      </div>

      {insight.topOpportunity && (
        <div className="p-2 rounded bg-green-500/10 border border-green-500/30 mb-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-green-400 mb-1">
            <TrendingUp className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>Opportunity</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-green-300">
            {insight.topOpportunity}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {insight.warning && (
        <div className="p-2 rounded bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-red-400 mb-1">
            <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>Warning</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-red-300">
            {insight.warning}
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mt-3 pt-2 border-t border-border/30 flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
          Confidence: {(insight.confidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%
        </span>
        <div className="w-20 h-1.5 bg-muted/30 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
          <div 
            className="h-full bg-purple-400 rounded-full"
            style={{ width: `${insight.confidence * 100}%` }}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Target, TrendingUp, TrendingDown, Minus, Clock, CheckCircle, XCircle, Activity } from "lucide-react";
import type { AIPrediction, PredictionAccuracy } from "@shared/schema";
import { WidgetTooltip, widgetDescriptions } from "./WidgetTooltip";

interface AIPredictionsPanelProps {
  predictions: AIPrediction[];
  resolved: AIPrediction[];
  accuracy: PredictionAccuracy[];
  isLoading?: boolean;
}

const TIMEFRAME_OPTIONS = [
  { value: "10m", label: "10 Minutes" },
  { value: "30m", label: "30 Minutes" },
  { value: "60m", label: "1 Hour" },
  { value: "4h", label: "4 Hours" },
  { value: "24h", label: "24 Hours" },
  { value: "all", label: "All Timeframes" },
];

function formatTimeRemaining(targetTime: string): string {
  const target = new Date(targetTime).getTime();
  const now = Date.now();
  const diff = target - now;
  
  if (diff <= 0) return "Resolving...";
  
  const minutes = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  
  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}h ${minutes % 60}m`;
  return `${minutes}m`;
}

function formatPrice(value: number): string {
  if (value >= 1000) return `$${value.toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })}`;
  if (value >= 1) return `$${value.toFixed(2)}`;
  return `$${value.toFixed(4)}`;
}

export function AIPredictionsPanel({ predictions = [], resolved = [], accuracy = [], isLoading }: AIPredictionsPanelProps) {
  const [selectedTimeframe, setSelectedTimeframe] = useState("10m");
  
  // Defensive: ensure arrays are always arrays
  const safePredictions = Array.isArray(predictions) ? predictions : [];
  const safeResolved = Array.isArray(resolved) ? resolved : [];
  
  const filteredPredictions = selectedTimeframe === "all" 
    ? safePredictions 
    : safePredictions.filter((p) => p?.timeframe === selectedTimeframe);
  
  const filteredResolved = selectedTimeframe === "all"
    ? safeResolved
    : safeResolved.filter((p) => p?.timeframe === selectedTimeframe);
  
  if (safePredictions.length === 0 && safeResolved.length === 0 && !isLoading) {
    return (
      <Card className="p-4 border-border/50 bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500/10 to-purple-500/10" data-testid="card-predictions-empty">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <Target className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-400 animate-pulse" />
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-sm text-foreground">
            AI PRICE PREDICTIONS
          </h3>
          <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.aiPredictions} />
        </div>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">
          Generating predictions...
        </p>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Card className="p-4 border-border/50 bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-500/10 to-purple-500/10" data-testid="card-predictions">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 flex-wrap">
        <Target className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-400" />
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-sm text-foreground">
          AI PRICE PREDICTIONS
        </h3>
        <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.aiPredictions} />
        
        <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
          <Select value={selectedTimeframe} onValueChange={setSelectedTimeframe}>
            <SelectTrigger 
              className="h-7 w-[130px] text-xs bg-background/50 border-cyan-500/30"
              data-testid="select-timeframe"
            >
              <SelectValue placeholder="Timeframe" />
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              {TIMEFRAME_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
                <SelectItem key={opt.value} value={opt.value} data-testid={`option-${opt.value}`}>
                  {opt.label}
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
          
          <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs border-cyan-500/50 text-cyan-400">
            {filteredPredictions.length} ACTIVE
          </Badge>
        </div>
      </div>

      {Array.isArray(accuracy) && accuracy.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-3 p-2 rounded bg-background/50 border border-border/30">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-400" />
            <span className="text-xs font-medium text-foreground">Accuracy Tracking</span>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-5 gap-1">
            {["10m", "30m", "60m", "4h", "24h"].map((tf) => {
              const acc = accuracy.find((a) => a.timeframe === tf);
              const rate = acc?.directionAccuracy || 0;
              const total = acc?.totalPredictions || 0;
              const isSelected = selectedTimeframe === tf || selectedTimeframe === "all";
              return (
                <div 
                  key={tf} 
                  className={`text-center p-1 rounded cursor-pointer transition-colors ${
                    isSelected ? "bg-cyan-500/20 border border-cyan-500/30" : "hover:bg-background/30"
                  }`}
                  onClick={() => setSelectedTimeframe(tf)}
                >
                  <div className="text-[10px] text-foreground/80 mb-1 font-medium">{tf}</div>
                  <div className={`text-xs font-mono font-bold ${
                    rate >= 60 ? "text-green-400" : rate >= 40 ? "text-yellow-400" : total > 0 ? "text-red-400" : "text-foreground/50"
                  }`}>
                    {total > 0 ? `${rate.toFixed(0)}%` : "-"}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[9px] text-foreground/60">{total} total</div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-2 max-h-[280px] overflow-y-auto scrollbar-thin">
        {filteredPredictions.length > 0 ? (
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            {filteredPredictions.map((pred) => (
              <PredictionRow key={pred.id} prediction={pred} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center py-4 text-foreground/60 text-xs">
            No predictions for {TIMEFRAME_OPTIONS.find(o => o.value === selectedTimeframe)?.label}
          </div>
        )}

        {filteredResolved.length > 0 && (
          <div className="pt-2 border-t border-border/30">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <CheckCircle className="w-3 h-3 text-foreground/60" />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-foreground/70 uppercase tracking-wider font-medium">Recent Results</span>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {filteredResolved.slice(0, 4).map((pred) => (
                <ResolvedRow key={pred.id} prediction={pred} />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function PredictionRow({ prediction }: { prediction: AIPrediction }) {
  if (!prediction) return null;
  
  const DirectionIcon = prediction.direction === "up" 
    ? TrendingUp 
    : prediction.direction === "down" 
      ? TrendingDown 
      : Minus;
  
  const predictedChange = prediction.predictedChange ?? 0;
  const confidence = prediction.confidence ?? 0;
  
  const isUp = prediction.direction === "up" || predictedChange > 0.1;
  const isDown = prediction.direction === "down" || predictedChange < -0.1;
  
  const bgColor = isUp
    ? "bg-green-500/15 border-green-500/30 hover:bg-green-500/20"
    : isDown
      ? "bg-red-500/15 border-red-500/30 hover:bg-red-500/20"
      : "bg-background/30 border-border/20 hover:bg-background/50";
  
  const textColor = isUp
    ? "text-green-400"
    : isDown
      ? "text-red-400"
      : "text-yellow-400";

  return (
    <div 
      className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded border transition-colors ${bgColor}`}
      data-testid={`prediction-${prediction.id}`}
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-1 min-w-0">
        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-foreground">
          {prediction.symbol}
        </span>
        <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[10px] px-1 py-0 border-foreground/30 text-foreground/80">
          {prediction.timeframe}
        </Badge>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        <DirectionIcon className={`w-3 h-3 ${textColor}`} />
        <span className={`font-mono text-xs font-bold ${textColor}`}>
          {predictedChange >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{predictedChange.toFixed(2)}%
        </span>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-foreground/70">
        <Clock className="w-3 h-3" />
        <span>{formatTimeRemaining(prediction.targetTime || new Date().toISOString())}</span>
      </div>
      
      <div className="w-12">
        <div className="h-1 bg-muted/30 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
          <div 
            className={`h-full rounded-full ${isUp ? "bg-green-400" : isDown ? "bg-red-400" : "bg-cyan-400"}`}
            style={{ width: `${confidence * 100}%` }}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="text-[9px] text-foreground/60 text-center mt-0.5">
          {(confidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ResolvedRow({ prediction }: { prediction: AIPrediction }) {
  if (!prediction) return null;
  
  const wasCorrect = prediction.wasCorrect;
  const actualChange = prediction.actualChange ?? 0;
  const predictedChange = prediction.predictedChange ?? 0;
  
  return (
    <div 
      className={`flex items-center gap-2 p-1.5 rounded text-[10px] ${
        wasCorrect 
          ? "bg-green-500/15 border border-green-500/25" 
          : "bg-red-500/15 border border-red-500/25"
      }`}
      data-testid={`resolved-${prediction.id}`}
    >
      {wasCorrect ? (
        <CheckCircle className="w-3 h-3 text-green-400" />
      ) : (
        <XCircle className="w-3 h-3 text-red-400" />
      )}
      
      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-foreground">{prediction.symbol ?? 'N/A'}</span>
      <Badge variant="outline" className="text-[9px] px-1 py-0 border-foreground/30 text-foreground/80">{prediction.timeframe ?? ''}</Badge>
      
      <span className="text-foreground/70">
        Pred: <span className={predictedChange >= 0 ? "text-green-400" : "text-red-400"}>
          {predictedChange >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{predictedChange.toFixed(1)}%
        </span>
      </span>
      
      <span className="text-foreground/70">
        Actual: <span className={actualChange >= 0 ? "text-green-400" : "text-red-400"}>
          {actualChange >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{actualChange.toFixed(1)}%
        </span>
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { ArrowUpRight, ArrowDownRight, Minus, TrendingUp, TrendingDown, ArrowLeftRight, DollarSign } from "lucide-react";
import { WidgetTooltip, widgetDescriptions } from "./WidgetTooltip";

interface FlowMetrics {
  stablecoinSupply: {
    current: number;
    previous24h: number;
    delta24h: number;
    deltaPercent: number;
    interpretation: string;
  };
  bridgeActivity: {
    totalVolume24h: number;
    totalVolumePrevDay: number;
    delta24h: number;
    deltaPercent: number;
    topBridges: Array<{
      name: string;
      volume24h: number;
      volumeChange: number;
    }>;
    interpretation: string;
  };
  netFlowSignal: {
    score: number;
    direction: "inflow" | "outflow" | "neutral";
    strength: "strong" | "moderate" | "weak";
    description: string;
  };
  lastUpdated: string;
}

function formatLargeNumber(num: number): string {
  const abs = Math.abs(num);
  if (abs >= 1e12) return (num / 1e12).toFixed(2) + "T";
  if (abs >= 1e9) return (num / 1e9).toFixed(2) + "B";
  if (abs >= 1e6) return (num / 1e6).toFixed(2) + "M";
  if (abs >= 1e3) return (num / 1e3).toFixed(1) + "K";
  return num.toFixed(2);
}

function FlowGauge({ score, direction, strength }: { score: number; direction: string; strength: string }) {
  const normalizedScore = (score + 1) / 2;
  const rotation = -90 + normalizedScore * 180;
  
  const getColor = () => {
    if (direction === "inflow") return "#00ff88";
    if (direction === "outflow") return "#ff4466";
    return "#ffaa00";
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative w-32 h-16 mx-auto">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 100 50" className="w-full h-full">
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id="flowGaugeGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#ff4466" />
            <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#ffaa00" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#00ff88" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>
        <path
          d="M 10 50 A 40 40 0 0 1 90 50"
          fill="none"
          stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.1)"
          strokeWidth="6"
        />
        <path
          d="M 10 50 A 40 40 0 0 1 90 50"
          fill="none"
          stroke="url(#flowGaugeGrad)"
          strokeWidth="6"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          opacity="0.3"
        />
        <motion.g
          initial={{ rotate: -90 }}
          animate={{ rotate: rotation }}
          transition={{ duration: 1, ease: "easeOut" }}
          style={{ transformOrigin: "50px 50px" }}
        >
          <line
            x1="50"
            y1="50"
            x2="50"
            y2="18"
            stroke={getColor()}
            strokeWidth="2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
          />
          <circle cx="50" cy="50" r="4" fill={getColor()} />
        </motion.g>
      </svg>
      <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 text-center">
        <span 
          className="text-xs font-bold uppercase"
          style={{ color: getColor() }}
        >
          {direction} ({strength})
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function CapitalFlowTracker() {
  const { data: flows, isLoading } = useQuery<FlowMetrics>({
    queryKey: ["/api/flows"],
    refetchInterval: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <Card className="p-4 bg-card/50 border-border/50">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-cyan-400">Capital Flow Delta</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="animate-pulse space-y-3">
          <div className="h-16 bg-muted/20 rounded" />
          <div className="h-20 bg-muted/20 rounded" />
        </div>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  if (!flows) {
    return (
      <Card className="p-4 bg-card/50 border-border/50">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-cyan-400">Capital Flow Delta</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center py-6 text-muted-foreground text-sm">
          Loading capital flow data...
        </div>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  const { stablecoinSupply, bridgeActivity, netFlowSignal } = flows;

  return (
    <Card className="p-4 bg-card/50 border-border/50" data-testid="capital-flow-tracker">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <DollarSign className="w-5 h-5 text-green-400" />
          <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-cyan-400">Capital Flow Delta</h3>
        </div>
        <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.capitalFlows} />
      </div>

      <FlowGauge 
        score={netFlowSignal.score} 
        direction={netFlowSignal.direction} 
        strength={netFlowSignal.strength}
      />

      <div className="mt-4 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground mb-4">
        {netFlowSignal.description}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div className="bg-background/30 rounded p-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1">
            <TrendingUp className="w-3 h-3" />
            Stablecoin Supply
          </div>
          <div className="text-lg font-mono font-bold text-foreground">
            ${formatLargeNumber(stablecoinSupply.current)}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
            {stablecoinSupply.deltaPercent > 0 ? (
              <ArrowUpRight className="w-3 h-3 text-green-400" />
            ) : stablecoinSupply.deltaPercent < 0 ? (
              <ArrowDownRight className="w-3 h-3 text-red-400" />
            ) : (
              <Minus className="w-3 h-3 text-yellow-400" />
            )}
            <span 
              className={`text-xs font-mono ${
                stablecoinSupply.deltaPercent > 0 
                  ? "text-green-400" 
                  : stablecoinSupply.deltaPercent < 0 
                    ? "text-red-400" 
                    : "text-yellow-400"
              }`}
            >
              {stablecoinSupply.deltaPercent > 0 ? "+" : ""}
              {stablecoinSupply.deltaPercent.toFixed(3)}%
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground mt-1">
            {stablecoinSupply.interpretation}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-background/30 rounded p-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-1">
            <ArrowLeftRight className="w-3 h-3" />
            Bridge Volume
          </div>
          <div className="text-lg font-mono font-bold text-foreground">
            ${formatLargeNumber(bridgeActivity.totalVolume24h)}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
            {bridgeActivity.deltaPercent > 0 ? (
              <ArrowUpRight className="w-3 h-3 text-green-400" />
            ) : bridgeActivity.deltaPercent < 0 ? (
              <ArrowDownRight className="w-3 h-3 text-red-400" />
            ) : (
              <Minus className="w-3 h-3 text-yellow-400" />
            )}
            <span 
              className={`text-xs font-mono ${
                bridgeActivity.deltaPercent > 0 
                  ? "text-green-400" 
                  : bridgeActivity.deltaPercent < 0 
                    ? "text-red-400" 
                    : "text-yellow-400"
              }`}
            >
              {bridgeActivity.deltaPercent > 0 ? "+" : ""}
              {bridgeActivity.deltaPercent.toFixed(1)}%
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground mt-1">
            {bridgeActivity.interpretation}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {bridgeActivity.topBridges.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-border/30">
          <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mb-2">Top Bridges (24h)</div>
          <div className="space-y-1">
            {bridgeActivity.topBridges.slice(0, 3).map((bridge, i) => (
              <div 
                key={bridge.name}
                className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs"
              >
                <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                  {i + 1}. {bridge.name}
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono text-foreground">
                  ${formatLargeNumber(bridge.volume24h)}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Activity, TrendingUp, TrendingDown, Minus } from "lucide-react";
import type { CorrelationData } from "@shared/schema";
import { WidgetTooltip, widgetDescriptions } from "./WidgetTooltip";

interface CorrelationTrackerProps {
  correlations: CorrelationData[];
}

export function CorrelationTracker({ correlations }: CorrelationTrackerProps) {
  if (correlations.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Card className="p-4 border-border/50 bg-card/30" data-testid="card-correlation-empty">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <Activity className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-400" />
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-sm text-foreground">
            BTC CORRELATION DELTA
          </h3>
          <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.correlations} />
        </div>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">
          Loading correlation data...
        </p>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Card className="p-4 border-border/50 bg-card/30" data-testid="card-correlation-tracker">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
        <Activity className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-400" />
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-sm text-foreground">
          BTC CORRELATION DELTA
        </h3>
        <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.correlations} />
        <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-[10px] border-purple-500/50 text-purple-400">
          24H CHANGE
        </Badge>
      </div>
      
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {correlations.slice(0, 6).map((corr) => {
          const delta = (corr.correlationDelta24h || 0) * 100;
          const correlation24h = (corr.correlation24h || corr.correlationWithBTC) * 100;
          const isPositiveDelta = delta >= 0;
          const absDelta = Math.abs(delta);
          
          return (
            <div 
              key={corr.coin}
              className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-2 rounded border transition-colors ${
                absDelta > 10 
                  ? isPositiveDelta 
                    ? "bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30" 
                    : "bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/30"
                  : "bg-background/30 border-border/20"
              }`}
              data-testid={`correlation-${corr.coin}`}
            >
              <span className="font-mono text-xs w-10 text-foreground font-bold">
                {corr.coin}
              </span>
              
              <div className="flex-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">24h Corr:</span>
                  <span className={`font-mono text-xs font-bold ${
                    correlation24h > 0 ? "text-cyan-400" : "text-magenta-400"
                  }`}>
                    {correlation24h > 0 ? "+" : ""}{correlation24h.toFixed(0)}%
                  </span>
                </div>
                
                <div className="h-1.5 bg-muted/30 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                  <div 
                    className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all ${
                      isPositiveDelta ? "bg-green-400" : "bg-red-400"
                    }`}
                    style={{ 
                      width: `${Math.min(100, absDelta * 5)}%`,
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 min-w-[60px] justify-end">
                {isPositiveDelta ? (
                  <TrendingUp className="w-3 h-3 text-green-400" />
                ) : (
                  <TrendingDown className="w-3 h-3 text-red-400" />
                )}
                <span className={`font-mono text-xs font-bold ${
                  isPositiveDelta ? "text-green-400" : "text-red-400"
                }`}>
                  {isPositiveDelta ? "+" : ""}{delta.toFixed(1)}%
                </span>
              </div>
              
              <Badge 
                variant="outline" 
                className={`text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0 ${
                  corr.strength === "strong" 
                    ? "border-purple-500/50 text-purple-400"
                    : corr.strength === "moderate"
                      ? "border-blue-500/50 text-blue-400"
                      : "border-foreground/30 text-foreground/70"
                }`}
              >
                {corr.strength.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
              </Badge>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      
      <div className="mt-3 pt-2 border-t border-border/30 grid grid-cols-3 text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <TrendingDown className="w-3 h-3 text-red-400" />
          <span>Weakening</span>
        </div>
        <div className="text-center">
          <span>24h Change</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 justify-end">
          <span>Strengthening</span>
          <TrendingUp className="w-3 h-3 text-green-400" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

START OF FILE: ./client/src/components/dashboard/CryptoCard.tsx
====================
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { TrendingUp, TrendingDown, Minus } from "lucide-react";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import type { CryptoData, CompositeSentiment } from "@shared/schema";
import { MiniSparkline } from "./MiniSparkline";

interface CryptoCardProps {
  crypto: CryptoData;
  sentiment?: CompositeSentiment;
  index: number;
}

export function CryptoCard({ crypto, sentiment, index }: CryptoCardProps) {
  const priceChange = crypto.price_change_percentage_24h ?? 0;
  const isPositive = priceChange > 0;
  const isNegative = priceChange < 0;

  const formatPrice = (price: number) => {
    if (price >= 1000) {
      return price.toLocaleString("en-US", {
        style: "currency",
        currency: "USD",
        minimumFractionDigits: 0,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
      });
    }
    if (price >= 1) {
      return price.toLocaleString("en-US", {
        style: "currency",
        currency: "USD",
        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
      });
    }
    return price.toLocaleString("en-US", {
      style: "currency",
      currency: "USD",
      minimumFractionDigits: 4,
      maximumFractionDigits: 6,
    });
  };

  const formatMarketCap = (cap: number) => {
    if (cap >= 1e12) return `$${(cap / 1e12).toFixed(2)}T`;
    if (cap >= 1e9) return `$${(cap / 1e9).toFixed(2)}B`;
    if (cap >= 1e6) return `$${(cap / 1e6).toFixed(2)}M`;
    return `$${cap.toLocaleString()}`;
  };

  const getSentimentColor = (label?: string) => {
    switch (label) {
      case "bullish":
        return "text-neon-green";
      case "bearish":
        return "text-neon-red";
      default:
        return "text-neon-yellow";
    }
  };

  const getSentimentBg = (label?: string) => {
    switch (label) {
      case "bullish":
        return "bg-neon-green/10 border-neon-green/30";
      case "bearish":
        return "bg-neon-red/10 border-neon-red/30";
      default:
        return "bg-neon-yellow/10 border-neon-yellow/30";
    }
  };

  return (
    <motion.div
      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
      transition={{ duration: 0.4, delay: index * 0.05 }}
    >
      <Card
        className="relative overflow-hidden border-border/50 bg-card/80 backdrop-blur-sm hover:border-primary/50 transition-all duration-300 group"
        data-testid={`card-crypto-${crypto.id}`}
      >
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-primary/5 via-transparent to-accent/5 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300" />
        
        <div className="relative p-4">
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="relative">
                <img
                  src={crypto.image}
                  alt={crypto.name}
                  className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full ring-2 ring-border/50"
                  data-testid={`img-crypto-${crypto.id}`}
                />
                <div className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-card border border-border flex items-center justify-center">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-muted-foreground">
                    #{crypto.market_cap_rank}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-display font-semibold text-foreground tracking-wide" data-testid={`text-crypto-name-${crypto.id}`}>
                  {crypto.name}
                </h3>
                <span className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase">
                  {crypto.symbol}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {sentiment && (
              <Badge
                variant="outline"
                className={`${getSentimentBg(sentiment.label)} ${getSentimentColor(sentiment.label)} font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider`}
                data-testid={`badge-sentiment-${crypto.id}`}
              >
                {sentiment.label}
              </Badge>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-end justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-2xl font-display font-bold tracking-tight number-scroll" data-testid={`text-price-${crypto.id}`}>
                  {formatPrice(crypto.current_price)}
                </p>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                  {isPositive ? (
                    <TrendingUp className="h-4 w-4 text-neon-green" />
                  ) : isNegative ? (
                    <TrendingDown className="h-4 w-4 text-neon-red" />
                  ) : (
                    <Minus className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                  )}
                  <span
                    className={`text-sm font-mono font-medium ${
                      isPositive ? "text-neon-green text-glow-green" : isNegative ? "text-neon-red text-glow-red" : "text-muted-foreground"
                    }`}
                    data-testid={`text-change-${crypto.id}`}
                  >
                    {priceChange >= 0 ? "+" : ""}
                    {priceChange.toFixed(2)}%
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {crypto.sparkline_in_7d && (
                <div className="w-24 h-12">
                  <MiniSparkline
                    data={crypto.sparkline_in_7d.price}
                    isPositive={isPositive}
                  />
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-border/30">
              <div>
                <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Market Cap
                </p>
                <p className="text-sm font-mono font-medium" data-testid={`text-mcap-${crypto.id}`}>
                  {formatMarketCap(crypto.market_cap)}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Volume 24h
                </p>
                <p className="text-sm font-mono font-medium" data-testid={`text-volume-${crypto.id}`}>
                  {formatMarketCap(crypto.total_volume)}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {sentiment && (
              <div className="pt-2">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Sentiment Score
                  </span>
                  <span className={`text-xs font-mono font-bold ${getSentimentColor(sentiment.label)}`}>
                    {((sentiment.compositeScore + 1) * 50).toFixed(0)}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className={`h-full transition-all duration-500 ${
                      sentiment.label === "bullish"
                        ? "bg-neon-green"
                        : sentiment.label === "bearish"
                        ? "bg-neon-red"
                        : "bg-neon-yellow"
                    }`}
                    style={{ width: `${((sentiment.compositeScore + 1) / 2) * 100}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </motion.div>
  );
}

START OF FILE: ./client/src/components/dashboard/DivergenceAlerts.tsx

import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { TrendingUp, TrendingDown, AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import type { DivergenceSignal } from "@shared/schema";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { WidgetTooltip, widgetDescriptions } from "./WidgetTooltip";

interface DivergenceAlertsProps {
  divergences: DivergenceSignal[];
}

export function DivergenceAlerts({ divergences }: DivergenceAlertsProps) {
  if (divergences.length === 0) {
    return (
      <Card className="p-4 border-border/50 bg-card/30" data-testid="card-divergence-empty">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-yellow-400" />
          <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-sm text-foreground">
            DIVERGENCE DETECTOR
          </h3>
          <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.divergenceAlerts} />
        </div>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">
          No significant divergences detected. Market sentiment aligns with price action.
        </p>
      </Card>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Card className="p-4 border-border/50 bg-card/30 overflow-hidden" data-testid="card-divergence-alerts">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
        <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 text-yellow-400 animate-pulse" />
        <h3 className="font-display font-bold text-sm text-foreground">
          DIVERGENCE DETECTOR
        </h3>
        <WidgetTooltip {...widgetDescriptions.divergenceAlerts} />
        <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-xs border-yellow-400/50 text-yellow-400">
          {divergences.length} SIGNAL{divergences.length > 1 ? "S" : ""}
        </Badge>
      </div>
      
      <div className="space-y-2">
        <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout">
          {divergences.map((signal, idx) => (
            <motion.div
              key={`${signal.coin}-${idx}`}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -20 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 20 }}
              transition={{ delay: idx * 0.1 }}
              className={`p-3 rounded-md border ${
                signal.type === "bullish"
                  ? "bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/30"
                  : "bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/30"
              }`}
              data-testid={`divergence-signal-${signal.coin}`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  {signal.type === "bullish" ? (
                    <TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4 text-green-400" />
                  ) : (
                    <TrendingDown className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400" />
                  )}
                  <span className="font-display font-bold text-sm">
                    {signal.coin}
                  </span>
                  <Badge 
                    variant="outline" 
                    className={`text-xs ${
                      signal.type === "bullish" 
                        ? "border-green-500/50 text-green-400" 
                        : "border-red-500/50 text-red-400"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {signal.type.toUpperCase()} DIVERGENCE
                  </Badge>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Strength:</span>
                  <div className="w-12 h-1.5 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div 
                      className={`h-full rounded-full ${
                        signal.type === "bullish" ? "bg-green-400" : "bg-red-400"
                      }`}
                      style={{ width: `${signal.strength * 100}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {signal.description}
              </p>
              <div className="flex gap-4 mt-1 text-xs">
                <span className={signal.priceChange24h >= 0 ? "text-green-400" : "text-red-400"}>
                  Price: {signal.priceChange24h >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{signal.priceChange24h.toFixed(1)}%
                </span>
                <span className={signal.sentimentScore >= 0 ? "text-green-400" : "text-red-400"}>
                  Sentiment: {signal.sentimentScore >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{(signal.sentimentScore * 100).toFixed(0)}%
                </span>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          ))}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

import { AlertTriangle, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";

interface ErrorStateProps {
  title?: string;
  message: string;
  onRetry?: () => void;
}

export function ErrorState({ title = "Something went wrong", message, onRetry }: ErrorStateProps) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-8 border-destructive/30 bg-destructive/5">
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-destructive/10 flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-8 w-8 text-destructive" />
        </div>
        <h3 className="font-display font-semibold text-lg text-destructive mb-2">
          {title}
        </h3>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground max-w-md mb-4">
          {message}
        </p>
        {onRetry && (
          <Button onClick={onRetry} variant="outline" className="gap-2">
            <RefreshCw className="h-4 w-4" />
            Try Again
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

import { Activity, Wifi, WifiOff, Zap } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";

interface HeaderProps {
  isConnected: boolean;
  lastUpdated: string | null;
}

export function Header({ isConnected, lastUpdated }: HeaderProps) {
  const formatTime = (timestamp: string | null) => {
    if (!timestamp) return "—";
    const date = new Date(timestamp);
    return date.toLocaleTimeString("en-US", {
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
      second: "2-digit",
      hour12: false,
    });
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-50 border-b border-border/50 bg-background/80 backdrop-blur-xl">
      <div className="container mx-auto px-4 py-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="h-10 w-10 rounded-md bg-gradient-to-br from-primary to-accent flex items-center justify-center glow-cyan">
                <Zap className="h-6 w-6 text-primary-foreground" />
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -bottom-1 -right-1 h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-neon-green pulse-green" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h1 className="font-display text-xl font-bold tracking-wider text-glow-cyan" data-testid="text-app-title">
                CRYPTOPULSE
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono tracking-wide">
                LIVE SENTIMENT TRACKER
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-card/50 border border-border/50">
              <Activity className="h-4 w-4 text-primary animate-glow-pulse" />
              <span className="text-xs font-mono text-muted-foreground">
                LAST UPDATE: <span className="text-foreground">{formatTime(lastUpdated)}</span>
              </span>
            </div>

            <Badge
              variant={isConnected ? "default" : "destructive"}
              className={`gap-1.5 font-mono text-xs ${
                isConnected ? "bg-neon-green/20 text-neon-green border-neon-green/50" : ""
              }`}
              data-testid="badge-connection-status"
            >
              {isConnected ? (
                <>
                  <Wifi className="h-3 w-3" />
                  LIVE
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <WifiOff className="h-3 w-3" />
                  OFFLINE
                </>
              )}
            </Badge>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
