# [NOTE: POTENTIAL SENSITIVE DATA REDACTED BY USER BEFORE UPLOAD]
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import type { CryptoData, SentimentData, AIInsight } from "@shared/schema";

const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "");

let cachedInsight: AIInsight | null = null;
let lastAnalysis: number = 0;
const ANALYSIS_COOLDOWN = 300000;

export async function generateMarketInsight(
  cryptos: CryptoData[],
  sentiment: SentimentData | null,
  polymarketData: any[]
): Promise<AIInsight> {
  const now = Date.now();
  
  if (cachedInsight && now - lastAnalysis < ANALYSIS_COOLDOWN) {
    console.log("[AI] Returning cached insight");
    return cachedInsight;
  }

  try {
    console.log("[AI] Generating market insight with Gemini...");
    
    const marketSummary = cryptos.slice(0, 5).map((c) => ({
      name: c.name,
      symbol: c.symbol.toUpperCase(),
      price: c.current_price,
      change24h: c.price_change_percentage_24h?.toFixed(2) + "%",
      change7d: c.price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency?.toFixed(2) + "%",
    }));
    
    const polymarketSummary = polymarketData.slice(0, 3).map((m) => ({
      question: m.question,
      topOutcome: m.outcomes?.[0]?.name || "Unknown",
      probability: ((m.outcomes?.[0]?.price || 0.5) * 100).toFixed(0) + "%",
    }));

    const prompt = `You are a crypto market analyst. Analyze this real-time data and provide a brief, actionable insight.

CURRENT MARKET DATA:
${JSON.stringify(marketSummary, null, 2)}

BTC SENTIMENT: ${sentiment?.label || "neutral"} (score: ${sentiment?.score?.toFixed(2) || "0"})

PREDICTION MARKETS:
${JSON.stringify(polymarketSummary, null, 2)}

Provide a JSON response with:
{
  "summary": "One sentence market overview",
  "trend": "bullish" | "bearish" | "neutral",
  "confidence": 0.0-1.0,
  "keySignal": "The most important signal right now",
  "riskLevel": "low" | "medium" | "high",
  "topOpportunity": "Best opportunity if any",
  "warning": "Key risk to watch"
}

Be concise and data-driven. Base analysis ONLY on the provided data. Respond with ONLY valid JSON, no markdown.`;

    const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: "gemini-2.0-flash" });
    
    const result = await model.generateContent(prompt);
    const response = await result.response;
    const content = response.text();
    
    const jsonMatch = content.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
    const jsonStr = jsonMatch ? jsonMatch[0] : "{}";
    const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonStr);
    
    const insight: AIInsight = {
      summary: parsed.summary || "Market analysis unavailable",
      trend: parsed.trend || "neutral",
      confidence: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, parsed.confidence || 0.5)),
      keySignal: parsed.keySignal || "No clear signal",
      riskLevel: parsed.riskLevel || "medium",
      topOpportunity: parsed.topOpportunity || null,
      warning: parsed.warning || null,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    
    cachedInsight = insight;
    lastAnalysis = now;
    
    console.log("[AI] Generated insight:", insight.trend, insight.confidence);
    return insight;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[AI] Analysis error:", error);
    
    return {
      summary: "Unable to generate AI insight at this time",
      trend: "neutral",
      confidence: 0,
      keySignal: "Analysis unavailable",
      riskLevel: "medium",
      topOpportunity: null,
      warning: "AI analysis temporarily unavailable",
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
  }
}

export function getCachedInsight(): AIInsight | null {
  return cachedInsight;
}

# [NOTE: POTENTIAL SENSITIVE DATA REDACTED BY USER BEFORE UPLOAD]
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import type { CryptoData, AIPrediction, PredictionAccuracy } from "@shared/schema";
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from "uuid";

const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "");

type Timeframe = "10m" | "30m" | "60m" | "4h" | "24h";

const TIMEFRAME_MS: Record<Timeframe, number> = {
  "10m": 10 * 60 * 1000,
  "30m": 30 * 60 * 1000,
  "60m": 60 * 60 * 1000,
  "4h": 4 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
  "24h": 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
};

const predictions: Map<string, AIPrediction> = new Map();
const accuracyStats: Map<Timeframe, PredictionAccuracy> = new Map();

let lastPredictionTime = 0;
const PREDICTION_COOLDOWN = 60000;

function calculateRSI(prices: number[], period = 14): number {
  if (prices.length < period + 1) return 50;
  
  let gains = 0;
  let losses = 0;
  
  for (let i = prices.length - period; i < prices.length; i++) {
    const change = prices[i] - prices[i - 1];
    if (change > 0) gains += change;
    else losses -= change;
  }
  
  const avgGain = gains / period;
  const avgLoss = losses / period;
  
  if (avgLoss === 0) return 100;
  const rs = avgGain / avgLoss;
  return 100 - (100 / (1 + rs));
}

function calculateMomentum(prices: number[], period = 10): number {
  if (prices.length < period) return 0;
  const current = prices[prices.length - 1];
  const past = prices[prices.length - period];
  return ((current - past) / past) * 100;
}

function calculateVolatility(prices: number[]): number {
  if (prices.length < 2) return 0;
  const returns: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < prices.length; i++) {
    returns.push((prices[i] - prices[i - 1]) / prices[i - 1]);
  }
  const mean = returns.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / returns.length;
  const variance = returns.reduce((sum, r) => sum + Math.pow(r - mean, 2), 0) / returns.length;
  return Math.sqrt(variance) * 100;
}

function calculateBollingerPosition(prices: number[], period = 20): number {
  if (prices.length < period) return 0.5;
  
  const recentPrices = prices.slice(-period);
  const sma = recentPrices.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / period;
  const stdDev = Math.sqrt(
    recentPrices.reduce((sum, p) => sum + Math.pow(p - sma, 2), 0) / period
  );
  
  const current = prices[prices.length - 1];
  const upperBand = sma + 2 * stdDev;
  const lowerBand = sma - 2 * stdDev;
  
  if (upperBand === lowerBand) return 0.5;
  return (current - lowerBand) / (upperBand - lowerBand);
}

function calculateMACD(prices: number[]): { macd: number; signal: number; histogram: number } {
  if (prices.length < 26) return { macd: 0, signal: 0, histogram: 0 };
  
  const ema12 = calculateEMA(prices, 12);
  const ema26 = calculateEMA(prices, 26);
  const macd = ema12 - ema26;
  
  const macdHistory: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 26; i < prices.length; i++) {
    const shortEma = calculateEMA(prices.slice(0, i + 1), 12);
    const longEma = calculateEMA(prices.slice(0, i + 1), 26);
    macdHistory.push(shortEma - longEma);
  }
  
  const signal = macdHistory.length >= 9 ? calculateEMA(macdHistory, 9) : macd;
  
  return { macd, signal, histogram: macd - signal };
}

function calculateEMA(prices: number[], period: number): number {
  if (prices.length < period) return prices[prices.length - 1] || 0;
  
  const multiplier = 2 / (period + 1);
  let ema = prices.slice(0, period).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / period;
  
  for (let i = period; i < prices.length; i++) {
    ema = (prices[i] - ema) * multiplier + ema;
  }
  
  return ema;
}

function generateQuantPrediction(
  crypto: CryptoData,
  timeframe: Timeframe,
  sentimentScore: number
): Omit<AIPrediction, "id" | "createdAt" | "targetTime" | "resolved" | "actualPrice" | "actualChange" | "accuracyScore" | "wasCorrect"> {
  const prices = crypto.sparkline_in_7d?.price || [];
  const currentPrice = crypto.current_price;
  
  const rsi = calculateRSI(prices);
  const momentum = calculateMomentum(prices);
  const volatility = calculateVolatility(prices);
  const bollingerPos = calculateBollingerPosition(prices);
  const macd = calculateMACD(prices);
  
  let technicalScore = 0;
  let signals: string[] = [];
  
  if (rsi < 30) {
    technicalScore += 0.3;
    signals.push("RSI oversold");
  } else if (rsi > 70) {
    technicalScore -= 0.3;
    signals.push("RSI overbought");
  } else {
    technicalScore += (rsi - 50) / 100;
  }
  
  if (momentum > 5) {
    technicalScore += 0.25;
    signals.push("Strong momentum");
  } else if (momentum < -5) {
    technicalScore -= 0.25;
    signals.push("Weak momentum");
  }
  
  if (bollingerPos < 0.2) {
    technicalScore += 0.2;
    signals.push("Near lower Bollinger");
  } else if (bollingerPos > 0.8) {
    technicalScore -= 0.2;
    signals.push("Near upper Bollinger");
  }
  
  if (macd.histogram > 0 && macd.macd > macd.signal) {
    technicalScore += 0.15;
    signals.push("MACD bullish");
  } else if (macd.histogram < 0 && macd.macd < macd.signal) {
    technicalScore -= 0.15;
    signals.push("MACD bearish");
  }
  
  const sentimentWeight = 0.2;
  const combinedScore = (technicalScore * (1 - sentimentWeight)) + (sentimentScore * sentimentWeight);
  
  const timeframeMultiplier: Record<Timeframe, number> = {
    "10m": 0.15,
    "30m": 0.3,
    "60m": 0.5,
    "4h": 1.5,
    "24h": 3,
  };
  
  const volatilityFactor = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2, volatility / 2));
  const baseChange = combinedScore * timeframeMultiplier[timeframe] * volatilityFactor;
  const predictedChange = Math.max(-20, Math.min(20, baseChange));
  
  const predictedPrice = currentPrice * (1 + predictedChange / 100);
  
  const baseConfidence = 0.5 + Math.abs(combinedScore) * 0.3;
  const volatilityPenalty = Math.min(0.2, volatility / 50);
  const confidence = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(0.9, baseConfidence - volatilityPenalty));
  
  const direction: "up" | "down" | "neutral" = 
    predictedChange > 0.5 ? "up" : predictedChange < -0.5 ? "down" : "neutral";
  
  return {
    symbol: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
    coinName: crypto.name,
    timeframe,
    priceAtPrediction: currentPrice,
    predictedPrice,
    predictedChange,
    direction,
    confidence,
    methodology: signals.slice(0, 3).join(", ") || "Technical analysis",
    indicators: {
      rsi,
      momentum,
      volatility,
      sentimentScore,
    },
  };
}

export async function generatePredictions(
  cryptos: CryptoData[],
  sentimentScore: number = 0
): Promise<AIPrediction[]> {
  const now = Date.now();
  
  if (now - lastPredictionTime < PREDICTION_COOLDOWN) {
    return getActivePredictions();
  }
  
  lastPredictionTime = now;
  const newPredictions: AIPrediction[] = [];
  const topCryptos = cryptos.slice(0, 5);
  const shortTimeframes: Timeframe[] = ["10m", "30m", "60m"];
  const longTimeframes: Timeframe[] = ["4h", "24h"];
  
  for (const crypto of topCryptos) {
    for (const tf of shortTimeframes) {
      const predBase = generateQuantPrediction(crypto, tf, sentimentScore);
      const prediction: AIPrediction = {
        id: uuidv4(),
        ...predBase,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        targetTime: new Date(now + TIMEFRAME_MS[tf]).toISOString(),
        resolved: false,
        actualPrice: null,
        actualChange: null,
        accuracyScore: null,
        wasCorrect: null,
      };
      predictions.set(prediction.id, prediction);
      newPredictions.push(prediction);
    }
  }
  
  const btc = cryptos.find((c) => c.symbol.toLowerCase() === "btc");
  if (btc) {
    for (const tf of longTimeframes) {
      const predBase = generateQuantPrediction(btc, tf, sentimentScore);
      const prediction: AIPrediction = {
        id: uuidv4(),
        ...predBase,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        targetTime: new Date(now + TIMEFRAME_MS[tf]).toISOString(),
        resolved: false,
        actualPrice: null,
        actualChange: null,
        accuracyScore: null,
        wasCorrect: null,
      };
      predictions.set(prediction.id, prediction);
      newPredictions.push(prediction);
    }
  }
  
  cleanupOldPredictions();
  
  return newPredictions;
}

export async function resolvePredictions(cryptos: CryptoData[]): Promise<void> {
  const now = Date.now();
  const priceMap = new Map(cryptos.map((c) => [c.symbol.toUpperCase(), c.current_price]));
  
  for (const [id, pred] of Array.from(predictions.entries())) {
    if (pred.resolved) continue;
    
    const targetTime = new Date(pred.targetTime).getTime();
    if (now >= targetTime) {
      const actualPrice = priceMap.get(pred.symbol);
      if (actualPrice !== undefined) {
        const actualChange = ((actualPrice - pred.priceAtPrediction) / pred.priceAtPrediction) * 100;
        const predictionError = Math.abs(pred.predictedChange - actualChange);
        const maxError = Math.max(Math.abs(pred.predictedChange), Math.abs(actualChange), 1);
        const accuracyScore = Math.max(0, 1 - predictionError / maxError);
        
        const actualDirection = actualChange > 0.5 ? "up" : actualChange < -0.5 ? "down" : "neutral";
        const wasCorrect = pred.direction === actualDirection || 
          (pred.direction === "neutral" && Math.abs(actualChange) < 1);
        
        pred.resolved = true;
        pred.actualPrice = actualPrice;
        pred.actualChange = actualChange;
        pred.accuracyScore = accuracyScore;
        pred.wasCorrect = wasCorrect;
        
        predictions.set(id, pred);
        updateAccuracyStats(pred);
      }
    }
  }
}

function updateAccuracyStats(pred: AIPrediction): void {
  const tf = pred.timeframe;
  const existing = accuracyStats.get(tf) || {
    timeframe: tf,
    totalPredictions: 0,
    correctPredictions: 0,
    accuracyRate: 0,
    averageError: 0,
    directionAccuracy: 0,
    lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
  
  existing.totalPredictions += 1;
  if (pred.wasCorrect) {
    existing.correctPredictions += 1;
  }
  existing.directionAccuracy = (existing.correctPredictions / existing.totalPredictions) * 100;
  
  const predictionError = Math.abs((pred.predictedChange || 0) - (pred.actualChange || 0));
  existing.averageError = (existing.averageError * (existing.totalPredictions - 1) + predictionError) / existing.totalPredictions;
  existing.accuracyRate = Math.max(0, 100 - existing.averageError * 10);
  existing.lastUpdated = new Date().toISOString();
  
  accuracyStats.set(tf, existing);
}

function cleanupOldPredictions(): void {
  const maxAge = 48 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const now = Date.now();
  
  for (const [id, pred] of Array.from(predictions.entries())) {
    const createdAt = new Date(pred.createdAt).getTime();
    if (now - createdAt > maxAge) {
      predictions.delete(id);
    }
  }
}

export function getActivePredictions(limit = 50): AIPrediction[] {
  const now = Date.now();
  return Array.from(predictions.values())
    .filter((p) => !p.resolved && new Date(p.targetTime).getTime() > now)
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.targetTime).getTime() - new Date(b.targetTime).getTime())
    .slice(0, limit);
}

export function getResolvedPredictions(limit = 20): AIPrediction[] {
  return Array.from(predictions.values())
    .filter((p) => p.resolved)
    .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.targetTime).getTime() - new Date(a.targetTime).getTime())
    .slice(0, limit);
}

export function getAccuracyStats(): PredictionAccuracy[] {
  return Array.from(accuracyStats.values());
}

export function getAllPredictionData(): {
  active: AIPrediction[];
  resolved: AIPrediction[];
  accuracy: PredictionAccuracy[];
} {
  return {
    active: getActivePredictions(),
    resolved: getResolvedPredictions(),
    accuracy: getAccuracyStats(),
  };
}

START OF FILE: ./server/services/aiTrader.ts

# [NOTE: POTENTIAL SENSITIVE DATA REDACTED BY USER BEFORE UPLOAD]
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from "@google/generative-ai";
import { AITradeSignal, CryptoData, PositionSide } from "@shared/schema";
import { getPortfolioState, canOpenPosition, getSharpeUlcerRatio } from "./paperTrading";
import { 
  calculateTechnicalIndicators, 
  generateQuantSignal, 
  generateMeanReversionSignal,
  generateMomentumFallbackSignal,
  detectMarketRegime,
  rankSignals,
  updateStrategyPerformance,
  getStrategyPerformance,
  shouldAdjustStrategy,
  getAdaptiveParams,
  recordModePerformance,
  getTotalModeTradeCount,
  BOOTSTRAP_TRADE_THRESHOLD,
  type QuantSignal,
  type TechnicalIndicators,
  type AdaptiveParams,
  type StrategyMode
} from "./quantStrategy";
import { 
  isWarmupComplete, 
  getBestStrategy, 
  getStrategyAllocation,
  generateAllLiveSignals,
  type Live4HSignal
} from "./syntheticWarmup";

const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || "");

const MAX_POSITION_PERCENT = 0.05;

interface MarketData {
  cryptos: CryptoData[];
  sentiment: { coin: string; score: number } | null;
  signals: {
    divergences: Array<{ coin: string; type: string; strength: number }>;
    whaleFlow: { netFlow: number; bullishness: number };
    correlations: Array<{ coin: string; correlationWithBTC: number; trend: string }>;
  } | null;
  onchain: {
    btc: { mvrvZScore: number; nupl: number } | null;
    eth: { mvrvZScore: number; nupl: number } | null;
    market: { overallSentiment: string };
  } | null;
  capitalFlow: {
    netFlowSignal?: { direction: string; strength: string; score: number };
  } | null;
  liquidations: Array<{ symbol: string; volatilityTrap: string }>;
}

let lastAnalysisTime = 0;
const ANALYSIS_COOLDOWN = 30000;

export async function analyzeMarketForTrades(data: MarketData): Promise<AITradeSignal[]> {
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - lastAnalysisTime < ANALYSIS_COOLDOWN) {
    return [];
  }
  lastAnalysisTime = now;

  const portfolio = getPortfolioState();
  
  // Get Sharpe/Ulcer ratio for adaptive learning
  const metrics = getSharpeUlcerRatio();
  const indicatorWeights = getIndicatorWeights();
  
  // Check if strategy needs adjustment based on PRIMARY METRIC (Sharpe/Ulcer)
  const strategyCheck = shouldAdjustStrategy(metrics.primary, indicatorWeights);
  if (strategyCheck.adjust) {
    console.log(`AI Trader: Strategy adjustment - ${strategyCheck.reason}. ${strategyCheck.action}`);
  }
  
  // Get current adaptive parameters (may have just been updated)
  const params = getAdaptiveParams();
  
  // Log current learning state
  console.log(`AI Trader: Primary Metric=${metrics.primary.toFixed(3)}, Threshold=${params.signalThreshold}, Risk=${(params.riskPercent * 100).toFixed(1)}%`);
  
  if (portfolio.currentDrawdown >= 20) {
    console.log("AI Trader: High drawdown (20%+), pausing trading");
    return [];
  }
  
  // Apply drawdown-based position size reduction
  let positionSizeMultiplier = 1.0;
  if (portfolio.currentDrawdown >= 15) {
    console.log("AI Trader: Elevated drawdown, reducing position sizes");
    positionSizeMultiplier = 0.5;
  }

  const signals: AITradeSignal[] = [];
  const quantSignals: QuantSignal[] = [];
  
  const stablecoins = ["usdt", "usdc", "dai", "busd", "tusd", "usdp", "fdusd"];
  const maxPositionValue = portfolio.equity * MAX_POSITION_PERCENT;
  const tradableCryptos = data.cryptos.filter(c => {
    const minTradeValue = c.current_price * 0.001;
    const isStablecoin = stablecoins.includes(c.symbol.toLowerCase());
    return !isStablecoin && minTradeValue <= maxPositionValue && (c.sparkline_in_7d?.price?.length || 0) > 30;
  });
  
  // Check if Synthetic Warm-Up is complete - this gives us pre-calculated Mike1 scores
  // from 90-day backtests so we're "smart from Day 1"
  const warmupComplete = isWarmupComplete();
  const allocation = getStrategyAllocation();
  const activeStrategy = getBestStrategy();
  
  // Check if we're in bootstrap phase (only if warmup failed or not complete)
  const totalModeTradeCount = getTotalModeTradeCount();
  const isBootstrapPhase = !warmupComplete && totalModeTradeCount < BOOTSTRAP_TRADE_THRESHOLD;
  
  if (warmupComplete) {
    console.log(`AI Trader: Using Warmup Mike1 - Primary: ${allocation.primary} (${(allocation.primaryWeight * 100).toFixed(0)}%)`);
    
    // USE 4H OHLC DATA FOR LIVE SIGNALS - MATCHES WARMUP LOGIC EXACTLY
    // This fixes the disconnect between warmup (4H OHLC) and live trading (sparkline)
    const cryptosForSignals = tradableCryptos.slice(0, 15).map(c => ({ id: c.id, current_price: c.current_price }));
    const live4HSignals = generateAllLiveSignals(cryptosForSignals);
    
    // Convert Live4HSignals to QuantSignals for the existing pipeline
    for (const liveSignal of live4HSignals) {
      const crypto = tradableCryptos.find(c => c.id === liveSignal.coinId);
      if (!crypto) continue;
      
      const prices = crypto.sparkline_in_7d?.price || [];
      const indicators = prices.length >= 30 ? calculateTechnicalIndicators(prices) : null;
      
      const quantSignal: QuantSignal = {
        symbol: liveSignal.symbol,
        coinName: crypto.name,
        action: liveSignal.side === 'long' ? 'buy' : 'sell',
        side: liveSignal.side,
        confidence: liveSignal.confidence,
        strength: liveSignal.confidence,
        indicators: indicators || {} as TechnicalIndicators,
        reasoning: liveSignal.reasoning,
        entryPrice: liveSignal.entryPrice,
        stopLoss: liveSignal.stopLoss,
        takeProfit: liveSignal.takeProfit,
        riskRewardRatio: 1.0,
        positionSizePercent: 5.0,
        timestamp: new Date(),
      };
      
      (quantSignal as any).signalMode = liveSignal.strategy as StrategyMode;
      (quantSignal as any).coinId = liveSignal.coinId;
      quantSignals.push(quantSignal);
    }
    
    // Skip the old sparkline-based signal generation when warmup is complete
    // The for loop below handles bootstrap/fallback cases
  } else if (isBootstrapPhase) {
    console.log(`AI Trader: BOOTSTRAP PHASE - ${totalModeTradeCount}/${BOOTSTRAP_TRADE_THRESHOLD} trades. Running ALL modes in parallel.`);
  } else {
    console.log(`AI Trader: Analyzing ${tradableCryptos.length} cryptos with adaptive parameters (mode: ${params.strategyMode})`);
  }
  
  const capitalFlowScore = data.capitalFlow?.netFlowSignal?.score || 0;
  
  // Only run sparkline-based signal generation if warmup is NOT complete
  // When warmup is complete, we use the 4H OHLC signals generated above
  if (!warmupComplete) {
    for (const crypto of tradableCryptos.slice(0, 15)) {
      const symbol = crypto.symbol.toUpperCase();
      const mvrvZScore = symbol === "BTC" 
        ? data.onchain?.btc?.mvrvZScore ?? null
        : symbol === "ETH" 
          ? data.onchain?.eth?.mvrvZScore ?? null
          : null;
      
      const prices = crypto.sparkline_in_7d?.price || [];
      const indicators = prices.length >= 30 ? calculateTechnicalIndicators(prices) : null;
    
      // BOOTSTRAP PHASE: Run ALL modes simultaneously to quickly collect performance data
      // Only used if warmup failed or is not complete
      if (isBootstrapPhase) {
      // Generate signals from BOTH Mean Reversion AND Vol Expansion with relaxed entry requirements
      const meanRevSignal = generateMeanReversionSignal(crypto, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights, true);
      const volExpSignal = generateQuantSignal(crypto, mvrvZScore, capitalFlowScore, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights, true);
      
      // Add mean reversion signal if valid
      if (meanRevSignal) {
        const sideBiasMatch = params.sideBias === 0 || 
          (params.sideBias > 0 && meanRevSignal.side === 'long') ||
          (params.sideBias < 0 && meanRevSignal.side === 'short');
        
        if (meanRevSignal.strength >= params.signalThreshold && sideBiasMatch) {
          meanRevSignal.reasoning.unshift(`[BOOTSTRAP] Mode: Mean Reversion`);
          (meanRevSignal as any).signalMode = 'meanReversion' as StrategyMode;
          quantSignals.push(meanRevSignal);
        }
      }
      
      // Add vol expansion signal if valid
      if (volExpSignal) {
        const sideBiasMatch = params.sideBias === 0 || 
          (params.sideBias > 0 && volExpSignal.side === 'long') ||
          (params.sideBias < 0 && volExpSignal.side === 'short');
        
        if (volExpSignal.strength >= params.signalThreshold && sideBiasMatch) {
          volExpSignal.reasoning.unshift(`[BOOTSTRAP] Mode: Vol Expansion`);
          (volExpSignal as any).signalMode = 'volExpansion' as StrategyMode;
          quantSignals.push(volExpSignal);
        }
      }
      
      // Note: Pairs Trading signals are generated by correlationStrategy separately
      continue; // Move to next crypto
    }
    
    // POST-BOOTSTRAP: Use weighted allocation based on Mike1 performance
    let quantSignal: QuantSignal | null = null;
    let signalMode: StrategyMode = 'volExpansion';
    
    // ADAPTIVE MODE SELECTION based on strategy mode and market regime
    if (params.strategyMode === 'adaptive' && indicators) {
      // In adaptive mode, choose signal type based on detected market regime
      const regime = detectMarketRegime(indicators);
      
      if (regime === 'ranging') {
        // Ranging market - prefer mean reversion
        quantSignal = generateMeanReversionSignal(crypto, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights);
        signalMode = 'meanReversion';
        if (quantSignal) {
          quantSignal.reasoning.unshift(`Regime: Ranging (ADX=${indicators.adx.toFixed(1)}) - Mean Reversion`);
        }
      } else if (regime === 'trending') {
        // Trending market - prefer vol expansion
        quantSignal = generateQuantSignal(crypto, mvrvZScore, capitalFlowScore, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights);
        signalMode = 'volExpansion';
        if (quantSignal) {
          quantSignal.reasoning.unshift(`Regime: Trending (ADX=${indicators.adx.toFixed(1)}) - Vol Expansion`);
        }
      } else {
        // Unknown regime - try both, take the stronger signal
        const volExpSignal = generateQuantSignal(crypto, mvrvZScore, capitalFlowScore, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights);
        const meanRevSignal = generateMeanReversionSignal(crypto, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights);
        
        if (volExpSignal && meanRevSignal) {
          // Take the one with higher confidence
          if (volExpSignal.confidence >= meanRevSignal.confidence) {
            quantSignal = volExpSignal;
            signalMode = 'volExpansion';
          } else {
            quantSignal = meanRevSignal;
            signalMode = 'meanReversion';
          }
        } else {
          quantSignal = volExpSignal || meanRevSignal;
          signalMode = volExpSignal ? 'volExpansion' : 'meanReversion';
        }
      }
    } else if (params.strategyMode === 'meanReversion') {
      // Fixed mean reversion mode
      quantSignal = generateMeanReversionSignal(crypto, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights);
      signalMode = 'meanReversion';
    } else {
      // Default: vol expansion mode
      quantSignal = generateQuantSignal(crypto, mvrvZScore, capitalFlowScore, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights);
      signalMode = 'volExpansion';
    }
    
    // Apply side bias filter
    if (quantSignal) {
      const sideBiasMatch = params.sideBias === 0 || 
        (params.sideBias > 0 && quantSignal.side === 'long') ||
        (params.sideBias < 0 && quantSignal.side === 'short');
      
      if (quantSignal.strength >= params.signalThreshold && sideBiasMatch) {
        // Tag signal with mode for performance tracking
        (quantSignal as any).signalMode = signalMode;
        quantSignals.push(quantSignal);
      }
    }
    }
  }
  
  const rankedSignals = rankSignals(quantSignals);
  console.log(`AI Trader: ${rankedSignals.length} signals above adaptive threshold (${params.signalThreshold})`);
  
  // "MOMENTUM FALLBACK" - If no signals from primary strategies, check for obvious moves
  // This catches major market moves that complex filters might block
  if (rankedSignals.length === 0) {
    console.log(`AI Trader: No primary signals - checking MOMENTUM FALLBACK...`);
    for (const crypto of tradableCryptos.slice(0, 15)) {
      const momentumSignal = generateMomentumFallbackSignal(crypto, portfolio.equity, params, indicatorWeights);
      if (momentumSignal) {
        momentumSignal.reasoning.unshift(`[FALLBACK] Obvious move caught by safety net`);
        (momentumSignal as any).signalMode = 'momentum' as StrategyMode;
        quantSignals.push(momentumSignal);
        console.log(`AI Trader: MOMENTUM FALLBACK triggered for ${crypto.symbol.toUpperCase()}`);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Re-rank if we added momentum signals
  const finalSignals = quantSignals.length > rankedSignals.length 
    ? rankSignals(quantSignals) 
    : rankedSignals;
  
  for (const qs of finalSignals) {
    const coinId = (qs as any).coinId;
    const crypto = data.cryptos.find(c => c.symbol.toUpperCase() === qs.symbol) 
      || (coinId ? data.cryptos.find(c => c.id === coinId) : null);
    if (!crypto) {
      console.log(`AI Trader: SKIP ${qs.symbol} - crypto not found in data.cryptos (available: ${data.cryptos.map(c => c.symbol).join(',')})`);
      continue;
    }
    
    const signal = convertQuantToTradeSignal(qs, crypto, data, portfolio, positionSizeMultiplier, params);
    if (signal) {
      const check = canOpenPosition(signal);
      if (check.allowed) {
        signals.push(signal);
      } else {
        console.log(`AI Trader: Position blocked for ${qs.symbol}: ${check.reason}`);
      }
    } else {
      console.log(`AI Trader: convertQuantToTradeSignal returned null for ${qs.symbol}`);
    }
  }

  // During bootstrap, allow more signals per cycle to quickly build trade history
  const maxSignals = isBootstrapPhase ? 4 : 2;
  return signals.slice(0, maxSignals);
}

// Export function to check if we're in bootstrap phase (for routes.ts to pass to openPosition)
export function isInBootstrapPhase(): boolean {
  const totalModeTradeCount = getTotalModeTradeCount();
  return totalModeTradeCount < BOOTSTRAP_TRADE_THRESHOLD;
}

function convertQuantToTradeSignal(
  qs: QuantSignal,
  crypto: CryptoData,
  data: MarketData,
  portfolio: any,
  positionSizeMultiplier: number = 1.0,
  adaptiveParams?: AdaptiveParams
): AITradeSignal | null {
  const priceChange24h = crypto.price_change_percentage_24h || 0;
  
  let positionSize = portfolio.equity * qs.positionSizePercent * positionSizeMultiplier;
  // Minimum position size of $3 or 3% of equity (whichever is larger) to get meaningful P&L
  // With small accounts, need larger positions to overcome slippage/fees
  const minPositionSize = Math.max(3, portfolio.equity * 0.03);
  positionSize = Math.max(positionSize, minPositionSize);
  positionSize = Math.min(positionSize, portfolio.equity * MAX_POSITION_PERCENT);
  
  const sentimentScore = data.sentiment?.score || 0;
  const whaleFlow = data.signals?.whaleFlow;
  const liquidationData = data.liquidations.find(l => l.symbol.includes(qs.symbol));
  
  // Get signalMode from the quant signal (tagged during generation)
  const signalMode = (qs as any).signalMode as StrategyMode | undefined;
  
  return {
    symbol: qs.symbol,
    coinName: qs.coinName,
    action: "buy",
    side: qs.side,
    confidence: qs.confidence,
    targetEntry: qs.entryPrice,
    stopLoss: qs.stopLoss,
    takeProfit: qs.takeProfit,
    riskRewardRatio: qs.riskRewardRatio,
    positionSize,
    reasoning: qs.reasoning.join(". ") + ".",
    signalMode,  // Include signal mode for tracking
    signals: {
      sentiment: sentimentScore,
      whaleFlow: whaleFlow ? (whaleFlow.bullishness > 0.5 ? "accumulation" : "distribution") : undefined,
      capitalFlow: data.capitalFlow?.netFlowSignal?.direction || "neutral",
      priceChange24h,
      liquidationRisk: liquidationData?.volatilityTrap,
    },
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
}

// Learning sample type for tracking trade context
interface LearningSample {
  pnlPercent: number;
  isWin: boolean;
  side: string;
  symbol: string;
  durationMs: number;
  indicators: Record<string, boolean | undefined>;
  entryGates?: {
    zScoreGate: boolean;
    meanProximityGate: boolean;
    adxGate: boolean;
    perfectSetup: boolean;
  };
  signalMode?: StrategyMode;
}

// Store learning samples for indicator attribution
const learningSamples: LearningSample[] = [];
const MAX_SAMPLES = 200;

// Track indicator win rates for adaptive weighting
interface IndicatorAttribution {
  trades: number;
  wins: number;
  totalPnl: number;
  weight: number;
}

const indicatorAttribution: Record<string, IndicatorAttribution> = {
  rsiOversold: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  rsiOverbought: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  macdBullish: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  macdBearish: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  volatilitySqueeze: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  meanProximity: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  adxHookingUp: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  trendUp: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  trendDown: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  mvrvUndervalued: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  mvrvOvervalued: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  capitalInflow: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
  capitalOutflow: { trades: 0, wins: 0, totalPnl: 0, weight: 1.0 },
};

// Track entry gate performance for learning
interface GatePerformance {
  trades: number;
  wins: number;
  avgPnl: number;
}

const gatePerformance: Record<string, GatePerformance> = {
  meanProximityGate: { trades: 0, wins: 0, avgPnl: 0 },
  adxGate: { trades: 0, wins: 0, avgPnl: 0 },
  perfectSetup: { trades: 0, wins: 0, avgPnl: 0 },
};

// Get gate performance stats for adaptive gate requirements
export function getGatePerformance(): Record<string, { winRate: number; avgPnl: number }> {
  const result: Record<string, { winRate: number; avgPnl: number }> = {};
  for (const [key, perf] of Object.entries(gatePerformance)) {
    if (perf.trades > 0) {
      result[key] = {
        winRate: (perf.wins / perf.trades) * 100,
        avgPnl: perf.avgPnl,
      };
    }
  }
  return result;
}

export function recordLearningSample(sample: LearningSample): void {
  learningSamples.push(sample);
  
  // Trim to max samples
  while (learningSamples.length > MAX_SAMPLES) {
    learningSamples.shift();
  }
  
  // Update indicator attribution
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(sample.indicators)) {
    if (value && indicatorAttribution[key]) {
      const attr = indicatorAttribution[key];
      attr.trades++;
      attr.totalPnl += sample.pnlPercent;
      if (sample.isWin) attr.wins++;
      
      // Recalculate weight based on performance
      if (attr.trades >= 5) {
        const winRate = attr.wins / attr.trades;
        const avgPnl = attr.totalPnl / attr.trades;
        // Weight = combination of win rate and avg pnl
        attr.weight = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(2.0, 0.5 + winRate + (avgPnl / 10)));
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Update entry gate performance for learning
  if (sample.entryGates) {
    for (const [key, passed] of Object.entries(sample.entryGates)) {
      if (passed && gatePerformance[key]) {
        const perf = gatePerformance[key];
        perf.trades++;
        if (sample.isWin) perf.wins++;
        perf.avgPnl = (perf.avgPnl * (perf.trades - 1) + sample.pnlPercent) / perf.trades;
      }
    }
    
    // Log gate performance if enough data
    const gateStats = getGatePerformance();
    if (Object.keys(gateStats).length > 0) {
      console.log(`AI Learning: Gate performance - ${Object.entries(gateStats)
        .map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v.winRate.toFixed(0)}% win, ${v.avgPnl.toFixed(2)}% pnl`)
        .join(', ')}`);
    }
  }
  
  console.log(`AI Learning: Sample recorded. Total samples: ${learningSamples.length}`);
}

export function getIndicatorWeights(): Record<string, number> {
  const weights: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const [key, attr] of Object.entries(indicatorAttribution)) {
    weights[key] = attr.weight;
  }
  return weights;
}

export function getLearningSummary(): { sampleCount: number; indicatorStats: Record<string, { trades: number; winRate: number; avgPnl: number; weight: number }> } {
  const indicatorStats: Record<string, { trades: number; winRate: number; avgPnl: number; weight: number }> = {};
  
  for (const [key, attr] of Object.entries(indicatorAttribution)) {
    if (attr.trades > 0) {
      indicatorStats[key] = {
        trades: attr.trades,
        winRate: (attr.wins / attr.trades) * 100,
        avgPnl: attr.totalPnl / attr.trades,
        weight: attr.weight,
      };
    }
  }
  
  return { sampleCount: learningSamples.length, indicatorStats };
}

export function recordTradeResult(pnlPercent: number): void {
  updateStrategyPerformance(pnlPercent);
  
  const perf = getStrategyPerformance();
  console.log(`AI Trader: Trade recorded (${pnlPercent >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${pnlPercent.toFixed(2)}%). ` +
    `Win rate: ${perf.winRate.toFixed(1)}%, Expectancy: ${perf.expectancy.toFixed(2)}%`);
}

export function getQuantStrategyStats() {
  return getStrategyPerformance();
}

export async function getAITradeAdvice(
  data: MarketData,
  currentPositions: Array<{ symbol: string; side: string; unrealizedPnlPercent: number }>
): Promise<string> {
  const prompt = `You are a professional crypto trader managing a $100 paper trading portfolio.

Current Market Data:
- BTC Price: $${data.cryptos.find(c => c.symbol === "btc")?.current_price || "N/A"}
- BTC Sentiment: ${data.sentiment?.score ? (data.sentiment.score * 100).toFixed(0) + "%" : "N/A"}
- MVRV Z-Score: ${data.onchain?.btc?.mvrvZScore?.toFixed(2) || "N/A"}
- Whale Flow: ${data.signals?.whaleFlow?.bullishness ? (data.signals.whaleFlow.bullishness > 0.5 ? "Accumulation" : "Distribution") : "N/A"}
- Capital Flow: ${data.capitalFlow?.netFlowSignal?.direction || "N/A"}
- Active Divergences: ${data.signals?.divergences?.length || 0}

Current Positions: ${currentPositions.length > 0 
  ? currentPositions.map(p => `${p.side} ${p.symbol} (${p.unrealizedPnlPercent.toFixed(1)}%)`).join(", ")
  : "None"}

Based on the data, provide a brief 2-3 sentence trading outlook. Focus on actionable insights.`;

  try {
    const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: "gemini-2.0-flash" });
    const result = await model.generateContent(prompt);
    const response = result.response;
    return response.text() || "Unable to generate advice.";
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("AI Advice error:", error);
    return "Market analysis temporarily unavailable.";
  }
}

export interface BinanceCandle {
  timestamp: number;
  open: number;
  high: number;
  low: number;
  close: number;
  volume: number;
}

const BINANCE_BASE_URL = "https://api.binance.com/api/v3";

const COIN_TO_BINANCE_SYMBOL: Record<string, string> = {
  bitcoin: "BTCUSDT",
  ethereum: "ETHUSDT",
  solana: "SOLUSDT",
  "binancecoin": "BNBUSDT",
  ripple: "XRPUSDT",
  dogecoin: "DOGEUSDT",
  cardano: "ADAUSDT",
  avalanche: "AVAXUSDT",
  polkadot: "DOTUSDT",
  chainlink: "LINKUSDT",
  tron: "TRXUSDT",
  toncoin: "TONUSDT",
  shiba: "SHIBUSDT",
  litecoin: "LTCUSDT",
  polygon: "MATICUSDT",
  uniswap: "UNIUSDT",
};

export async function get4HCandlesBinance(coinId: string, days: number = 90): Promise<BinanceCandle[]> {
  const symbol = COIN_TO_BINANCE_SYMBOL[coinId.toLowerCase()];
  
  if (!symbol) {
    console.log(`[Binance] No symbol mapping for ${coinId}`);
    return [];
  }
  
  try {
    const endTime = Date.now();
    const startTime = endTime - (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    
    const url = new URL(`${BINANCE_BASE_URL}/klines`);
    url.searchParams.set("symbol", symbol);
    url.searchParams.set("interval", "4h");
    url.searchParams.set("startTime", startTime.toString());
    url.searchParams.set("endTime", endTime.toString());
    url.searchParams.set("limit", "1000");
    
    console.log(`[Binance] Fetching 4H OHLC for ${symbol} (${days} days)...`);
    
    const response = await fetch(url.toString());
    
    if (!response.ok) {
      const errorText = await response.text();
      console.error(`[Binance] API error for ${symbol}: ${response.status} ${errorText}`);
      return [];
    }
    
    const data = await response.json();
    
    const candles: BinanceCandle[] = data.map((kline: any[]) => ({
      timestamp: kline[0],
      open: parseFloat(kline[1]),
      high: parseFloat(kline[2]),
      low: parseFloat(kline[3]),
      close: parseFloat(kline[4]),
      volume: parseFloat(kline[5]),
    }));
    
    console.log(`[Binance] Got ${candles.length} candles for ${symbol}`);
    return candles;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`[Binance] Fetch error for ${coinId}:`, error);
    return [];
  }
}

export async function getMultiple4HCandlesBinance(coinIds: string[], days: number = 90): Promise<Map<string, BinanceCandle[]>> {
  console.log(`[Binance] Fetching 4H OHLC for ${coinIds.length} coins...`);
  
  const results = new Map<string, BinanceCandle[]>();
  
  for (const coinId of coinIds) {
    try {
      const candles = await get4HCandlesBinance(coinId, days);
      results.set(coinId, candles);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 200));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[Binance] Failed to fetch OHLC for ${coinId}:`, error);
      results.set(coinId, []);
    }
  }
  
  return results;
}

# [NOTE: POTENTIAL SENSITIVE DATA REDACTED BY USER BEFORE UPLOAD]
import type { CryptoData } from "@shared/schema";

// CoinGecko API configuration
// Demo keys use api.coingecko.com, Pro keys use pro-api.coingecko.com
// Pro keys typically have "pro" in the environment variable name or are explicitly configured
const apiKey = process.env.COINGECKO_API_KEY || "";
const isProKey = process.env.COINGECKO_IS_PRO === "true"; // Must be explicitly set for Pro access
const COINGECKO_BASE_URL = isProKey 
  ? "https://pro-api.coingecko.com/api/v3"
  : "https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3";
const API_KEY_HEADER = isProKey ? "x-cg-pro-api-key" : "x-cg-demo-api-key";

if (apiKey) {
  console.log(`[CoinGecko] Using ${isProKey ? 'Pro' : 'Demo'} API with API key`);
}

let cachedData: CryptoData[] | null = null;
let lastFetch: number = 0;
const CACHE_DURATION = 30000;

// OHLC data cache - 4H candles for each coin
export interface OHLCCandle {
  timestamp: number;
  open: number;
  high: number;
  low: number;
  close: number;
}

interface OHLCCache {
  data: OHLCCandle[];
  lastFetch: number;
}

const ohlcCache: Map<string, OHLCCache> = new Map();
const OHLC_CACHE_DURATION = 4 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 4 hours (matches candle timeframe)

export async function getTopCryptos(): Promise<CryptoData[]> {
  const now = Date.now();
  
  if (cachedData && now - lastFetch < CACHE_DURATION) {
    console.log("[CoinGecko] Returning cached data");
    return cachedData;
  }

  try {
    console.log("[CoinGecko] Fetching fresh data...");
    
    const params = new URLSearchParams({
      vs_currency: "usd",
      order: "volume_desc",
      per_page: "20",
      page: "1",
      sparkline: "true",
      price_change_percentage: "24h,7d",
    });

    const apiKey = process.env.COINGECKO_API_KEY;
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      "Accept": "application/json",
    };
    
    if (apiKey) {
      headers[API_KEY_HEADER] = apiKey;
    }

    const response = await fetch(
      `${COINGECKO_BASE_URL}/coins/markets?${params}`,
      { headers }
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errorText = await response.text();
      console.error("[CoinGecko] API error:", response.status, errorText);
      
      if (response.status === 429) {
        console.log("[CoinGecko] Rate limited, using cached data if available");
        if (cachedData) return cachedData;
      }
      
      throw new Error(`CoinGecko API error: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    
    const cryptos: CryptoData[] = data.map((coin: any) => ({
      id: coin.id,
      symbol: coin.symbol,
      name: coin.name,
      image: coin.image,
      current_price: coin.current_price,
      market_cap: coin.market_cap,
      market_cap_rank: coin.market_cap_rank,
      total_volume: coin.total_volume,
      price_change_percentage_24h: coin.price_change_percentage_24h,
      price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency: coin.price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency,
      sparkline_in_7d: coin.sparkline_in_7d,
      last_updated: coin.last_updated,
    }));

    cachedData = cryptos;
    lastFetch = now;
    
    console.log(`[CoinGecko] Fetched ${cryptos.length} cryptocurrencies`);
    return cryptos;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[CoinGecko] Fetch error:", error);
    
    if (cachedData) {
      console.log("[CoinGecko] Returning stale cached data");
      return cachedData;
    }
    
    throw error;
  }
}

export function getCachedCryptos(): CryptoData[] | null {
  return cachedData;
}

// Shared source of truth: Top 12 tradable cryptocurrencies by volume
// Excludes stablecoins, wrapped tokens, gold tokens, and USD derivatives
// Deduplicates by symbol
export interface TradableCrypto {
  id: string;
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  volume: number;
}

const STABLECOINS = ['usdt', 'usdc', 'dai', 'busd', 'tusd', 'usdp', 'fdusd', 'usd1'];
const EXCLUDED_IDS = [
  'wrapped-solana', 
  'binance-bridged-usdt-bnb-smart-chain', 
  'tether-gold', 
  'pax-gold', 
  'usd1-wlfi',
  'weth',
  'wbnb',
  'wrapped-bitcoin',
  'l2-standard-bridged-weth-base'
];

export async function getTop12TradableCryptos(): Promise<TradableCrypto[]> {
  const cryptos = await getTopCryptos();
  const seenSymbols = new Set<string>();
  
  return cryptos
    .filter(c => {
      const sym = c.symbol.toLowerCase();
      // Exclude stablecoins, wrapped tokens, and derivatives
      if (STABLECOINS.includes(sym) || EXCLUDED_IDS.includes(c.id)) return false;
      // Deduplicate by symbol (keep first occurrence - highest volume)
      if (seenSymbols.has(sym)) return false;
      seenSymbols.add(sym);
      return true;
    })
    .slice(0, 15)
    .map(c => ({
      id: c.id,
      symbol: c.symbol.toUpperCase(),
      name: c.name,
      volume: c.total_volume
    }));
}

/**
 * Fetch 4H OHLC candles for a specific coin (up to 90 days)
 * CoinGecko returns 4H candles for 31-90 day requests
 * @param coinId - CoinGecko coin ID (e.g., "bitcoin", "ethereum")
 * @param days - Number of days (default 90 for full historical data)
 */
export async function get4HCandles(coinId: string, days: number = 90): Promise<OHLCCandle[]> {
  const now = Date.now();
  const cached = ohlcCache.get(coinId);
  
  // Return cached data if fresh
  if (cached && now - cached.lastFetch < OHLC_CACHE_DURATION) {
    console.log(`[CoinGecko] Returning cached 4H OHLC for ${coinId}`);
    return cached.data;
  }

  try {
    console.log(`[CoinGecko] Fetching 4H OHLC for ${coinId} (${days} days)...`);
    
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      "Accept": "application/json",
    };
    
    if (apiKey) {
      headers[API_KEY_HEADER] = apiKey;
    }

    // CoinGecko OHLC endpoint
    // For Pro keys: use interval=hourly to get hourly candles (up to 90 days)
    // For Demo keys: auto-granularity (4-day intervals for 31+ days)
    const intervalParam = isProKey ? "&interval=hourly" : "";
    const response = await fetch(
      `${COINGECKO_BASE_URL}/coins/${coinId}/ohlc?vs_currency=usd&days=${days}${intervalParam}`,
      { headers }
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errorText = await response.text();
      console.error(`[CoinGecko] OHLC error for ${coinId}:`, response.status, errorText);
      
      if (response.status === 429 && cached) {
        console.log(`[CoinGecko] Rate limited, using stale OHLC cache for ${coinId}`);
        return cached.data;
      }
      
      throw new Error(`CoinGecko OHLC API error: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    
    // CoinGecko returns [timestamp, open, high, low, close] arrays
    const candles: OHLCCandle[] = data.map((candle: number[]) => ({
      timestamp: candle[0],
      open: candle[1],
      high: candle[2],
      low: candle[3],
      close: candle[4],
    }));

    // Cache the data
    ohlcCache.set(coinId, {
      data: candles,
      lastFetch: now,
    });
    
    console.log(`[CoinGecko] Fetched ${candles.length} 4H candles for ${coinId}`);
    return candles;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`[CoinGecko] OHLC fetch error for ${coinId}:`, error);
    
    // Return stale cache if available
    if (cached) {
      console.log(`[CoinGecko] Returning stale OHLC cache for ${coinId}`);
      return cached.data;
    }
    
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Fetch 4H OHLC data for multiple coins in parallel
 * Used for synthetic warm-up on startup
 */
export async function getMultiple4HCandles(coinIds: string[], days: number = 90): Promise<Map<string, OHLCCandle[]>> {
  console.log(`[CoinGecko] Fetching 4H OHLC for ${coinIds.length} coins...`);
  
  const results = new Map<string, OHLCCandle[]>();
  
  // Fetch with rate limiting (1 request per 1.5 seconds for free tier)
  for (const coinId of coinIds) {
    try {
      const candles = await get4HCandles(coinId, days);
      results.set(coinId, candles);
      
      // Rate limit delay (CoinGecko free tier: ~30 calls/min)
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2100));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`[CoinGecko] Failed to fetch OHLC for ${coinId}:`, error);
      results.set(coinId, []);
    }
  }
  
  return results;
}

/**
 * Get cached 4H candles for a coin (if available)
 */
export function getCached4HCandles(coinId: string): OHLCCandle[] | null {
  const cached = ohlcCache.get(coinId);
  return cached ? cached.data : null;
}

END OF FILE: ./server/services/coingecko.ts

START OF FILE: ./server/services/correlationStrategy.ts

import type { CryptoData, AITradeSignal, Position, TradeRecord } from "@shared/schema";
import { openPosition, closePosition, getPortfolioState, getTrades, updatePositionPrices } from "./paperTrading";

interface PriceHistory {
  symbol: string;
  prices: number[];
  returns: number[];
  timestamps: number[];
}

interface CorrelationPair {
  pair1: string;
  pair2: string;
  currentCorrelation: number;
  historicalCorrelation: number;
  correlationDelta: number;
  zScore: number;
  spreadReturn: number;
}

interface PairPosition {
  pairId: string;
  longSymbol: string;
  shortSymbol: string;
  longPositionId: string | null;
  shortPositionId: string | null;
  entrySpread: number;
  entryTime: string;
  targetSpread: number;
  hedgeRatio: number;      // OLS-derived β for proper position sizing
  entryZScore: number;     // Z-score at entry for monitoring
}

export interface StrategyMetrics {
  sharpeRatio: number;
  ulcerIndex: number;
  sharpeUlcerRatio: number;
  totalReturn: number;
  annualizedReturn: number;
  maxDrawdown: number;
  winRate: number;
  totalTrades: number;
  avgHoldingPeriodHours: number;
}

interface CorrelationStrategyState {
  priceHistory: Map<string, PriceHistory>;
  correlationMatrix: Map<string, number>;
  activePairs: PairPosition[];
  dailyReturns: number[];
  equityHistory: number[];
  lastAnalysis: number;
  enabled: boolean;
}

const LOOKBACK_PERIOD = 20;
const CORRELATION_THRESHOLD = 0.6;
const ZSCORE_ENTRY = 2.0;
const ZSCORE_EXIT = 0.0; // Per spec: Exit when Z-Score returns to 0 (mean)
const ZSCORE_BROKEN_LEASH = 3.5; // Per Mike1 spec: Kill switch when correlation breaks down
const MAX_PAIRS = 3;
const ANALYSIS_COOLDOWN = 60000;

let strategyState: CorrelationStrategyState = {
  priceHistory: new Map(),
  correlationMatrix: new Map(),
  activePairs: [],
  dailyReturns: [],
  equityHistory: [100],
  lastAnalysis: 0,
  enabled: true,
};

const STABLECOINS = ['usdt', 'usdc', 'dai', 'busd', 'tusd', 'usdp', 'usdd', 'frax', 'lusd', 'susd'];

function isStablecoin(symbol: string): boolean {
  return STABLECOINS.includes(symbol.toLowerCase());
}

function calculateReturns(prices: number[]): number[] {
  const returns: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < prices.length; i++) {
    returns.push((prices[i] - prices[i - 1]) / prices[i - 1]);
  }
  return returns;
}

function calculateCorrelation(returns1: number[], returns2: number[]): number {
  if (returns1.length !== returns2.length || returns1.length < 5) return 0;
  
  const n = returns1.length;
  const mean1 = returns1.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
  const mean2 = returns2.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
  
  let cov = 0;
  let var1 = 0;
  let var2 = 0;
  
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const diff1 = returns1[i] - mean1;
    const diff2 = returns2[i] - mean2;
    cov += diff1 * diff2;
    var1 += diff1 * diff1;
    var2 += diff2 * diff2;
  }
  
  const std1 = Math.sqrt(var1 / n);
  const std2 = Math.sqrt(var2 / n);
  
  if (std1 === 0 || std2 === 0) return 0;
  
  return cov / (n * std1 * std2);
}

function calculateZScore(currentSpread: number, spreadHistory: number[]): number {
  if (spreadHistory.length < 5) return 0;
  
  const mean = spreadHistory.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / spreadHistory.length;
  const variance = spreadHistory.reduce((sum, val) => sum + Math.pow(val - mean, 2), 0) / spreadHistory.length;
  const std = Math.sqrt(variance);
  
  if (std === 0) return 0;
  return (currentSpread - mean) / std;
}

// ENGLE-GRANGER COINTEGRATION TEST
// Per spec: Use statsmodels-style cointegration check (p-value < 0.05)
// Since we can't use Python statsmodels, we implement ADF-like test on OLS residuals
function testCointegration(
  pricesY: number[],
  pricesX: number[]
): { isCointegrated: boolean; pValue: number; adfStatistic: number } {
  if (pricesY.length < 20 || pricesX.length < 20) {
    return { isCointegrated: false, pValue: 1.0, adfStatistic: 0 };
  }
  
  const minLen = Math.min(pricesY.length, pricesX.length);
  const logY = pricesY.slice(-minLen).map(p => Math.log(p));
  const logX = pricesX.slice(-minLen).map(p => Math.log(p));
  
  // Step 1: Run OLS regression to get residuals
  const n = logY.length;
  const meanY = logY.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
  const meanX = logX.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
  
  let numerator = 0;
  let denominator = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    numerator += (logX[i] - meanX) * (logY[i] - meanY);
    denominator += (logX[i] - meanX) * (logX[i] - meanX);
  }
  
  const beta = denominator !== 0 ? numerator / denominator : 1.0;
  const alpha = meanY - beta * meanX;
  
  // Residuals = Y - (alpha + beta * X)
  const residuals: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    residuals.push(logY[i] - (alpha + beta * logX[i]));
  }
  
  // Step 2: ADF test on residuals (simplified Dickey-Fuller)
  // Test statistic: t-stat from regression: ΔY = α + γY_{t-1} + ε
  const deltaResiduals: number[] = [];
  const laggedResiduals: number[] = [];
  
  for (let i = 1; i < residuals.length; i++) {
    deltaResiduals.push(residuals[i] - residuals[i - 1]);
    laggedResiduals.push(residuals[i - 1]);
  }
  
  // Regress delta on lagged
  const m = deltaResiduals.length;
  const meanDelta = deltaResiduals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / m;
  const meanLag = laggedResiduals.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / m;
  
  let numAdf = 0, denAdf = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < m; i++) {
    numAdf += (laggedResiduals[i] - meanLag) * (deltaResiduals[i] - meanDelta);
    denAdf += (laggedResiduals[i] - meanLag) * (laggedResiduals[i] - meanLag);
  }
  
  const gamma = denAdf !== 0 ? numAdf / denAdf : 0;
  
  // Calculate standard error of gamma
  let ssResid = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < m; i++) {
    const predicted = meanDelta + gamma * (laggedResiduals[i] - meanLag);
    ssResid += Math.pow(deltaResiduals[i] - predicted, 2);
  }
  const mse = ssResid / (m - 2);
  const seGamma = Math.sqrt(mse / denAdf);
  
  const adfStatistic = seGamma !== 0 ? gamma / seGamma : 0;
  
  // Critical values for Engle-Granger (n=50): -3.34 (5%), -2.93 (10%)
  // Use simplified p-value approximation based on ADF statistic
  let pValue: number;
  if (adfStatistic < -3.5) pValue = 0.01;
  else if (adfStatistic < -3.0) pValue = 0.05;
  else if (adfStatistic < -2.5) pValue = 0.10;
  else if (adfStatistic < -2.0) pValue = 0.25;
  else pValue = 0.50;
  
  const isCointegrated = pValue < 0.05;
  
  console.log(`[Cointegration] ADF stat=${adfStatistic.toFixed(3)}, p-value≈${pValue.toFixed(3)}, cointegrated=${isCointegrated}`);
  
  return { isCointegrated, pValue, adfStatistic };
}

// OLS REGRESSION FOR HEDGE RATIO (BETA) CALCULATION
// Per Mike1 spec: Run OLS Regression: ln(Y) = α + β × ln(X)
// β is the multiplier - if β = 1.5, Short $1.50 of X for every $1.00 Long of Y
// This ensures proper hedge ratio instead of naive 1:1 sizing
function calculateOLSHedgeRatio(
  pricesY: number[], // Long asset prices
  pricesX: number[]  // Short asset prices
): { beta: number; alpha: number; rSquared: number } {
  if (pricesY.length < 10 || pricesX.length < 10) {
    return { beta: 1.0, alpha: 0, rSquared: 0 };
  }
  
  // Use log prices for proper cointegration analysis
  const minLen = Math.min(pricesY.length, pricesX.length);
  const logY = pricesY.slice(-minLen).map(p => Math.log(p));
  const logX = pricesX.slice(-minLen).map(p => Math.log(p));
  
  const n = logY.length;
  const meanY = logY.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
  const meanX = logX.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / n;
  
  // Calculate β (slope) and α (intercept) using least squares
  let numerator = 0;
  let denominator = 0;
  
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const xDiff = logX[i] - meanX;
    const yDiff = logY[i] - meanY;
    numerator += xDiff * yDiff;
    denominator += xDiff * xDiff;
  }
  
  const beta = denominator !== 0 ? numerator / denominator : 1.0;
  const alpha = meanY - beta * meanX;
  
  // Calculate R² (coefficient of determination)
  const ssTotal = logY.reduce((sum, y) => sum + Math.pow(y - meanY, 2), 0);
  const ssResidual = logY.reduce((sum, y, i) => {
    const predicted = alpha + beta * logX[i];
    return sum + Math.pow(y - predicted, 2);
  }, 0);
  
  const rSquared = ssTotal > 0 ? 1 - (ssResidual / ssTotal) : 0;
  
  console.log(`[OLS Hedge] β=${beta.toFixed(4)}, α=${alpha.toFixed(4)}, R²=${rSquared.toFixed(4)}`);
  
  return { beta, alpha, rSquared };
}

// Check for "Broken Leash" condition - correlation breakdown
// Per Mike1 spec: Hard stop if Spread Z-Score expands beyond 3.5 standard deviations
function isBrokenLeash(zScore: number): boolean {
  const absZ = Math.abs(zScore);
  if (absZ > ZSCORE_BROKEN_LEASH) {
    console.log(`[BROKEN LEASH] Z-Score ${zScore.toFixed(2)} exceeds ${ZSCORE_BROKEN_LEASH} - correlation failure detected!`);
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

export function calculateSharpeRatio(returns: number[], riskFreeRate: number = 0): number {
  if (returns.length < 5) return 0;
  
  const meanReturn = returns.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / returns.length;
  const excessReturn = meanReturn - (riskFreeRate / 252);
  
  const variance = returns.reduce((sum, r) => sum + Math.pow(r - meanReturn, 2), 0) / returns.length;
  const std = Math.sqrt(variance);
  
  if (std === 0) return 0;
  
  return (excessReturn / std) * Math.sqrt(252);
}

export function calculateUlcerIndex(equityHistory: number[]): number {
  if (equityHistory.length < 2) return 0;
  
  let peak = equityHistory[0];
  let sumSquaredDrawdown = 0;
  
  for (const equity of equityHistory) {
    if (equity > peak) {
      peak = equity;
    }
    const drawdown = ((peak - equity) / peak) * 100;
    sumSquaredDrawdown += drawdown * drawdown;
  }
  
  return Math.sqrt(sumSquaredDrawdown / equityHistory.length);
}

function calculateMaxDrawdown(equityHistory: number[]): number {
  if (equityHistory.length < 2) return 0;
  
  let peak = equityHistory[0];
  let maxDD = 0;
  
  for (const equity of equityHistory) {
    if (equity > peak) peak = equity;
    const dd = (peak - equity) / peak;
    if (dd > maxDD) maxDD = dd;
  }
  
  return maxDD * 100;
}

export function updatePriceHistory(cryptos: CryptoData[]): void {
  const now = Date.now();
  
  for (const crypto of cryptos) {
    if (isStablecoin(crypto.symbol)) continue;
    
    const symbol = crypto.symbol.toLowerCase();
    let history = strategyState.priceHistory.get(symbol);
    
    if (!history) {
      history = {
        symbol,
        prices: [],
        returns: [],
        timestamps: [],
      };
      strategyState.priceHistory.set(symbol, history);
    }
    
    if (history.timestamps.length === 0 || now - history.timestamps[history.timestamps.length - 1] >= 30000) {
      history.prices.push(crypto.current_price);
      history.timestamps.push(now);
      
      if (history.prices.length > LOOKBACK_PERIOD + 1) {
        history.prices.shift();
        history.timestamps.shift();
      }
      
      history.returns = calculateReturns(history.prices);
    }
  }
}

export function findCorrelationPairs(): CorrelationPair[] {
  const pairs: CorrelationPair[] = [];
  const symbols = Array.from(strategyState.priceHistory.keys());
  
  for (let i = 0; i < symbols.length; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < symbols.length; j++) {
      const sym1 = symbols[i];
      const sym2 = symbols[j];
      
      const history1 = strategyState.priceHistory.get(sym1);
      const history2 = strategyState.priceHistory.get(sym2);
      
      if (!history1 || !history2) continue;
      if (history1.returns.length < 10 || history2.returns.length < 10) continue;
      if (history1.prices.length < 20 || history2.prices.length < 20) continue;
      
      const minLen = Math.min(history1.returns.length, history2.returns.length);
      const returns1 = history1.returns.slice(-minLen);
      const returns2 = history2.returns.slice(-minLen);
      
      const currentCorr = calculateCorrelation(returns1, returns2);
      
      // Per spec: Use cointegration test (p-value < 0.05) instead of just correlation
      const cointegrationResult = testCointegration(history1.prices, history2.prices);
      
      // Skip pairs that fail both correlation and cointegration tests
      if (Math.abs(currentCorr) < CORRELATION_THRESHOLD && !cointegrationResult.isCointegrated) {
        continue;
      }
      
      const halfLen = Math.floor(minLen / 2);
      const historicalCorr = halfLen >= 5 
        ? calculateCorrelation(returns1.slice(0, halfLen), returns2.slice(0, halfLen))
        : currentCorr;
      
      const corrDelta = currentCorr - historicalCorr;
      
      const spreadReturns: number[] = [];
      for (let k = 0; k < minLen; k++) {
        spreadReturns.push(returns1[k] - returns2[k]);
      }
      
      const currentSpread = spreadReturns[spreadReturns.length - 1] || 0;
      const zScore = calculateZScore(currentSpread, spreadReturns);
      
      // Prefer cointegrated pairs, but also accept highly correlated pairs
      if (cointegrationResult.isCointegrated || Math.abs(currentCorr) >= CORRELATION_THRESHOLD) {
        pairs.push({
          pair1: sym1,
          pair2: sym2,
          currentCorrelation: currentCorr,
          historicalCorrelation: historicalCorr,
          correlationDelta: corrDelta,
          zScore,
          spreadReturn: currentSpread,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  
  return pairs.sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.zScore) - Math.abs(a.zScore));
}

function getCryptoName(symbol: string, cryptos: CryptoData[]): string {
  const crypto = cryptos.find(c => c.symbol.toLowerCase() === symbol.toLowerCase());
  return crypto?.name || symbol.toUpperCase();
}

export async function analyzeCorrelationOpportunities(
  cryptos: CryptoData[]
): Promise<{ signals: any[]; executedTrades: any[] }> {
  const now = Date.now();
  const signals: any[] = [];
  const executedTrades: any[] = [];
  
  if (!strategyState.enabled) {
    return { signals, executedTrades };
  }
  
  if (now - strategyState.lastAnalysis < ANALYSIS_COOLDOWN) {
    return { signals, executedTrades };
  }
  
  strategyState.lastAnalysis = now;
  
  updatePriceHistory(cryptos);
  
  const priceMap = new Map(cryptos.map(c => [c.symbol.toLowerCase(), c.current_price]));
  updatePositionPrices(priceMap);
  
  const portfolio = getPortfolioState();
  strategyState.equityHistory.push(portfolio.equity);
  if (strategyState.equityHistory.length > 252) {
    strategyState.equityHistory.shift();
  }
  
  if (strategyState.equityHistory.length >= 2) {
    const prevEquity = strategyState.equityHistory[strategyState.equityHistory.length - 2];
    const currEquity = strategyState.equityHistory[strategyState.equityHistory.length - 1];
    const dailyReturn = (currEquity - prevEquity) / prevEquity;
    strategyState.dailyReturns.push(dailyReturn);
    if (strategyState.dailyReturns.length > 252) {
      strategyState.dailyReturns.shift();
    }
  }
  
  for (const pair of [...strategyState.activePairs]) {
    const history1 = strategyState.priceHistory.get(pair.longSymbol);
    const history2 = strategyState.priceHistory.get(pair.shortSymbol);
    
    if (!history1 || !history2) continue;
    
    const minLen = Math.min(history1.returns.length, history2.returns.length);
    if (minLen < 5) continue;
    
    const spreadReturns: number[] = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < minLen; k++) {
      spreadReturns.push(history1.returns[k] - history2.returns[k]);
    }
    
    const currentSpread = spreadReturns[spreadReturns.length - 1] || 0;
    const zScore = calculateZScore(currentSpread, spreadReturns);
    
    // Per Mike1 spec: BROKEN LEASH check - correlation failure kill switch
    // Exit immediately if z-score exceeds 3.5 (correlation breakdown)
    if (isBrokenLeash(zScore)) {
      console.log(`[BROKEN LEASH] Force closing pair ${pair.longSymbol}/${pair.shortSymbol} - correlation failure!`);
      
      if (pair.longPositionId) {
        const longPrice = priceMap.get(pair.longSymbol);
        if (longPrice) {
          const longResult = closePosition(pair.longPositionId, longPrice, "stopped_out");
          if (longResult) {
            executedTrades.push({ type: 'close_long', symbol: pair.longSymbol, reason: 'broken_leash' });
          }
        }
      }
      if (pair.shortPositionId) {
        const shortPrice = priceMap.get(pair.shortSymbol);
        if (shortPrice) {
          const shortResult = closePosition(pair.shortPositionId, shortPrice, "stopped_out");
          if (shortResult) {
            executedTrades.push({ type: 'close_short', symbol: pair.shortSymbol, reason: 'broken_leash' });
          }
        }
      }
      
      strategyState.activePairs = strategyState.activePairs.filter(p => p.pairId !== pair.pairId);
      continue;
    }
    
    // Normal exit: z-score reverted to mean
    if (Math.abs(zScore) < ZSCORE_EXIT) {
      console.log(`[Correlation Strategy] Closing pair ${pair.longSymbol}/${pair.shortSymbol} - z-score reverted to ${zScore.toFixed(2)}`);
      
      if (pair.longPositionId) {
        const longPrice = priceMap.get(pair.longSymbol);
        if (longPrice) {
          const longResult = closePosition(pair.longPositionId, longPrice, "closed");
          if (longResult) {
            executedTrades.push({ type: 'close_long', symbol: pair.longSymbol, reason: 'spread_reversion' });
          }
        }
      }
      if (pair.shortPositionId) {
        const shortPrice = priceMap.get(pair.shortSymbol);
        if (shortPrice) {
          const shortResult = closePosition(pair.shortPositionId, shortPrice, "closed");
          if (shortResult) {
            executedTrades.push({ type: 'close_short', symbol: pair.shortSymbol, reason: 'spread_reversion' });
          }
        }
      }
      
      strategyState.activePairs = strategyState.activePairs.filter(p => p.pairId !== pair.pairId);
    }
  }
  
  if (strategyState.activePairs.length >= MAX_PAIRS) {
    return { signals, executedTrades };
  }
  
  const correlationPairs = findCorrelationPairs();
  
  for (const pair of correlationPairs) {
    if (strategyState.activePairs.length >= MAX_PAIRS) break;
    
    const alreadyTrading = strategyState.activePairs.some(
      p => (p.longSymbol === pair.pair1 && p.shortSymbol === pair.pair2) ||
           (p.longSymbol === pair.pair2 && p.shortSymbol === pair.pair1)
    );
    
    if (alreadyTrading) continue;
    
    if (Math.abs(pair.zScore) >= ZSCORE_ENTRY) {
      const longSymbol = pair.zScore < 0 ? pair.pair1 : pair.pair2;
      const shortSymbol = pair.zScore < 0 ? pair.pair2 : pair.pair1;
      
      const longPrice = priceMap.get(longSymbol);
      const shortPrice = priceMap.get(shortSymbol);
      
      if (!longPrice || !shortPrice) continue;
      
      // Per Mike1 spec: Calculate OLS hedge ratio for proper position sizing
      const longHistory = strategyState.priceHistory.get(longSymbol);
      const shortHistory = strategyState.priceHistory.get(shortSymbol);
      
      let hedgeRatio = 1.0; // Default to 1:1 if not enough data
      if (longHistory && shortHistory && longHistory.prices.length >= 10) {
        const olsResult = calculateOLSHedgeRatio(longHistory.prices, shortHistory.prices);
        // Only use hedge ratio if R² is reasonable (good fit)
        if (olsResult.rSquared > 0.3) {
          hedgeRatio = Math.abs(olsResult.beta);
          // Cap hedge ratio to prevent extreme imbalances
          hedgeRatio = Math.min(Math.max(hedgeRatio, 0.5), 2.0);
        }
      }
      
      signals.push({
        type: 'correlation_pair',
        longSymbol: longSymbol.toUpperCase(),
        shortSymbol: shortSymbol.toUpperCase(),
        zScore: pair.zScore,
        correlation: pair.currentCorrelation,
        correlationDelta: pair.correlationDelta,
        confidence: Math.min(1, Math.abs(pair.zScore) / 3),
        hedgeRatio,
      });
      
      console.log(`[Correlation Strategy] Opening pair: Long ${longSymbol.toUpperCase()}, Short ${shortSymbol.toUpperCase()}`);
      console.log(`  Z-Score: ${pair.zScore.toFixed(2)}, Correlation: ${pair.currentCorrelation.toFixed(2)}, Hedge β: ${hedgeRatio.toFixed(3)}`);
      
      // Per Mike1 spec: Use hedge ratio (β) for position sizing
      // Short $β of X for every $1.00 Long of Y
      const baseLongSize = 0.03; // 3% of equity for long leg
      const adjustedShortSize = baseLongSize * hedgeRatio; // β-weighted short size
      
      const longSignal: AITradeSignal = {
        symbol: longSymbol,
        coinName: getCryptoName(longSymbol, cryptos),
        action: 'buy',
        side: 'long',
        confidence: Math.min(1, Math.abs(pair.zScore) / 3),
        targetEntry: longPrice,
        stopLoss: longPrice * 0.95,
        takeProfit: longPrice * 1.03,
        riskRewardRatio: 0.6,
        positionSize: baseLongSize,
        reasoning: `Correlation pair trade: z-score ${pair.zScore.toFixed(2)}, hedge β=${hedgeRatio.toFixed(3)}`,
        signals: {
          correlation: pair.currentCorrelation,
        },
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      
      const shortSignal: AITradeSignal = {
        symbol: shortSymbol,
        coinName: getCryptoName(shortSymbol, cryptos),
        action: 'sell',
        side: 'short',
        confidence: Math.min(1, Math.abs(pair.zScore) / 3),
        targetEntry: shortPrice,
        stopLoss: shortPrice * 1.05,
        takeProfit: shortPrice * 0.97,
        riskRewardRatio: 0.6,
        positionSize: adjustedShortSize, // β-weighted position size
        reasoning: `Correlation pair trade: z-score ${pair.zScore.toFixed(2)}, hedge β=${hedgeRatio.toFixed(3)}`,
        signals: {
          correlation: pair.currentCorrelation,
        },
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      
      const longResult = openPosition(longSignal);
      const shortResult = openPosition(shortSignal);
      
      if (longResult || shortResult) {
        strategyState.activePairs.push({
          pairId: `${longSymbol}-${shortSymbol}-${Date.now()}`,
          longSymbol,
          shortSymbol,
          longPositionId: longResult?.id || null,
          shortPositionId: shortResult?.id || null,
          entrySpread: pair.spreadReturn,
          entryTime: new Date().toISOString(),
          targetSpread: 0,
          hedgeRatio,            // Store β for monitoring
          entryZScore: pair.zScore, // Store entry z-score
        });
        
        if (longResult) {
          executedTrades.push({ 
            type: 'open_long', 
            symbol: longSymbol, 
            price: longResult.entryPrice,
            reason: 'correlation_divergence',
            hedgeRatio,
          });
        }
        if (shortResult) {
          executedTrades.push({ 
            type: 'open_short', 
            symbol: shortSymbol, 
            price: shortResult.entryPrice,
            reason: 'correlation_divergence',
            hedgeRatio,
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }
  
  return { signals, executedTrades };
}

export function getCorrelationStrategyMetrics(): StrategyMetrics {
  const allTrades = getTrades();
  const correlationTrades = allTrades.filter(t => 
    t.aiReasoning?.includes('Correlation pair') || t.aiReasoning?.includes('correlation')
  );
  const winningTrades = correlationTrades.filter(t => (t.pnl || 0) > 0);
  
  const sharpeRatio = calculateSharpeRatio(strategyState.dailyReturns);
  const ulcerIndex = calculateUlcerIndex(strategyState.equityHistory);
  
  const sharpeUlcerRatio = ulcerIndex > 0 ? sharpeRatio / ulcerIndex : sharpeRatio;
  
  const totalReturn = strategyState.equityHistory.length > 0
    ? ((strategyState.equityHistory[strategyState.equityHistory.length - 1] - 100) / 100) * 100
    : 0;
  
  const daysTrading = strategyState.dailyReturns.length || 1;
  const annualizedReturn = totalReturn * (252 / daysTrading);
  
  const maxDrawdown = calculateMaxDrawdown(strategyState.equityHistory);
  
  let totalHoldingMs = 0;
  for (const trade of correlationTrades) {
    if (trade.openedAt && trade.closedAt) {
      const openTime = new Date(trade.openedAt).getTime();
      const closeTime = new Date(trade.closedAt).getTime();
      totalHoldingMs += closeTime - openTime;
    }
  }
  const avgHoldingPeriodHours = correlationTrades.length > 0 ? (totalHoldingMs / correlationTrades.length) / (1000 * 60 * 60) : 0;
  
  return {
    sharpeRatio,
    ulcerIndex,
    sharpeUlcerRatio,
    totalReturn,
    annualizedReturn,
    maxDrawdown,
    winRate: correlationTrades.length > 0 ? (winningTrades.length / correlationTrades.length) * 100 : 0,
    totalTrades: correlationTrades.length,
    avgHoldingPeriodHours,
  };
}

export function getOverallRiskMetrics(): StrategyMetrics {
  const allTrades = getTrades();
  const winningTrades = allTrades.filter(t => (t.pnl || 0) > 0);
  
  const sharpeRatio = calculateSharpeRatio(strategyState.dailyReturns);
  const ulcerIndex = calculateUlcerIndex(strategyState.equityHistory);
  const sharpeUlcerRatio = ulcerIndex > 0 ? sharpeRatio / ulcerIndex : sharpeRatio;
  
  const portfolio = getPortfolioState();
  const totalReturn = ((portfolio.equity - 100) / 100) * 100;
  
  const daysTrading = strategyState.dailyReturns.length || 1;
  const annualizedReturn = totalReturn * (252 / daysTrading);
  
  const maxDrawdown = calculateMaxDrawdown(strategyState.equityHistory);
  
  let totalHoldingMs = 0;
  for (const trade of allTrades) {
    if (trade.openedAt && trade.closedAt) {
      const openTime = new Date(trade.openedAt).getTime();
      const closeTime = new Date(trade.closedAt).getTime();
      totalHoldingMs += closeTime - openTime;
    }
  }
  const avgHoldingPeriodHours = allTrades.length > 0 ? (totalHoldingMs / allTrades.length) / (1000 * 60 * 60) : 0;
  
  return {
    sharpeRatio,
    ulcerIndex,
    sharpeUlcerRatio,
    totalReturn,
    annualizedReturn,
    maxDrawdown,
    winRate: allTrades.length > 0 ? (winningTrades.length / allTrades.length) * 100 : 0,
    totalTrades: allTrades.length,
    avgHoldingPeriodHours,
  };
}

// Get price history maps for dynamic order updates
export function getPriceHistoryMaps(): { 
  priceHistory: Map<string, number[]>;
  atrMap: Map<string, number>;
  volatilityMap: Map<string, number>;
} {
  const priceHistory = new Map<string, number[]>();
  const atrMap = new Map<string, number>();
  const volatilityMap = new Map<string, number>();
  
  strategyState.priceHistory.forEach((history, symbol) => {
    if (history.prices.length >= 10) {
      priceHistory.set(symbol, [...history.prices]);
      
      // Calculate ATR from price history (using close-to-close as proxy)
      const prices = history.prices;
      const trueRanges: number[] = [];
      for (let i = 1; i < prices.length; i++) {
        const priceChange = Math.abs(prices[i] - prices[i - 1]);
        // Multiply by 1.5 to approximate intraday high-low from close-to-close
        trueRanges.push(priceChange * 1.5);
      }
      if (trueRanges.length >= 14) {
        const atr = trueRanges.slice(-14).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / 14;
        atrMap.set(symbol, atr);
      }
      
      // Calculate volatility from returns
      if (history.returns.length >= 10) {
        const recentReturns = history.returns.slice(-20);
        const mean = recentReturns.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / recentReturns.length;
        const variance = recentReturns.reduce((sum, r) => sum + Math.pow(r - mean, 2), 0) / recentReturns.length;
        const volatility = Math.sqrt(variance);
        volatilityMap.set(symbol, volatility);
      }
    }
  });
  
  return { priceHistory, atrMap, volatilityMap };
}
