====================
// Using native fetch (Node.js 18+)

interface StablecoinData {
  date: string;
  totalCirculating: { peggedUSD: number };
  totalCirculatingUSD: { peggedUSD: number };
}

interface BridgeData {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  displayName: string;
  volumePrevDay: number;
  volumePrev2Day: number;
  last24hVolume: number;
  weeklyVolume: number;
  monthlyVolume: number;
}

interface FlowMetrics {
  stablecoinSupply: {
    current: number;
    previous24h: number;
    delta24h: number;
    deltaPercent: number;
    interpretation: string;
  };
  bridgeActivity: {
    totalVolume24h: number;
    totalVolumePrevDay: number;
    delta24h: number;
    deltaPercent: number;
    topBridges: Array<{
      name: string;
      volume24h: number;
      volumeChange: number;
    }>;
    interpretation: string;
  };
  netFlowSignal: {
    score: number;
    direction: "inflow" | "outflow" | "neutral";
    strength: "strong" | "moderate" | "weak";
    description: string;
  };
  lastUpdated: string;
}

const CACHE_TTL = 5 * 60 * 1000;
let cache: { data: FlowMetrics | null; timestamp: number } = {
  data: null,
  timestamp: 0,
};

async function fetchStablecoinData(): Promise<StablecoinData[]> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(
      "https://stablecoins.llama.fi/stablecoincharts/all?stablecoin=1",
      {
        headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
        timeout: 10000,
      } as any
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Stablecoin API error: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const data = (await response.json()) as StablecoinData[];
    return data.slice(-7);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to fetch stablecoin data:", error);
    return [];
  }
}

async function fetchBridgeData(): Promise<BridgeData[]> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch("https://bridges.llama.fi/bridges", {
      headers: { Accept: "application/json" },
      timeout: 10000,
    } as any);

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Bridge API error: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const data = (await response.json()) as { bridges: BridgeData[] };
    return data.bridges || [];
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to fetch bridge data:", error);
    return [];
  }
}

function interpretStablecoinFlow(deltaPercent: number): string {
  if (deltaPercent > 0.5) return "Strong fiat inflows - bullish signal";
  if (deltaPercent > 0.1) return "Moderate fiat inflows - positive";
  if (deltaPercent < -0.5) return "Strong fiat outflows - bearish signal";
  if (deltaPercent < -0.1) return "Moderate fiat outflows - caution";
  return "Stable supply - neutral market";
}

function interpretBridgeActivity(deltaPercent: number): string {
  if (deltaPercent > 50) return "Surge in cross-chain activity - high interest";
  if (deltaPercent > 20) return "Increased bridge usage - growing activity";
  if (deltaPercent < -30) return "Declining cross-chain transfers - lower activity";
  if (deltaPercent < -10) return "Slightly reduced bridge volume";
  return "Normal cross-chain activity levels";
}

function calculateNetFlowSignal(
  stableDelta: number,
  bridgeDelta: number
): FlowMetrics["netFlowSignal"] {
  const stableScore = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, stableDelta / 0.5));
  const bridgeScore = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, bridgeDelta / 50));
  const combinedScore = stableScore * 0.7 + bridgeScore * 0.3;

  let direction: "inflow" | "outflow" | "neutral" = "neutral";
  let strength: "strong" | "moderate" | "weak" = "weak";
  let description = "";

  if (combinedScore > 0.3) {
    direction = "inflow";
    strength = combinedScore > 0.6 ? "strong" : "moderate";
    description = "Capital flowing into crypto markets";
  } else if (combinedScore < -0.3) {
    direction = "outflow";
    strength = combinedScore < -0.6 ? "strong" : "moderate";
    description = "Capital flowing out of crypto markets";
  } else {
    direction = "neutral";
    strength = "weak";
    description = "Balanced capital flows";
  }

  return {
    score: Math.round(combinedScore * 100) / 100,
    direction,
    strength,
    description,
  };
}

export async function getFlowMetrics(): Promise<FlowMetrics | null> {
  const now = Date.now();

  if (cache.data && now - cache.timestamp < CACHE_TTL) {
    return cache.data;
  }

  try {
    const [stablecoinData, bridgeData] = await Promise.all([
      fetchStablecoinData(),
      fetchBridgeData(),
    ]);

    if (stablecoinData.length < 2) {
      console.error("Insufficient stablecoin data");
      return cache.data;
    }

    const latestStable = stablecoinData[stablecoinData.length - 1];
    const prevStable = stablecoinData[stablecoinData.length - 2];

    const currentSupply = latestStable.totalCirculatingUSD?.peggedUSD || 0;
    const prevSupply = prevStable.totalCirculatingUSD?.peggedUSD || 0;
    const supplyDelta = currentSupply - prevSupply;
    const supplyDeltaPercent =
      prevSupply > 0 ? (supplyDelta / prevSupply) * 100 : 0;

    const topBridges = bridgeData
      .filter((b) => b.last24hVolume > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.last24hVolume - a.last24hVolume)
      .slice(0, 5)
      .map((b) => ({
        name: b.displayName || b.name,
        volume24h: b.last24hVolume,
        volumeChange:
          b.volumePrev2Day > 0
            ? ((b.volumePrevDay - b.volumePrev2Day) / b.volumePrev2Day) * 100
            : 0,
      }));

    const totalVolume24h = bridgeData.reduce(
      (sum, b) => sum + (b.last24hVolume || 0),
      0
    );
    const totalVolumePrevDay = bridgeData.reduce(
      (sum, b) => sum + (b.volumePrev2Day || 0),
      0
    );
    const bridgeDelta = totalVolume24h - totalVolumePrevDay;
    const bridgeDeltaPercent =
      totalVolumePrevDay > 0 ? (bridgeDelta / totalVolumePrevDay) * 100 : 0;

    const metrics: FlowMetrics = {
      stablecoinSupply: {
        current: currentSupply,
        previous24h: prevSupply,
        delta24h: supplyDelta,
        deltaPercent: Math.round(supplyDeltaPercent * 1000) / 1000,
        interpretation: interpretStablecoinFlow(supplyDeltaPercent),
      },
      bridgeActivity: {
        totalVolume24h,
        totalVolumePrevDay,
        delta24h: bridgeDelta,
        deltaPercent: Math.round(bridgeDeltaPercent * 100) / 100,
        topBridges,
        interpretation: interpretBridgeActivity(bridgeDeltaPercent),
      },
      netFlowSignal: calculateNetFlowSignal(
        supplyDeltaPercent,
        bridgeDeltaPercent
      ),
      lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    cache = { data: metrics, timestamp: now };
    return metrics;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Failed to calculate flow metrics:", error);
    return cache.data;
  }
}

# [NOTE: POTENTIAL SENSITIVE DATA REDACTED BY USER BEFORE UPLOAD]
import type { WhaleTransaction } from "@shared/schema";

const HELIUS_BASE_URL = "https://api.helius.xyz/v0";
const WHALE_THRESHOLD_USD = 100000;
const WHALE_THRESHOLD_SOL = 500;

let cachedTransactions: WhaleTransaction[] = [];
let lastFetch: number = 0;
const CACHE_DURATION = 15000;

const SOL_MINT = "So11111111111111111111111111111111111111112";
const USDC_MINT = "EPjFWdd5AufqSSqeM2qN1xzybapC8G4wEGGkZwyTDt1v";
const USDT_MINT = "Es9vMFrzaCERmJfrF4H2FYD4KCoNkY11McCe8BenwNYB";

const TOKEN_SYMBOLS: Record<string, string> = {
  [SOL_MINT]: "SOL",
  [USDC_MINT]: "USDC",
  [USDT_MINT]: "USDT",
};

export async function getWhaleTransactions(): Promise<WhaleTransaction[]> {
  const apiKey = process.env.HELIUS_API_KEY;
  
  if (!apiKey) {
    console.log("[Helius] No API key configured, returning empty array");
    return cachedTransactions;
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  
  if (cachedTransactions.length > 0 && now - lastFetch < CACHE_DURATION) {
    console.log("[Helius] Returning cached transactions");
    return cachedTransactions;
  }

  try {
    console.log("[Helius] Fetching recent transactions...");
    
    const response = await fetch(
      `${HELIUS_BASE_URL}/addresses/EPjFWdd5AufqSSqeM2qN1xzybapC8G4wEGGkZwyTDt1v/transactions?api-key=${apiKey}&limit=50`,
      {
        headers: {
          "Accept": "application/json",
        },
      }
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      const errorText = await response.text();
      console.error("[Helius] API error:", response.status, errorText);
      
      if (response.status === 429 || response.status === 401) {
        console.log("[Helius] Rate limited or unauthorized, returning cached data");
        return cachedTransactions;
      }
      
      throw new Error(`Helius API error: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const transactions = await response.json();
    
    const whaleTransactions: WhaleTransaction[] = [];
    
    for (const tx of transactions) {
      if (!tx.nativeTransfers && !tx.tokenTransfers) continue;
      
      if (tx.nativeTransfers) {
        for (const transfer of tx.nativeTransfers) {
          const amountSol = transfer.amount / 1e9;
          
          if (amountSol >= WHALE_THRESHOLD_SOL) {
            whaleTransactions.push({
              signature: tx.signature,
              timestamp: tx.timestamp,
              type: "transfer",
              amount: amountSol,
              amountUsd: amountSol * 150,
              token: SOL_MINT,
              tokenSymbol: "SOL",
              from: transfer.fromUserAccount || "unknown",
              to: transfer.toUserAccount || "unknown",
              isWhale: true,
            });
          }
        }
      }
      
      if (tx.tokenTransfers) {
        for (const transfer of tx.tokenTransfers) {
          const decimals = transfer.tokenStandard === "Fungible" ? 6 : 0;
          const amount = transfer.tokenAmount / Math.pow(10, decimals);
          
          const isStablecoin = [USDC_MINT, USDT_MINT].includes(transfer.mint);
          
          if (isStablecoin && amount >= WHALE_THRESHOLD_USD) {
            whaleTransactions.push({
              signature: tx.signature,
              timestamp: tx.timestamp,
              type: determineTransactionType(tx),
              amount: amount,
              amountUsd: amount,
              token: transfer.mint,
              tokenSymbol: TOKEN_SYMBOLS[transfer.mint] || "TOKEN",
              from: transfer.fromUserAccount || "unknown",
              to: transfer.toUserAccount || "unknown",
              isWhale: true,
            });
          }
        }
      }
    }
    
    const uniqueWhales = whaleTransactions
      .filter((tx, index, self) => 
        index === self.findIndex((t) => t.signature === tx.signature)
      )
      .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
      .slice(0, 20);
    
    cachedTransactions = uniqueWhales;
    lastFetch = now;
    
    console.log(`[Helius] Found ${uniqueWhales.length} whale transactions`);
    return uniqueWhales;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[Helius] Fetch error:", error);
    return cachedTransactions;
  }
}

function determineTransactionType(tx: any): "transfer" | "swap" | "mint" | "burn" | "unknown" {
  const type = tx.type?.toLowerCase() || "";
  
  if (type.includes("swap") || type.includes("exchange")) return "swap";
  if (type.includes("mint")) return "mint";
  if (type.includes("burn")) return "burn";
  if (type.includes("transfer")) return "transfer";
  
  return "transfer";
}

export function getCachedWhaleTransactions(): WhaleTransaction[] {
  return cachedTransactions;
}

import type { LiquidationData, CryptoData } from "@shared/schema";

const BINANCE_FUTURES_URL = "https://fapi.binance.com/fapi/v1";

let cachedLiquidations: LiquidationData[] = [];
let lastFetch: number = 0;
const CACHE_DURATION = 30000;

const TRACKED_SYMBOLS = ["BTCUSDT", "ETHUSDT", "SOLUSDT", "XRPUSDT", "DOGEUSDT"];

export async function getLiquidations(): Promise<LiquidationData[]> {
  const now = Date.now();
  
  if (cachedLiquidations.length > 0 && now - lastFetch < CACHE_DURATION) {
    return cachedLiquidations;
  }

  try {
    console.log("[Liquidations] Fetching from Binance Futures...");
    
    const allLiquidations: LiquidationData[] = [];
    
    for (const symbol of TRACKED_SYMBOLS) {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(
          `${BINANCE_FUTURES_URL}/allForceOrders?symbol=${symbol}&limit=20`,
          {
            headers: {
              "Accept": "application/json",
            },
          }
        );

        if (!response.ok) {
          console.error(`[Liquidations] ${symbol} error:`, response.status);
          continue;
        }

        const orders = await response.json();
        
        for (const order of orders) {
          const price = parseFloat(order.price);
          const qty = parseFloat(order.origQty);
          const usdValue = price * qty;
          
          if (usdValue >= 10000) {
            allLiquidations.push({
              symbol: order.symbol,
              side: order.side.toLowerCase() as "buy" | "sell",
              price,
              quantity: qty,
              usdValue,
              time: order.time,
              type: order.side === "BUY" ? "short" : "long",
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[Liquidations] Error fetching ${symbol}:`, err);
      }
    }
    
    const sorted = allLiquidations
      .sort((a, b) => b.time - a.time)
      .slice(0, 50);
    
    cachedLiquidations = sorted;
    lastFetch = now;
    
    console.log(`[Liquidations] Found ${sorted.length} significant liquidations`);
    return sorted;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[Liquidations] Fetch error:", error);
    return cachedLiquidations;
  }
}

export function calculateLiquidationClusters(liquidations: LiquidationData[]): {
  symbol: string;
  longLiquidationLevel: number;
  shortLiquidationLevel: number;
  totalLongLiquidated: number;
  totalShortLiquidated: number;
  volatilityTrap: "high" | "medium" | "low";
}[] {
  const bySymbol = new Map<string, LiquidationData[]>();
  
  for (const liq of liquidations) {
    const existing = bySymbol.get(liq.symbol) || [];
    existing.push(liq);
    bySymbol.set(liq.symbol, existing);
  }
  
  const clusters = [];
  
  for (const [symbol, liqs] of Array.from(bySymbol.entries())) {
    const longs = liqs.filter((l: LiquidationData) => l.type === "long");
    const shorts = liqs.filter((l: LiquidationData) => l.type === "short");
    
    const avgLongPrice = longs.length > 0
      ? longs.reduce((sum: number, l: LiquidationData) => sum + l.price, 0) / longs.length
      : 0;
    const avgShortPrice = shorts.length > 0
      ? shorts.reduce((sum: number, l: LiquidationData) => sum + l.price, 0) / shorts.length
      : 0;
    
    const totalLongUsd = longs.reduce((sum: number, l: LiquidationData) => sum + l.usdValue, 0);
    const totalShortUsd = shorts.reduce((sum: number, l: LiquidationData) => sum + l.usdValue, 0);
    
    const totalLiquidated = totalLongUsd + totalShortUsd;
    let volatilityTrap: "high" | "medium" | "low" = "low";
    
    if (totalLiquidated > 1000000) volatilityTrap = "high";
    else if (totalLiquidated > 100000) volatilityTrap = "medium";
    
    clusters.push({
      symbol: symbol.replace("USDT", ""),
      longLiquidationLevel: avgLongPrice,
      shortLiquidationLevel: avgShortPrice,
      totalLongLiquidated: totalLongUsd,
      totalShortLiquidated: totalShortUsd,
      volatilityTrap,
    });
  }
  
  return clusters;
}

export function calculateVolatilityZones(cryptos: CryptoData[]): {
  symbol: string;
  currentPrice: number;
  support: number;
  resistance: number;
  volatility24h: number;
  riskLevel: "high" | "medium" | "low";
  priceRange: string;
}[] {
  const zones = [];
  
  const tracked = cryptos.filter(c => 
    ["btc", "eth", "sol", "xrp", "doge", "bnb"].includes(c.symbol.toLowerCase())
  );
  
  for (const crypto of tracked) {
    const sparkline = crypto.sparkline_in_7d?.price || [];
    if (sparkline.length < 24) continue;
    
    const last24h = sparkline.slice(-24);
    const high24h = Math.max(...last24h);
    const low24h = Math.min(...last24h);
    const current = crypto.current_price;
    
    const volatility = ((high24h - low24h) / current) * 100;
    
    const support = low24h * 0.98;
    const resistance = high24h * 1.02;
    
    let riskLevel: "high" | "medium" | "low" = "low";
    if (volatility > 8) riskLevel = "high";
    else if (volatility > 4) riskLevel = "medium";
    
    const distToSupport = ((current - support) / current) * 100;
    const distToResistance = ((resistance - current) / current) * 100;
    
    zones.push({
      symbol: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
      currentPrice: current,
      support,
      resistance,
      volatility24h: volatility,
      riskLevel,
      priceRange: `$${support.toFixed(2)} - $${resistance.toFixed(2)}`,
    });
  }
  
  return zones.sort((a, b) => b.volatility24h - a.volatility24h);
}

export function getCachedLiquidations(): LiquidationData[] {
  return cachedLiquidations;
}

END OF FILE: ./server/services/liquidations.ts

import { CryptoData } from "@shared/schema";

interface OnChainMetrics {
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
  marketCap: number;
  mvrvZScore: number;
  mvrvInterpretation: string;
  nupl: number;
  nuplInterpretation: string;
  nuplZone: "capitulation" | "hope" | "optimism" | "belief" | "euphoria";
  priceToAvg7d: number;
  timestamp: string;
}

interface AllOnChainMetrics {
  btc: OnChainMetrics | null;
  eth: OnChainMetrics | null;
  market: {
    totalMarketCap: number;
    avgMvrv: number;
    overallSentiment: string;
  };
  lastUpdated: string;
}

const CACHE_TTL = 60 * 1000;
let cache: { data: AllOnChainMetrics | null; timestamp: number } = {
  data: null,
  timestamp: 0,
};

function calculateMVRVZScore(
  currentMarketCap: number,
  sparklinePrices: number[],
  currentPrice: number
): { zScore: number; interpretation: string } {
  if (!sparklinePrices || sparklinePrices.length < 24) {
    return { zScore: 0, interpretation: "Insufficient data" };
  }

  const avgPrice = sparklinePrices.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / sparklinePrices.length;
  const circulatingSupply = currentMarketCap / currentPrice;
  const realizedCapProxy = avgPrice * circulatingSupply;

  const priceVariance = sparklinePrices.reduce((sum, p) => {
    return sum + Math.pow(p - avgPrice, 2);
  }, 0) / sparklinePrices.length;
  const priceStdDev = Math.sqrt(priceVariance);
  const marketCapStdDev = priceStdDev * circulatingSupply;

  if (marketCapStdDev === 0) {
    return { zScore: 0, interpretation: "Stable market" };
  }

  const zScore = (currentMarketCap - realizedCapProxy) / marketCapStdDev;
  
  let interpretation: string;
  if (zScore > 7) {
    interpretation = "Extreme overvaluation - major correction likely";
  } else if (zScore > 4) {
    interpretation = "Significantly overvalued - caution advised";
  } else if (zScore > 2) {
    interpretation = "Moderately overvalued - take profits";
  } else if (zScore > 0) {
    interpretation = "Fair value to slightly overvalued";
  } else if (zScore > -1) {
    interpretation = "Undervalued - accumulation zone";
  } else {
    interpretation = "Deeply undervalued - strong buy signal";
  }

  return { zScore: Math.round(zScore * 100) / 100, interpretation };
}

function calculateNUPL(
  currentMarketCap: number,
  sparklinePrices: number[],
  currentPrice: number
): { nupl: number; interpretation: string; zone: OnChainMetrics["nuplZone"] } {
  if (!sparklinePrices || sparklinePrices.length < 24) {
    return { nupl: 0, interpretation: "Insufficient data", zone: "hope" };
  }

  const avgPrice = sparklinePrices.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / sparklinePrices.length;
  const circulatingSupply = currentMarketCap / currentPrice;
  const realizedCapProxy = avgPrice * circulatingSupply;

  const nupl = (currentMarketCap - realizedCapProxy) / currentMarketCap;
  const nuplPercent = nupl * 100;

  let interpretation: string;
  let zone: OnChainMetrics["nuplZone"];

  if (nuplPercent > 75) {
    interpretation = "Euphoria - extreme greed, market top likely";
    zone = "euphoria";
  } else if (nuplPercent > 50) {
    interpretation = "Belief - strong profit taking, be cautious";
    zone = "belief";
  } else if (nuplPercent > 25) {
    interpretation = "Optimism - healthy bull market";
    zone = "optimism";
  } else if (nuplPercent > 0) {
    interpretation = "Hope - recovery phase, accumulate";
    zone = "hope";
  } else {
    interpretation = "Capitulation - maximum fear, best time to buy";
    zone = "capitulation";
  }

  return { 
    nupl: Math.round(nupl * 1000) / 1000, 
    interpretation, 
    zone 
  };
}

function calculatePriceToAvg(
  currentPrice: number,
  sparklinePrices: number[]
): number {
  if (!sparklinePrices || sparklinePrices.length === 0) return 0;
  const avgPrice = sparklinePrices.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / sparklinePrices.length;
  return Math.round(((currentPrice / avgPrice) - 1) * 10000) / 100;
}

function calculateMetricsForCrypto(crypto: CryptoData): OnChainMetrics | null {
  const sparkline = crypto.sparkline_in_7d?.price;
  if (!sparkline || sparkline.length < 24) {
    return null;
  }

  const { zScore, interpretation: mvrvInterpretation } = calculateMVRVZScore(
    crypto.market_cap,
    sparkline,
    crypto.current_price
  );

  const { nupl, interpretation: nuplInterpretation, zone } = calculateNUPL(
    crypto.market_cap,
    sparkline,
    crypto.current_price
  );

  const priceToAvg7d = calculatePriceToAvg(crypto.current_price, sparkline);

  return {
    symbol: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
    name: crypto.name,
    marketCap: crypto.market_cap,
    mvrvZScore: zScore,
    mvrvInterpretation,
    nupl,
    nuplInterpretation,
    nuplZone: zone,
    priceToAvg7d,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
}

export function calculateOnChainMetrics(cryptos: CryptoData[]): AllOnChainMetrics {
  const now = Date.now();

  if (cache.data && now - cache.timestamp < CACHE_TTL) {
    return cache.data;
  }

  const btc = cryptos.find(c => c.symbol.toLowerCase() === "btc");
  const eth = cryptos.find(c => c.symbol.toLowerCase() === "eth");

  const btcMetrics = btc ? calculateMetricsForCrypto(btc) : null;
  const ethMetrics = eth ? calculateMetricsForCrypto(eth) : null;

  const totalMarketCap = cryptos.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.market_cap, 0);

  const validMetrics = [btcMetrics, ethMetrics].filter(m => m !== null);
  const avgMvrv = validMetrics.length > 0
    ? validMetrics.reduce((sum, m) => sum + m!.mvrvZScore, 0) / validMetrics.length
    : 0;

  let overallSentiment: string;
  if (avgMvrv > 3) {
    overallSentiment = "Overheated - risk of correction";
  } else if (avgMvrv > 1) {
    overallSentiment = "Bullish - momentum positive";
  } else if (avgMvrv > -0.5) {
    overallSentiment = "Neutral - fair value range";
  } else {
    overallSentiment = "Undervalued - accumulation opportunity";
  }

  const metrics: AllOnChainMetrics = {
    btc: btcMetrics,
    eth: ethMetrics,
    market: {
      totalMarketCap,
      avgMvrv: Math.round(avgMvrv * 100) / 100,
      overallSentiment,
    },
    lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  cache = { data: metrics, timestamp: now };
  return metrics;
}

import { 
  Position, 
  TradeRecord, 
  PortfolioState, 
  TradingStats,
  AITradeSignal,
  BrokerConfig,
  PositionSide,
  OrderType 
} from "@shared/schema";
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from "uuid";
import { recordTradeResult, recordLearningSample } from "./aiTrader";
import { recordModePerformance, calculateScaleOutTakeProfits, type StrategyMode, type ModeAllocation } from "./quantStrategy";
import { getStrategyAllocation } from "./syntheticWarmup";
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

// Minimum interval between dynamic order updates (10 minutes in ms)
const ORDER_UPDATE_COOLDOWN_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;

// Per-symbol trade cooldown to prevent "machine gun" re-entry loops
// After a trade closes (stop-out or TP), wait 5 minutes before re-entering same symbol
const TRADE_COOLDOWN_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutes

// FILE-BASED LOCK SYSTEM - more reliable than in-memory Map
// Survives restarts and prevents race conditions across async operations
const LOCK_FILE = '/tmp/trade_cooldown_lock.json';

// In-memory cache for immediate machine-gun prevention (catches same-millisecond calls)
// File is used for persistence across restarts
const cooldownCache: Map<string, number> = new Map();

// In-memory mutex to serialize file access and prevent race conditions
// This ensures only one operation can read/modify/write the file at a time
let fileLockMutex = false;
let pendingLockOperations: (() => void)[] = [];

function acquireFileLock(): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!fileLockMutex) {
      fileLockMutex = true;
      resolve();
    } else {
      pendingLockOperations.push(resolve);
    }
  });
}

function releaseFileLock(): void {
  if (pendingLockOperations.length > 0) {
    const next = pendingLockOperations.shift()!;
    next();
  } else {
    fileLockMutex = false;
  }
}

// File-based cooldown functions for reliability with mutex protection
function loadCooldownLocks(): Record<string, number> {
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(LOCK_FILE)) {
      fs.writeFileSync(LOCK_FILE, JSON.stringify({}), { flag: 'wx' });
      return {};
    }
    const data = fs.readFileSync(LOCK_FILE, 'utf-8');
    const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null) {
      throw new Error('Invalid lock file format');
    }
    return parsed;
  } catch (error) {
    console.log(`[LOCK FILE] Error loading locks, resetting file: ${error}`);
    // Reset the file to prevent repeated corrupted states
    try {
      fs.writeFileSync(LOCK_FILE, JSON.stringify({}));
    } catch (writeError) {
      console.error(`[LOCK FILE] CRITICAL: Could not reset corrupted lock file: ${writeError}`);
    }
    return {};
  }
}

function saveCooldownLocks(locks: Record<string, number>): void {
  try {
    // Atomic write: write to temp file then rename
    const tempFile = LOCK_FILE + '.tmp';
    fs.writeFileSync(tempFile, JSON.stringify(locks));
    fs.renameSync(tempFile, LOCK_FILE);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(`[LOCK FILE] Error saving locks: ${error}`);
  }
}

function setSymbolCooldown(symbol: string): void {
  // Synchronous operation with in-memory cache for immediate machine-gun prevention
  // Also writes to file for persistence
  const now = Date.now();
  
  // Immediate in-memory cache to catch same-millisecond calls
  cooldownCache.set(symbol, now);
  
  // Also persist to file
  const locks = loadCooldownLocks();
  locks[symbol] = now;
  saveCooldownLocks(locks);
  console.log(`[LOCK FILE] ${symbol}: Cooldown written to file at ${new Date().toISOString()}`);
}

function clearSymbolCooldown(symbol: string): void {
  // Clear from in-memory cache
  cooldownCache.delete(symbol);
  
  // Also clear from file
  const locks = loadCooldownLocks();
  delete locks[symbol];
  saveCooldownLocks(locks);
  console.log(`[LOCK FILE] ${symbol}: Cooldown cleared from file`);
}

function getSymbolCooldownTime(symbol: string): number {
  // Check in-memory cache first (catches same-millisecond calls)
  const cacheTime = cooldownCache.get(symbol);
  if (cacheTime) {
    return cacheTime;
  }
  
  // Fall back to file (catches restarts)
  const locks = loadCooldownLocks();
  return locks[symbol] || 0;
}

// Minimum profit target threshold (0.8%) - trades with smaller targets don't cover fees
const MIN_PROFIT_TARGET_PERCENT = 0.8;

// Learning sample captures trade context for AI adaptation
export interface LearningSample {
  pnlPercent: number;
  isWin: boolean;
  side: PositionSide;
  symbol: string;
  durationMs: number;
  indicators: {
    rsiOversold?: boolean;
    rsiOverbought?: boolean;
    macdBullish?: boolean;
    macdBearish?: boolean;
    volatilitySqueeze?: boolean;
    meanProximity?: boolean;
    adxHookingUp?: boolean;
    trendUp?: boolean;
    trendDown?: boolean;
    mvrvUndervalued?: boolean;
    mvrvOvervalued?: boolean;
    capitalInflow?: boolean;
    capitalOutflow?: boolean;
  };
  entryGates: {
    zScoreGate: boolean;
    meanProximityGate: boolean;
    adxGate: boolean;
    perfectSetup: boolean;
  };
  signalMode?: StrategyMode;
}

// Rolling metrics for Sharpe/Ulcer calculation
interface RollingMetrics {
  returns: number[];  // pnl% per trade
  equityHistory: number[];  // equity snapshots
  timestamps: number[];  // for time-based calculations
  maxSampleSize: number;
}

const rollingMetrics: RollingMetrics = {
  returns: [],
  equityHistory: [],
  timestamps: [],
  maxSampleSize: 200,
};

// Calculate Sharpe Ratio from rolling returns
function calculateSharpeRatio(): number {
  if (rollingMetrics.returns.length < 5) return 0;
  
  const returns = rollingMetrics.returns;
  const mean = returns.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / returns.length;
  
  if (returns.length < 2) return mean > 0 ? 1 : -1;
  
  const variance = returns.reduce((sum, r) => sum + Math.pow(r - mean, 2), 0) / (returns.length - 1);
  const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
  
  // Annualize assuming ~30 trades per day
  const sharpe = stdDev > 0 ? (mean / stdDev) * Math.sqrt(252) : 0;
  return sharpe;
}

// Calculate Ulcer Index (measures downside volatility/pain)
function calculateUlcerIndex(): number {
  if (rollingMetrics.equityHistory.length < 3) return 0;
  
  const equity = rollingMetrics.equityHistory;
  let sumSquaredDrawdown = 0;
  let runningMax = equity[0];
  
  for (let i = 0; i < equity.length; i++) {
    runningMax = Math.max(runningMax, equity[i]);
    const drawdownPercent = ((runningMax - equity[i]) / runningMax) * 100;
    sumSquaredDrawdown += drawdownPercent * drawdownPercent;
  }
  
  return Math.sqrt(sumSquaredDrawdown / equity.length);
}

// PRIMARY METRIC: Mike1 = Sharpe / (Ulcer Index + 0.001)
// The +0.001 smoothing factor prevents divide-by-zero in perfect uptrends
// This is the "North Star" metric for strategy selection and regime switching
export function getSharpeUlcerRatio(): { sharpe: number; ulcer: number; primary: number } {
  const sharpe = calculateSharpeRatio();
  const ulcer = calculateUlcerIndex();
  
  // Mike1 metric: Sharpe / (Ulcer + 0.001) - proper smoothing per spec
  const SMOOTHING_FACTOR = 0.001;
  const primary = sharpe / (ulcer + SMOOTHING_FACTOR);
  
  return { sharpe, ulcer, primary };
}

// Update rolling metrics when a trade closes
export function updateRollingMetrics(pnlPercent: number, currentEquity: number): void {
  const now = Date.now();
  
  rollingMetrics.returns.push(pnlPercent);
  rollingMetrics.equityHistory.push(currentEquity);
  rollingMetrics.timestamps.push(now);
  
  // Keep only recent samples
  while (rollingMetrics.returns.length > rollingMetrics.maxSampleSize) {
    rollingMetrics.returns.shift();
    rollingMetrics.equityHistory.shift();
    rollingMetrics.timestamps.shift();
  }
}

// Extract indicator flags from trade reasoning for learning
function extractIndicatorFlags(trade: TradeRecord): LearningSample['indicators'] {
  const reasoning = trade.aiReasoning || '';
  return {
    rsiOversold: reasoning.includes('oversold'),
    rsiOverbought: reasoning.includes('overbought'),
    macdBullish: reasoning.includes('bullish crossover') || reasoning.includes('MACD bullish'),
    macdBearish: reasoning.includes('bearish crossover') || reasoning.includes('MACD bearish'),
    volatilitySqueeze: reasoning.includes('squeeze'),
    meanProximity: reasoning.includes('mean') || reasoning.includes('SMA100'),
    adxHookingUp: reasoning.includes('ADX hooking'),
    trendUp: reasoning.includes('uptrend'),
    trendDown: reasoning.includes('downtrend'),
    mvrvUndervalued: reasoning.includes('undervalued'),
    mvrvOvervalued: reasoning.includes('overvalued'),
    capitalInflow: reasoning.includes('inflow'),
    capitalOutflow: reasoning.includes('outflow'),
  };
}

// Extract entry gate information from trade reasoning
function extractEntryGates(trade: TradeRecord): LearningSample['entryGates'] {
  const reasoning = trade.aiReasoning || '';
  return {
    zScoreGate: true, // Always passed if trade opened
    meanProximityGate: reasoning.includes('mean') || reasoning.includes('SMA100'),
    adxGate: reasoning.includes('ADX hooking') || reasoning.includes('ADX trending'),
    perfectSetup: reasoning.includes('squeeze') && (reasoning.includes('mean') || reasoning.includes('SMA100')),
  };
}

// Extract signal mode from trade record (uses stored value first, then fallback to parsing)
function extractSignalMode(trade: TradeRecord): StrategyMode | undefined {
  // Prefer the explicitly stored signalMode if available
  if (trade.signalMode) {
    return trade.signalMode as StrategyMode;
  }
  
  // Fallback to parsing reasoning for backward compatibility
  const reasoning = trade.aiReasoning || '';
  if (reasoning.includes('Mean Reversion') || reasoning.includes('meanReversion')) {
    return 'meanReversion';
  } else if (reasoning.includes('Vol Expansion') || reasoning.includes('volExpansion')) {
    return 'volExpansion';
  }
  return 'adaptive'; // Default if not specified
}

const INITIAL_BALANCE = 500;
const MAX_POSITION_PERCENT = 0.10; // 10% per position to allow scale-out with minimum lots
const MAX_CRYPTO_EXPOSURE = 0.20;
const MAX_DRAWDOWN_PERCENT = 0.25;

const BROKER: BrokerConfig = {
  name: "Simulated Binance Futures",
  minLotSize: 0.001,
  maxLeverage: 10,
  makerFee: 0.0002,
  takerFee: 0.0004,
  avgSlippage: 0.0005,
  maxSlippage: 0.002,
};

// Minimum notional value for a trade (Binance Futures minimum is typically $5-10)
const MIN_NOTIONAL_VALUE = 10.0;

interface TradingState {
  balance: number;
  equity: number;
  positions: Position[];
  trades: TradeRecord[];
  peakEquity: number;
  maxDrawdown: number;
  startedAt: string;
}

let tradingState: TradingState = {
  balance: INITIAL_BALANCE,
  equity: INITIAL_BALANCE,
  positions: [],
  trades: [],
  peakEquity: INITIAL_BALANCE,
  maxDrawdown: 0,
  startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
};

let isTradingActive = true;

export function startTrading(): void {
  isTradingActive = true;
  console.log("[Paper Trading] Trading started");
}

export function stopTrading(): void {
  isTradingActive = false;
  console.log("[Paper Trading] Trading stopped");
}

export function isTradingEnabled(): boolean {
  return isTradingActive;
}

function calculateSlippage(price: number, side: PositionSide, orderType: OrderType): number {
  if (orderType === "limit") return 0;
  
  const slippagePercent = BROKER.avgSlippage + (Math.random() * (BROKER.maxSlippage - BROKER.avgSlippage));
  return side === "long" ? price * slippagePercent : -price * slippagePercent;
}

function calculateFees(notionalValue: number, orderType: OrderType): number {
  const feeRate = orderType === "limit" ? BROKER.makerFee : BROKER.takerFee;
  return notionalValue * feeRate;
}

function roundToLotSize(quantity: number): number {
  return Math.floor(quantity / BROKER.minLotSize) * BROKER.minLotSize;
}

export function getPortfolioState(): PortfolioState {
  const unrealizedPnl = tradingState.positions.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.unrealizedPnl, 0);
  const usedMargin = tradingState.positions.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.marginUsed, 0);
  const realizedPnl = tradingState.trades
    .filter(t => t.status === "closed" || t.status === "stopped_out")
    .reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.pnl || 0), 0);
  
  const equity = tradingState.balance + unrealizedPnl;
  const currentDrawdown = tradingState.peakEquity > 0 
    ? ((tradingState.peakEquity - equity) / tradingState.peakEquity) * 100
    : 0;

  return {
    balance: tradingState.balance,
    equity,
    availableMargin: tradingState.balance - usedMargin,
    usedMargin,
    unrealizedPnl,
    realizedPnl,
    totalPnl: realizedPnl + unrealizedPnl,
    totalPnlPercent: ((realizedPnl + unrealizedPnl) / INITIAL_BALANCE) * 100,
    positions: tradingState.positions,
    maxDrawdown: tradingState.maxDrawdown,
    currentDrawdown: Math.max(0, currentDrawdown),
    peakEquity: tradingState.peakEquity,
    lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
}

export function getTrades(): TradeRecord[] {
  return [...tradingState.trades].reverse();
}

export function getTradingStats(): TradingStats {
  const closedTrades = tradingState.trades.filter(t => t.status !== "open");
  const winningTrades = closedTrades.filter(t => (t.pnl || 0) > 0);
  const losingTrades = closedTrades.filter(t => (t.pnl || 0) < 0);
  
  const totalWins = winningTrades.reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.pnl || 0), 0);
  const totalLosses = Math.abs(losingTrades.reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.pnl || 0), 0));
  
  const avgWin = winningTrades.length > 0 ? totalWins / winningTrades.length : 0;
  const avgLoss = losingTrades.length > 0 ? totalLosses / losingTrades.length : 0;
  
  const cryptoExposure = new Map<string, number>();
  tradingState.positions.forEach(p => {
    const current = cryptoExposure.get(p.symbol) || 0;
    cryptoExposure.set(p.symbol, current + p.marginUsed);
  });

  const exposureArray = Array.from(cryptoExposure.entries()).map(([symbol, exposure]) => ({
    symbol,
    exposure,
    exposurePercent: (exposure / tradingState.equity) * 100,
  }));

  const monthlyReturns: { month: string; return: number; trades: number; }[] = [];
  const tradesByMonth = new Map<string, TradeRecord[]>();
  
  closedTrades.forEach(trade => {
    if (trade.closedAt) {
      const month = trade.closedAt.substring(0, 7);
      const existing = tradesByMonth.get(month) || [];
      existing.push(trade);
      tradesByMonth.set(month, existing);
    }
  });

  tradesByMonth.forEach((trades, month) => {
    const monthReturn = trades.reduce((sum, t) => sum + (t.pnl || 0), 0);
    monthlyReturns.push({
      month,
      return: (monthReturn / INITIAL_BALANCE) * 100,
      trades: trades.length,
    });
  });

  return {
    totalTrades: closedTrades.length,
    winningTrades: winningTrades.length,
    losingTrades: losingTrades.length,
    winRate: closedTrades.length > 0 ? (winningTrades.length / closedTrades.length) * 100 : 0,
    avgWin,
    avgLoss,
    profitFactor: totalLosses > 0 ? totalWins / totalLosses : totalWins > 0 ? Infinity : 0,
    sharpeRatio: null,
    maxDrawdown: tradingState.maxDrawdown,
    avgDrawdown: tradingState.maxDrawdown / 2,
    bestTrade: closedTrades.length > 0 ? Math.max(...closedTrades.map(t => t.pnl || 0)) : 0,
    worstTrade: closedTrades.length > 0 ? Math.min(...closedTrades.map(t => t.pnl || 0)) : 0,
    avgHoldingTime: "N/A",
    totalFees: tradingState.trades.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.fees, 0),
    totalSlippage: tradingState.trades.reduce((sum, t) => sum + t.slippage, 0),
    monthlyReturns,
    cryptoExposure: exposureArray,
  };
}

export function updatePositionPrices(prices: Map<string, number>): void {
  let positionsToClose: string[] = [];
  
  tradingState.positions.forEach(position => {
    const currentPrice = prices.get(position.symbol.toLowerCase());
    if (!currentPrice) return;
    
    position.currentPrice = currentPrice;
    
    if (position.side === "long") {
      position.unrealizedPnl = (currentPrice - position.entryPrice) * position.quantity;
    } else {
      position.unrealizedPnl = (position.entryPrice - currentPrice) * position.quantity;
    }
    
    // Use entry-price base for consistency with realized pnlPercent calculations
    const currentPositionMargin = position.quantity * position.entryPrice;
    position.unrealizedPnlPercent = currentPositionMargin > 0 
      ? (position.unrealizedPnl / currentPositionMargin) * 100 
      : 0;
    
    const stopHit = position.side === "long" 
      ? currentPrice <= position.stopLoss
      : currentPrice >= position.stopLoss;
    
    // Check scale-out take profit levels first
    if (position.takeProfitLevels && position.takeProfitLevels.length > 0 && !stopHit) {
      checkScaleOutTakeProfits(position, currentPrice);
    }
    
    // Check if position should be fully closed (stop hit or all TPs triggered)
    const allTPsTriggered = position.takeProfitLevels?.every(tp => tp.triggered) ?? false;
    const legacyTPHit = !position.takeProfitLevels && position.takeProfit && (
      position.side === "long" 
        ? currentPrice >= position.takeProfit
        : currentPrice <= position.takeProfit
    );
    
    if (stopHit || legacyTPHit || (allTPsTriggered && position.quantity <= BROKER.minLotSize)) {
      positionsToClose.push(position.id);
    }
  });

  positionsToClose.forEach(id => {
    const position = tradingState.positions.find(p => p.id === id);
    if (position) {
      // Determine if stopped out (depends on position side)
      const stoppedOut = position.side === "long"
        ? position.currentPrice <= position.stopLoss
        : position.currentPrice >= position.stopLoss;
      closePosition(id, position.currentPrice, stoppedOut ? "stopped_out" : "closed");
    }
  });

  const unrealizedPnl = tradingState.positions.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.unrealizedPnl, 0);
  tradingState.equity = tradingState.balance + unrealizedPnl;
  
  if (tradingState.equity > tradingState.peakEquity) {
    tradingState.peakEquity = tradingState.equity;
  }
  
  const currentDrawdown = ((tradingState.peakEquity - tradingState.equity) / tradingState.peakEquity) * 100;
  if (currentDrawdown > tradingState.maxDrawdown) {
    tradingState.maxDrawdown = currentDrawdown;
  }
}

// Check and execute scale-out take profit levels
function checkScaleOutTakeProfits(position: Position, currentPrice: number): void {
  if (!position.takeProfitLevels) return;
  
  for (let i = 0; i < position.takeProfitLevels.length; i++) {
    const tpLevel = position.takeProfitLevels[i];
    if (tpLevel.triggered) continue; // Already hit this level
    
    const tpHit = position.side === "long"
      ? currentPrice >= tpLevel.price
      : currentPrice <= tpLevel.price;
    
    if (tpHit) {
      // Calculate quantity to close at this level
      const quantityToClose = roundToLotSize((tpLevel.quantityPercent / 100) * position.initialQuantity);
      
      if (quantityToClose >= BROKER.minLotSize && position.quantity > BROKER.minLotSize) {
        // Execute partial close
        const actualClose = Math.min(quantityToClose, position.quantity - BROKER.minLotSize);
        if (actualClose >= BROKER.minLotSize) {
          executePartialClose(position, actualClose, currentPrice, i + 1);
        }
      }
      
      // Mark this level as triggered
      tpLevel.triggered = true;
      
      console.log(`[Scale-Out] ${position.symbol} TP${i+1} HIT @ $${currentPrice.toFixed(2)} ` +
        `(target: $${tpLevel.price.toFixed(2)}), closed ${tpLevel.quantityPercent}% ` +
        `(${(tpLevel.hitProbability*100).toFixed(0)}% probability)`);
    }
  }
}

// Execute a partial position close (scale-out)
// Take-profit exits use LIMIT orders (maker fee) to reduce costs
function executePartialClose(position: Position, quantityToClose: number, exitPrice: number, tpLevel: number): void {
  // Take-profit uses limit orders - no slippage on planned exits
  const slippage = 0; // Limit order = no slippage
  const executedPrice = exitPrice;
  
  const notionalValue = quantityToClose * executedPrice;
  // Use LIMIT order type for take-profit (maker fee 0.02% vs taker 0.04%)
  const fees = calculateFees(notionalValue, "limit");
  
  let pnl: number;
  if (position.side === "long") {
    pnl = (executedPrice - position.entryPrice) * quantityToClose;
  } else {
    pnl = (position.entryPrice - executedPrice) * quantityToClose;
  }
  pnl -= fees;
  
  // Add P&L to balance
  tradingState.balance += pnl;
  
  // Reduce position quantity and margin
  const portionClosed = quantityToClose / position.quantity;
  position.quantity -= quantityToClose;
  position.quantity = roundToLotSize(position.quantity);
  position.marginUsed *= (1 - portionClosed);
  
  // CRITICAL FIX: Update trade record with cumulative PnL from partial closes
  const tradeIndex = tradingState.trades.findIndex(t => t.id === position.id);
  let cumulativePnlPercent = 0;
  
  if (tradeIndex !== -1) {
    const trade = tradingState.trades[tradeIndex];
    const existingPnl = trade.pnl || 0;
    const existingFees = trade.fees || 0;
    const existingSlippage = trade.slippage || 0;
    const newCumulativePnl = existingPnl + pnl;
    
    // Use original margin (initial qty * entry) for consistent pnlPercent calculation, with zero guard
    const originalMargin = trade.quantity * trade.entryPrice;
    cumulativePnlPercent = originalMargin > 0 ? (newCumulativePnl / originalMargin) * 100 : 0;
    
    // Accumulate PnL, fees, and slippage from partial closes
    tradingState.trades[tradeIndex] = {
      ...trade,
      pnl: newCumulativePnl,
      pnlPercent: cumulativePnlPercent, // Update running pnlPercent
      fees: existingFees + fees,
      slippage: existingSlippage + Math.abs(slippage),
    };
  }
  
  // Calculate this chunk's pnlPercent for logging (using entry price as base, with zero guard)
  const chunkMargin = quantityToClose * position.entryPrice;
  const chunkPnlPercent = chunkMargin > 0 ? (pnl / chunkMargin) * 100 : 0;
  
  console.log(`[Partial Close] ${position.symbol} TP${tpLevel}: closed ${quantityToClose.toFixed(4)} units ` +
    `@ $${executedPrice.toFixed(2)}, Chunk P&L: $${pnl.toFixed(4)} (${chunkPnlPercent.toFixed(2)}%), ` +
    `Cumulative: ${cumulativePnlPercent.toFixed(2)}%, remaining: ${position.quantity.toFixed(4)} units`);
  
  // Record partial trade result using chunk pnlPercent (each partial close is its own datapoint)
  recordTradeResult(chunkPnlPercent);
  
  // Update rolling metrics with chunk pnlPercent
  rollingMetrics.returns.push(chunkPnlPercent);
  if (rollingMetrics.returns.length > rollingMetrics.maxSampleSize) {
    rollingMetrics.returns.shift();
  }
}

// Dynamic order update - recalculate ONLY take profits (stop loss is FIXED at entry)
// Maximum update frequency: every 10 minutes
export function updateDynamicOrders(
  priceHistory: Map<string, number[]>, // symbol -> recent prices array
  atrMap: Map<string, number>,         // symbol -> current ATR
  volatilityMap: Map<string, number>   // symbol -> current volatility
): void {
  const now = Date.now();
  
  tradingState.positions.forEach(position => {
    // Check cooldown - only update if 10 minutes have passed
    const lastUpdate = position.lastOrderUpdate 
      ? new Date(position.lastOrderUpdate).getTime() 
      : 0;
    
    if (now - lastUpdate < ORDER_UPDATE_COOLDOWN_MS) {
      return; // Skip - too soon since last update
    }
    
    const atr = atrMap.get(position.symbol.toLowerCase());
    const volatility = volatilityMap.get(position.symbol.toLowerCase());
    
    if (!atr) {
      return; // Not enough data
    }
    
    const currentPrice = position.currentPrice;
    
    // STOP LOSS IS FIXED - No trailing, no updates after entry
    // Only take profits are dynamically updated
    
    // Calculate new scale-out take profit levels based on current market conditions
    const vol = volatility || 0.03;
    let volMultiplier = 1.0;
    if (vol < 0.02) {
      volMultiplier = 1.15; // Low vol = wider targets
    } else if (vol > 0.05) {
      volMultiplier = 0.85; // High vol = tighter targets
    }
    
    // Calculate new TP levels using current ATR (scale-out system)
    const tp1Distance = Math.max(atr * 0.35 * volMultiplier, currentPrice * 0.005);
    const tp2Distance = Math.max(atr * 0.55 * volMultiplier, currentPrice * 0.005);
    const tp3Distance = Math.max(atr * 0.80 * volMultiplier, currentPrice * 0.005);
    
    let newTP1: number, newTP2: number, newTP3: number;
    if (position.side === "long") {
      newTP1 = currentPrice + tp1Distance;
      newTP2 = currentPrice + tp2Distance;
      newTP3 = currentPrice + tp3Distance;
    } else {
      newTP1 = currentPrice - tp1Distance;
      newTP2 = currentPrice - tp2Distance;
      newTP3 = currentPrice - tp3Distance;
    }
    
    // Update the scale-out TP levels if they haven't been triggered yet
    if (position.takeProfitLevels && position.takeProfitLevels.length > 0) {
      const updatedLevels = position.takeProfitLevels.map((level, index) => {
        if (level.triggered) return level; // Don't update levels already triggered
        
        const newPrice = index === 0 ? newTP1 : index === 1 ? newTP2 : newTP3;
        return { ...level, price: newPrice };
      });
      
      position.takeProfitLevels = updatedLevels;
      
      // Primary TP tracks TP3 (final target, index 2)
      const tp3Level = updatedLevels[2];
      if (tp3Level && !tp3Level.triggered) {
        position.takeProfit = tp3Level.price;
      }
      
      position.lastOrderUpdate = new Date().toISOString();
      
      console.log(`[Dynamic TP Update] ${position.symbol}: TP1=${newTP1.toFixed(2)}, TP2=${newTP2.toFixed(2)}, TP3=${newTP3.toFixed(2)} (SL fixed at ${position.stopLoss.toFixed(2)})`);
    }
  });
}

// Check if symbol is on cooldown (prevents machine-gun re-entry)
// Uses FILE-BASED LOCK for reliability across async operations and restarts
function isSymbolOnCooldown(symbol: string): { onCooldown: boolean; remainingMs: number } {
  const lastTradeTime = getSymbolCooldownTime(symbol);
  if (!lastTradeTime) {
    return { onCooldown: false, remainingMs: 0 };
  }
  
  const now = Date.now();
  const elapsed = now - lastTradeTime;
  const remaining = TRADE_COOLDOWN_MS - elapsed;
  
  if (remaining > 0) {
    return { onCooldown: true, remainingMs: remaining };
  }
  
  // Cooldown expired, clean up
  clearSymbolCooldown(symbol);
  return { onCooldown: false, remainingMs: 0 };
}

// Note: setSymbolCooldown and clearSymbolCooldown are now file-based functions defined at top of file

export function canOpenPosition(signal: AITradeSignal): { allowed: boolean; reason?: string } {
  const portfolio = getPortfolioState();
  
  // NOTE: Cooldown check moved to openPosition() to be set BEFORE this function is called
  // This prevents the machine-gun bug where multiple signals fire in same millisecond
  
  // Check minimum profit target (must be >= 0.8% to cover fees)
  // CRITICAL: This gate MUST run to prevent taking crumb trades that lose money to fees
  if (signal.takeProfit && signal.targetEntry) {
    const targetDistance = Math.abs(signal.takeProfit - signal.targetEntry) / signal.targetEntry * 100;
    if (targetDistance < MIN_PROFIT_TARGET_PERCENT) {
      console.log(`[0.8% GATE] REJECTED ${signal.symbol}: Target ${targetDistance.toFixed(3)}% < ${MIN_PROFIT_TARGET_PERCENT}% min`);
      return { allowed: false, reason: `Target ${targetDistance.toFixed(2)}% < ${MIN_PROFIT_TARGET_PERCENT}% min (fees would eat profit)` };
    }
    console.log(`[0.8% GATE] PASSED ${signal.symbol}: Target ${targetDistance.toFixed(3)}% >= ${MIN_PROFIT_TARGET_PERCENT}%`);
  } else {
    // If no TP or entry, log warning and reject - we need TP to calculate target distance
    console.log(`[0.8% GATE] REJECTED ${signal.symbol}: Missing takeProfit or targetEntry - cannot validate profitability`);
    return { allowed: false, reason: `Missing takeProfit or targetEntry - cannot validate profitability gate` };
  }
  
  if (portfolio.currentDrawdown >= MAX_DRAWDOWN_PERCENT * 100) {
    return { allowed: false, reason: "Maximum drawdown reached (25%)" };
  }
  
  const positionValue = signal.positionSize;
  if (positionValue > portfolio.equity * MAX_POSITION_PERCENT) {
    return { allowed: false, reason: `Position exceeds 5% max (${((positionValue / portfolio.equity) * 100).toFixed(1)}%)` };
  }
  
  const currentCryptoExposure = tradingState.positions
    .filter(p => p.symbol === signal.symbol)
    .reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.marginUsed, 0);
  
  if (currentCryptoExposure + positionValue > portfolio.equity * MAX_CRYPTO_EXPOSURE) {
    return { allowed: false, reason: `Would exceed 20% max exposure for ${signal.symbol}` };
  }
  
  if (positionValue > portfolio.availableMargin) {
    return { allowed: false, reason: "Insufficient margin available" };
  }
  
  return { allowed: true };
}

export function openPosition(signal: AITradeSignal, bootstrapOverride: boolean = false): Position | null {
  // CRITICAL: Set cooldown IMMEDIATELY before any processing to prevent machine-gun bug
  // This MUST happen before canOpenPosition check because multiple signals can arrive
  // in the same millisecond and all pass the check before any one sets the cooldown
  const existingCooldown = isSymbolOnCooldown(signal.symbol);
  if (existingCooldown.onCooldown) {
    const remainingSec = Math.ceil(existingCooldown.remainingMs / 1000);
    console.log(`[COOLDOWN BLOCK] ${signal.symbol}: Rejected - ${remainingSec}s remaining (machine-gun prevention)`);
    return null;
  }
  
  // Set cooldown IMMEDIATELY - before any async operations or checks
  // This prevents race conditions where multiple trades fire in same millisecond
  // Uses FILE-BASED LOCK for reliability across async operations
  setSymbolCooldown(signal.symbol);
  console.log(`[COOLDOWN SET] ${signal.symbol}: Cooldown activated BEFORE trade execution`);
  
  const check = canOpenPosition(signal);
  if (!check.allowed) {
    console.log(`Cannot open position: ${check.reason}`);
    // Clear cooldown on validation failure to allow retry
    // (machine-gun prevention still works because same-millisecond calls are blocked)
    clearSymbolCooldown(signal.symbol);
    console.log(`[COOLDOWN CLEAR] ${signal.symbol}: Cleared due to validation failure`);
    return null;
  }
  
  const slippage = calculateSlippage(signal.targetEntry, signal.side!, "market");
  const executedPrice = signal.targetEntry + slippage;
  
  // Apply DYNAMIC 70/30 Allocation - Best Mike1 scorer gets 70%, second-best gets 30%
  // Uses warmup scores initially, switches to live scores after 5+ trades per mode
  let allocationWeight = 1.0; // Default to full allocation
  
  if (signal.signalMode) {
    const stratAlloc = getStrategyAllocation();
    const mode = signal.signalMode as string;
    
    if (mode === stratAlloc.primary) {
      allocationWeight = stratAlloc.primaryWeight;
    } else if (mode === stratAlloc.secondary) {
      allocationWeight = stratAlloc.secondaryWeight;
    } else if (mode === 'momentum' || mode === 'adaptive') {
      // Momentum fallback and adaptive signals get PRIMARY allocation
      // These are safety-net signals that fire when no primary strategy signals
      allocationWeight = stratAlloc.primaryWeight;
      console.log(`[Conviction Alloc] ${mode} signal -> using primary allocation (${(stratAlloc.primaryWeight*100).toFixed(0)}%)`);
    } else {
      allocationWeight = 0; // Not selected
    }
    
    // Skip trade if allocation is 0 (mode has negative Mike1)
    if (allocationWeight <= 0) {
      console.log(`[Conviction Alloc] Skipping ${signal.signalMode} trade - zero allocation`);
      clearSymbolCooldown(signal.symbol);
      console.log(`[COOLDOWN CLEAR] ${signal.symbol}: Cleared due to zero allocation`);
      return null;
    }
  }
  
  // Scale position size by allocation weight
  const scaledPositionSize = signal.positionSize * allocationWeight;
  const stratAlloc = signal.signalMode ? getStrategyAllocation() : null;
  console.log(`[Conviction Alloc] DYNAMIC 70/30: ${stratAlloc?.primary}=70%, ${stratAlloc?.secondary}=30%`);
  console.log(`[Conviction Alloc] ${signal.signalMode}: ${(allocationWeight*100).toFixed(0)}% allocation, size: $${signal.positionSize.toFixed(2)} -> $${scaledPositionSize.toFixed(2)}`);
  
  // Calculate quantity from scaled position size in dollars
  let quantity = scaledPositionSize / executedPrice;
  quantity = roundToLotSize(quantity);
  
  // For scale-out (profit ladder) to work, we need at least 3x minimum lot size
  // This allows 70% close (2 units) + 30% runner (1 unit)
  const MIN_SCALEOUT_LOTS = 3;
  const minScaleoutQuantity = BROKER.minLotSize * MIN_SCALEOUT_LOTS;
  
  // Ensure minimum quantity for scale-out
  if (quantity < minScaleoutQuantity) {
    quantity = minScaleoutQuantity;
    console.log(`[Position Size] Bumped to ${MIN_SCALEOUT_LOTS}x min lot (${minScaleoutQuantity}) for scale-out capability`);
  }
  
  // Calculate actual notional value after lot rounding
  const notionalValue = quantity * executedPrice;
  
  // Check minimum notional value (broker requirement)
  if (notionalValue < MIN_NOTIONAL_VALUE) {
    console.log(`[MIN NOTIONAL] REJECTED ${signal.symbol}: $${notionalValue.toFixed(2)} < $${MIN_NOTIONAL_VALUE} broker minimum`);
    clearSymbolCooldown(signal.symbol);
    console.log(`[COOLDOWN CLEAR] ${signal.symbol}: Cleared due to min notional rejection`);
    return null;
  }
  console.log(`[MIN NOTIONAL] PASSED ${signal.symbol}: $${notionalValue.toFixed(2)} >= $${MIN_NOTIONAL_VALUE}`);
  
  // Get current portfolio state for validation
  const portfolio = getPortfolioState();
  
  // Strict validation: reject if actual position exceeds max position size
  if (notionalValue > portfolio.equity * MAX_POSITION_PERCENT) {
    console.log(`Position value $${notionalValue.toFixed(2)} exceeds max ${(MAX_POSITION_PERCENT * 100).toFixed(0)}% of equity`);
    clearSymbolCooldown(signal.symbol);
    console.log(`[COOLDOWN CLEAR] ${signal.symbol}: Cleared due to max position size`);
    return null;
  }
  
  // Reject if not enough available margin
  if (notionalValue > portfolio.availableMargin) {
    console.log(`Position value $${notionalValue.toFixed(2)} exceeds available margin $${portfolio.availableMargin.toFixed(2)}`);
    clearSymbolCooldown(signal.symbol);
    console.log(`[COOLDOWN CLEAR] ${signal.symbol}: Cleared due to insufficient margin`);
    return null;
  }
  
  // Check max crypto exposure (20%) - must use actual notional after rounding
  const currentCryptoExposure = tradingState.positions
    .filter(p => p.symbol === signal.symbol)
    .reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.marginUsed, 0);
  
  if (currentCryptoExposure + notionalValue > portfolio.equity * MAX_CRYPTO_EXPOSURE) {
    console.log(`Would exceed 20% max exposure for ${signal.symbol} (current: $${currentCryptoExposure.toFixed(2)}, adding: $${notionalValue.toFixed(2)})`);
    clearSymbolCooldown(signal.symbol);
    console.log(`[COOLDOWN CLEAR] ${signal.symbol}: Cleared due to max crypto exposure`);
    return null;
  }
  
  const fees = calculateFees(notionalValue, "market");
  // Use actual notional value as marginUsed for consistent P&L calculations
  const marginUsed = notionalValue;
  
  // Only deduct entry fees from balance (margin is tracked separately via usedMargin)
  tradingState.balance -= fees;
  
  // Calculate scale-out take profit levels if we have ATR info
  // For now, use signal.takeProfit as TP1 reference and calculate TP2, TP3
  let takeProfitLevels: Position['takeProfitLevels'] = undefined;
  if (signal.takeProfit && signal.stopLoss) {
    // Estimate ATR from stop distance
    const stopDistance = Math.abs(executedPrice - signal.stopLoss);
    const estimatedATR = stopDistance / 1.5; // Stop is typically 1.5x ATR
    const tpDistance = Math.abs(signal.takeProfit - executedPrice);
    const estimatedVolatility = tpDistance / executedPrice;
    
    // Use the imported function for scale-out take profit calculation
    takeProfitLevels = calculateScaleOutTakeProfits(
      executedPrice,
      estimatedATR,
      estimatedVolatility,
      signal.side!
    );
  }
  
  const position: Position = {
    id: uuidv4(),
    symbol: signal.symbol,
    coinName: signal.coinName,
    side: signal.side!,
    quantity,
    initialQuantity: quantity, // Track original size for scale-out calculations
    entryPrice: executedPrice,
    currentPrice: executedPrice,
    stopLoss: signal.stopLoss,
    takeProfit: signal.takeProfit,
    takeProfitLevels, // Scale-out TP levels
    unrealizedPnl: 0,  // Start at 0, fees already deducted from balance
    unrealizedPnlPercent: 0,
    marginUsed,
    leverage: 1,
    openedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    aiReasoning: signal.reasoning,
  };
  
  const trade: TradeRecord = {
    id: position.id,
    symbol: signal.symbol,
    coinName: signal.coinName,
    side: signal.side!,
    orderType: "market",
    quantity,
    entryPrice: executedPrice,
    exitPrice: null,
    stopLoss: signal.stopLoss,
    takeProfit: signal.takeProfit,
    pnl: null,
    pnlPercent: null,
    fees,             // Starts with entry fees, accumulates exit fees
    entryFees: fees,  // Store entry fees separately for net PnL calculation
    slippage: Math.abs(slippage),
    status: "open",
    openedAt: position.openedAt,
    closedAt: null,
    aiReasoning: signal.reasoning,
    signalMode: signal.signalMode,  // Store signal mode for performance tracking
    signals: signal.signals,
  };
  
  tradingState.positions.push(position);
  tradingState.trades.push(trade);
  
  // Detailed logging for debugging position sizing and stops
  const stopPercent = Math.abs(signal.stopLoss - executedPrice) / executedPrice * 100;
  console.log(`[TRADE] Opened ${signal.side?.toUpperCase()} ${signal.symbol}:`);
  console.log(`  Entry: $${executedPrice.toFixed(2)} | Qty: ${quantity.toFixed(6)} | Value: $${notionalValue.toFixed(2)}`);
  console.log(`  Stop: $${signal.stopLoss.toFixed(2)} (${stopPercent.toFixed(2)}% away) | TP: $${signal.takeProfit?.toFixed(2) || 'N/A'}`);
  console.log(`  Signal posSize: $${signal.positionSize.toFixed(2)} | Margin: $${marginUsed.toFixed(2)}`);
  
  return position;
}

export function closePosition(positionId: string, exitPrice: number, status: "closed" | "stopped_out" = "closed"): TradeRecord | null {
  const positionIndex = tradingState.positions.findIndex(p => p.id === positionId);
  if (positionIndex === -1) return null;
  
  const position = tradingState.positions[positionIndex];
  
  // Stop-outs use market orders (slippage + taker fees) for guaranteed exit
  // Take-profits use limit orders (no slippage + maker fees) for cost reduction
  const isStopOut = status === "stopped_out";
  const orderType: OrderType = isStopOut ? "market" : "limit";
  const slippage = isStopOut 
    ? calculateSlippage(exitPrice, position.side === "long" ? "short" : "long", "market")
    : 0; // Limit orders have no slippage
  const executedPrice = exitPrice + slippage;
  
  const notionalValue = position.quantity * executedPrice;
  const fees = calculateFees(notionalValue, orderType);
  
  let pnl: number;
  if (position.side === "long") {
    pnl = (executedPrice - position.entryPrice) * position.quantity;
  } else {
    pnl = (position.entryPrice - executedPrice) * position.quantity;
  }
  pnl -= fees;
  
  // Use entry price base for consistent pnlPercent calculation (matches partial close logic)
  const remainingMargin = position.quantity * position.entryPrice;
  const pnlPercent = remainingMargin > 0 ? (pnl / remainingMargin) * 100 : 0;
  
  // Only add P&L to balance (margin was never subtracted, it's tracked via usedMargin)
  tradingState.balance += pnl;
  
  tradingState.positions.splice(positionIndex, 1);
  
  const tradeIndex = tradingState.trades.findIndex(t => t.id === positionId);
  let totalPnl = pnl;
  let totalPnlPercent = pnlPercent;
  
  if (tradeIndex !== -1) {
    const trade = tradingState.trades[tradeIndex];
    // CRITICAL FIX: Add to accumulated PnL from partial closes (not overwrite)
    const accumulatedPnl = trade.pnl || 0;
    const grossPnl = accumulatedPnl + pnl;  // PnL after exit fees only
    
    // CRITICAL: Include entry fees in net PnL for true performance measurement
    const entryFees = trade.entryFees || 0;
    totalPnl = grossPnl - entryFees;  // True net PnL after ALL fees (entry + exit)
    
    // Calculate total PnL percent based on original margin (initial quantity * entry price), with zero guard
    const originalMargin = trade.quantity * trade.entryPrice;
    totalPnlPercent = originalMargin > 0 ? (totalPnl / originalMargin) * 100 : 0;
    
    tradingState.trades[tradeIndex] = {
      ...trade,
      exitPrice: executedPrice,
      pnl: totalPnl,
      pnlPercent: totalPnlPercent,
      fees: trade.fees + fees,
      slippage: trade.slippage + Math.abs(slippage),
      status,
      closedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
  }
  
  console.log(`Closed position: ${position.symbol} @ $${executedPrice.toFixed(2)}, Final PnL: $${totalPnl.toFixed(4)} (${totalPnlPercent.toFixed(2)}%)`);
  
  // Calculate chunk pnlPercent for this final close (using entry price as base)
  const chunkMargin = position.quantity * position.entryPrice;
  const chunkPnlPercent = chunkMargin > 0 ? (pnl / chunkMargin) * 100 : 0;
  
  // Record trade result for strategy performance (use chunk pnlPercent - each close is a datapoint)
  recordTradeResult(chunkPnlPercent);
  
  // Update rolling metrics for Sharpe/Ulcer calculation (use chunk pnlPercent)
  const currentEquity = tradingState.balance + tradingState.positions.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.unrealizedPnl, 0);
  updateRollingMetrics(chunkPnlPercent, currentEquity);
  
  // Record learning sample for AI adaptation
  const closedTrade = tradingState.trades[tradeIndex];
  if (closedTrade) {
    const durationMs = closedTrade.closedAt && closedTrade.openedAt 
      ? new Date(closedTrade.closedAt).getTime() - new Date(closedTrade.openedAt).getTime()
      : 0;
    
    const signalMode = extractSignalMode(closedTrade);
    
    // Use TOTAL values for learning sample (whole trade performance)
    recordLearningSample({
      pnlPercent: totalPnlPercent, // Use total trade pnlPercent
      isWin: totalPnl > 0,         // Use total trade pnl for win/loss
      side: position.side,
      symbol: position.symbol,
      durationMs,
      indicators: extractIndicatorFlags(closedTrade),
      entryGates: extractEntryGates(closedTrade),
      signalMode,
    });
    
    // Track mode performance for adaptive switching (use chunk pnlPercent for rolling metrics)
    if (signalMode) {
      recordModePerformance(signalMode, chunkPnlPercent, currentEquity);
    }
  }
  
  // Set cooldown for this symbol to prevent machine-gun re-entry
  setSymbolCooldown(position.symbol);
  
  return closedTrade || null;
}

export function closeAllPositions(prices: Map<string, number>): void {
  const positionIds = tradingState.positions.map(p => p.id);
  
  positionIds.forEach(id => {
    const position = tradingState.positions.find(p => p.id === id);
    if (position) {
      const price = prices.get(position.symbol.toLowerCase()) || position.currentPrice;
      closePosition(id, price);
    }
  });
}

export function resetPortfolio(): void {
  tradingState = {
    balance: INITIAL_BALANCE,
    equity: INITIAL_BALANCE,
    positions: [],
    trades: [],
    peakEquity: INITIAL_BALANCE,
    maxDrawdown: 0,
    startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  };
}

export function getBrokerConfig(): BrokerConfig {
  return BROKER;
}

import type { PolymarketData } from "@shared/schema";

const GAMMA_API_URL = "https://gamma-api.polymarket.com";

let cachedMarkets: PolymarketData[] = [];
let lastFetch: number = 0;
const CACHE_DURATION = 120000;

const CRYPTO_KEYWORDS = [
  "bitcoin", "btc", "ethereum", "eth", "solana", "sol", 
  "crypto", "cryptocurrency", "xrp", "dogecoin", "doge",
  "binance", "coinbase", "cardano", "ada", "ripple", "bnb", 
  "avalanche", "avax", "polygon", "matic", "chainlink", "link", 
  "uniswap", "uni", "stablecoin", "usdt", "usdc", "defi", "nft", 
  "web3", "altcoin", "memecoin", "shiba", "pepe", "floki",
  "tether", "litecoin", "ltc", "polkadot", "dot", "cosmos", "atom",
  "near", "fantom", "ftm", "arbitrum", "optimism", "layer 2",
  "blockchain", "token", "mining", "halving", "satoshi"
];

function isCryptoRelated(question: string, description: string): boolean {
  const text = `${question} ${description}`.toLowerCase();
  return CRYPTO_KEYWORDS.some(keyword => {
    const regex = new RegExp(`\\b${keyword}\\b`, 'i');
    return regex.test(text);
  });
}

export async function getCryptoMarkets(): Promise<PolymarketData[]> {
  const now = Date.now();
  
  if (cachedMarkets.length > 0 && now - lastFetch < CACHE_DURATION) {
    console.log("[Polymarket] Returning cached data");
    return cachedMarkets;
  }

  try {
    console.log("[Polymarket] Fetching crypto prediction markets...");
    
    const response = await fetch(
      `${GAMMA_API_URL}/markets?closed=false&limit=200&order=volume&ascending=false`,
      {
        headers: {
          "Accept": "application/json",
        },
      }
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      console.error("[Polymarket] API error:", response.status);
      return cachedMarkets;
    }

    const allMarkets = await response.json();
    console.log(`[Polymarket] Received ${allMarkets.length} total markets`);
    
    const cryptoMarkets: PolymarketData[] = allMarkets
      .filter((market: any) => {
        const question = market.question || "";
        const description = market.description || "";
        const isCrypto = isCryptoRelated(question, description);
        if (isCrypto) {
          console.log(`[Polymarket] Matched: ${question.slice(0, 50)}...`);
        }
        return isCrypto;
      })
      .slice(0, 10)
      .map((market: any) => {
        let outcomes: string[] = [];
        if (typeof market.outcomes === "string") {
          try {
            outcomes = JSON.parse(market.outcomes);
          } catch {
            outcomes = ["Yes", "No"];
          }
        } else if (Array.isArray(market.outcomes)) {
          outcomes = market.outcomes;
        } else {
          outcomes = ["Yes", "No"];
        }
        
        let prices: number[] = [];
        if (typeof market.outcomePrices === "string") {
          try {
            prices = JSON.parse(market.outcomePrices).map((p: string) => parseFloat(p));
          } catch {
            prices = [0.5, 0.5];
          }
        } else if (Array.isArray(market.outcomePrices)) {
          prices = market.outcomePrices.map((p: any) => parseFloat(p));
        } else {
          prices = [0.5, 0.5];
        }
        
        return {
          id: market.id,
          question: market.question,
          description: market.description?.slice(0, 200) || "",
          outcomes: outcomes.map((outcome: string, idx: number) => ({
            name: outcome,
            price: prices[idx] || 0.5,
          })),
          volume: parseFloat(market.volume) || 0,
          liquidity: parseFloat(market.liquidity) || 0,
          endDate: market.endDate,
          category: "crypto",
        };
      });

    if (cryptoMarkets.length === 0) {
      console.log("[Polymarket] No crypto markets found in API response");
    }
    
    cachedMarkets = cryptoMarkets;
    lastFetch = now;
    
    console.log(`[Polymarket] Found ${cryptoMarkets.length} crypto prediction markets`);
    return cryptoMarkets;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[Polymarket] Fetch error:", error);
    return cachedMarkets;
  }
}

export function getCachedPolymarkets(): PolymarketData[] {
  return cachedMarkets;
}
