import type { CryptoData, SentimentData, WhaleTransaction, DivergenceSignal, CorrelationData } from "@shared/schema";

export function detectDivergences(
  cryptos: CryptoData[],
  btcSentiment: SentimentData | null
): DivergenceSignal[] {
  const signals: DivergenceSignal[] = [];
  const sentimentScore = btcSentiment?.score || 0;
  
  for (const crypto of cryptos) {
    const change24h = crypto.price_change_percentage_24h || 0;
    const change7d = crypto.price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency || 0;
    
    const momentum24h = change24h > 0 ? 1 : change24h < 0 ? -1 : 0;
    const momentum7d = change7d > 0 ? 1 : change7d < 0 ? -1 : 0;
    
    const dailyAvg7d = change7d / 7;
    const deviation = change24h - dailyAvg7d;
    
    let signal: DivergenceSignal | null = null;
    
    if (change24h <= -3 && change7d > 0) {
      signal = {
        coin: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
        coinName: crypto.name,
        type: "bullish",
        priceChange24h: change24h,
        sentimentScore: sentimentScore,
        strength: Math.min(1, Math.abs(change24h) / 8),
        description: `24h dip (${change24h.toFixed(1)}%) vs 7d gain (${change7d.toFixed(1)}%) - potential bounce`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
    }
    else if (change24h >= 3 && change7d < -2) {
      signal = {
        coin: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
        coinName: crypto.name,
        type: "bearish",
        priceChange24h: change24h,
        sentimentScore: sentimentScore,
        strength: Math.min(1, Math.abs(change24h) / 8),
        description: `24h pump (${change24h.toFixed(1)}%) vs 7d loss (${change7d.toFixed(1)}%) - dead cat bounce?`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
    }
    else if (Math.abs(deviation) > 2) {
      const isOutperforming = deviation > 0;
      signal = {
        coin: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
        coinName: crypto.name,
        type: isOutperforming ? "bullish" : "bearish",
        priceChange24h: change24h,
        sentimentScore: sentimentScore,
        strength: Math.min(1, Math.abs(deviation) / 5),
        description: isOutperforming
          ? `Outperforming 7d avg by ${deviation.toFixed(1)}% today`
          : `Underperforming 7d avg by ${Math.abs(deviation).toFixed(1)}% today`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
    }
    else if (momentum24h !== momentum7d && Math.abs(change24h) >= 1.5) {
      const isBullish = momentum24h > momentum7d;
      signal = {
        coin: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
        coinName: crypto.name,
        type: isBullish ? "bullish" : "bearish",
        priceChange24h: change24h,
        sentimentScore: sentimentScore,
        strength: Math.min(1, Math.abs(change24h) / 6),
        description: isBullish
          ? `Momentum shift: recovering from 7d downtrend`
          : `Momentum shift: weakening after 7d uptrend`,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
    }
    
    if (signal) {
      signals.push(signal);
    }
  }
  
  if (signals.length === 0 && cryptos.length > 0) {
    const volatile = [...cryptos]
      .sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.price_change_percentage_24h || 0) - Math.abs(a.price_change_percentage_24h || 0))
      .slice(0, 2);
    
    for (const crypto of volatile) {
      const change = crypto.price_change_percentage_24h || 0;
      if (Math.abs(change) >= 0.5) {
        signals.push({
          coin: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
          coinName: crypto.name,
          type: change > 0 ? "bullish" : "bearish",
          priceChange24h: change,
          sentimentScore: sentimentScore,
          strength: Math.min(1, Math.abs(change) / 5),
          description: `24h: ${change > 0 ? '+' : ''}${change.toFixed(1)}% | 7d: ${(crypto.price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency || 0).toFixed(1)}%`,
          timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
        });
      }
    }
  }
  
  return signals.sort((a, b) => b.strength - a.strength).slice(0, 5);
}

export function calculateWhaleNetFlow(
  transactions: WhaleTransaction[],
  cryptos: CryptoData[] = []
): {
  netFlow: number;
  bullishness: number;
  inflow: number;
  outflow: number;
  largestTransaction: WhaleTransaction | null;
  volumeDelta: number;
  interpretation: string;
} {
  if (transactions.length > 0) {
    let inflow = 0;
    let outflow = 0;
    let largest: WhaleTransaction | null = null;
    
    for (const tx of transactions) {
      const isExchange = tx.to.includes("exchange") || 
                         tx.to.length < 20 ||
                         tx.type === "swap";
      
      if (isExchange) {
        outflow += tx.amountUsd || 0;
      } else {
        inflow += tx.amountUsd || 0;
      }
      
      if (!largest || (tx.amountUsd || 0) > (largest.amountUsd || 0)) {
        largest = tx;
      }
    }
    
    const netFlow = inflow - outflow;
    const total = inflow + outflow;
    const bullishness = total > 0 ? 0.5 + (netFlow / total) * 0.5 : 0.5;
    
    return {
      netFlow,
      bullishness: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, bullishness)),
      inflow,
      outflow,
      largestTransaction: largest,
      volumeDelta: 0,
      interpretation: netFlow > 0 ? "Accumulation detected" : "Distribution detected",
    };
  }
  
  if (cryptos.length > 0) {
    const btc = cryptos.find(c => c.symbol === "btc");
    const eth = cryptos.find(c => c.symbol === "eth");
    const sol = cryptos.find(c => c.symbol === "sol");
    
    let volumeScore = 0;
    let priceScore = 0;
    let count = 0;
    
    for (const crypto of [btc, eth, sol].filter(Boolean)) {
      if (!crypto) continue;
      
      const avgDailyVol = crypto.market_cap * 0.03;
      const volRatio = crypto.total_volume / avgDailyVol;
      volumeScore += volRatio > 1.2 ? 1 : volRatio < 0.8 ? -1 : 0;
      
      const priceChange = crypto.price_change_percentage_24h || 0;
      priceScore += priceChange > 2 ? 1 : priceChange < -2 ? -1 : 0;
      count++;
    }
    
    if (count > 0) {
      volumeScore /= count;
      priceScore /= count;
    }
    
    const combinedScore = (volumeScore + priceScore) / 2;
    const bullishness = 0.5 + combinedScore * 0.3;
    
    let interpretation = "Market neutral";
    if (combinedScore > 0.3) interpretation = "High volume + rising prices: Smart money buying";
    else if (combinedScore < -0.3) interpretation = "High volume + falling prices: Distribution likely";
    else if (volumeScore > 0.5) interpretation = "Volume spike detected - watch for breakout";
    else if (volumeScore < -0.5) interpretation = "Low volume - consolidation phase";
    
    const volumeDelta = ((volumeScore - 1) * 100);
    
    return {
      netFlow: combinedScore * 1000000,
      bullishness: Math.max(0.1, Math.min(0.9, bullishness)),
      inflow: Math.max(0, combinedScore) * 500000,
      outflow: Math.max(0, -combinedScore) * 500000,
      largestTransaction: null,
      volumeDelta,
      interpretation,
    };
  }
  
  return {
    netFlow: 0,
    bullishness: 0.5,
    inflow: 0,
    outflow: 0,
    largestTransaction: null,
    volumeDelta: 0,
    interpretation: "No data available",
  };
}

export function calculateCorrelations(
  cryptos: CryptoData[]
): CorrelationData[] {
  const correlations: CorrelationData[] = [];
  
  const btc = cryptos.find((c) => c.symbol === "btc");
  if (!btc || !btc.sparkline_in_7d?.price) return correlations;
  
  const btcPrices = btc.sparkline_in_7d.price;
  
  for (const crypto of cryptos) {
    if (crypto.symbol === "btc") continue;
    if (!crypto.sparkline_in_7d?.price) continue;
    
    const prices = crypto.sparkline_in_7d.price;
    const minLen = Math.min(btcPrices.length, prices.length);
    
    if (minLen < 10) continue;
    
    const btcReturns: number[] = [];
    const coinReturns: number[] = [];
    
    for (let i = 1; i < minLen; i++) {
      btcReturns.push((btcPrices[i] - btcPrices[i - 1]) / btcPrices[i - 1]);
      coinReturns.push((prices[i] - prices[i - 1]) / prices[i - 1]);
    }
    
    const correlation = pearsonCorrelation(btcReturns, coinReturns);
    
    let trend: "strengthening" | "weakening" | "stable" = "stable";
    let correlation24h = correlation;
    let correlationDelta24h = 0;
    
    // Calculate 24h correlation and delta
    // Sparkline has ~168 data points (hourly for 7 days)
    // Last 24 points = last 24 hours
    if (minLen >= 48) {
      // Recent 24h correlation
      const recent24hBtc = btcReturns.slice(-24);
      const recent24hCoin = coinReturns.slice(-24);
      correlation24h = pearsonCorrelation(recent24hBtc, recent24hCoin);
      
      // Previous 24h correlation (24-48 hours ago)
      const prev24hBtc = btcReturns.slice(-48, -24);
      const prev24hCoin = coinReturns.slice(-48, -24);
      const prevCorrelation = pearsonCorrelation(prev24hBtc, prev24hCoin);
      
      // Delta = change in correlation over 24 hours
      correlationDelta24h = correlation24h - prevCorrelation;
      
      // Trend based on correlation change
      if (correlationDelta24h > 0.1) trend = "strengthening";
      else if (correlationDelta24h < -0.1) trend = "weakening";
    }
    
    correlations.push({
      coin: crypto.symbol.toUpperCase(),
      coinName: crypto.name,
      correlationWithBTC: correlation,
      correlation24h,
      correlationDelta24h,
      trend,
      strength: Math.abs(correlation) > 0.7 ? "strong" : Math.abs(correlation) > 0.4 ? "moderate" : "weak",
    });
  }
  
  return correlations.sort((a, b) => Math.abs(b.correlationWithBTC) - Math.abs(a.correlationWithBTC));
}

function pearsonCorrelation(x: number[], y: number[]): number {
  const n = x.length;
  if (n === 0) return 0;
  
  const sumX = x.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const sumY = y.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const sumXY = x.reduce((acc, xi, i) => acc + xi * y[i], 0);
  const sumX2 = x.reduce((a, b) => a + b * b, 0);
  const sumY2 = y.reduce((a, b) => a + b * b, 0);
  
  const numerator = n * sumXY - sumX * sumY;
  const denominator = Math.sqrt((n * sumX2 - sumX * sumX) * (n * sumY2 - sumY * sumY));
  
  if (denominator === 0) return 0;
  return numerator / denominator;
}

import { getMultiple4HCandles, type OHLCCandle } from "./coingecko";

function getCryptoSymbol(coinId: string): string {

export interface BacktestTrade {
  strategy: 'meanReversion' | 'volExpansion' | 'pairsTrading';
  coinId: string;
  side: 'long' | 'short';
  entryPrice: number;
  exitPrice: number;
  entryTime: number;
  exitTime: number;
  pnlPercent: number;
  rMultiple: number;
}

export interface StrategyMike1 {
  meanReversion: number;
  volExpansion: number;
  pairsTrading: number;
}

interface BacktestResult {
  trades: BacktestTrade[];
  sharpeRatio: number;
  ulcerIndex: number;
  mike1Score: number;
}

let warmupMike1Scores: StrategyMike1 | null = null;
let warmupComplete = false;
let warmupTrades: BacktestTrade[] = [];
let cached4HOHLCData: Map<string, OHLCCandle[]> = new Map();

export function calculate4HATR(candles: OHLCCandle[], period: number = 14): number {
  if (candles.length < period + 1) return 0;
  
  let atrSum = 0;
  for (let i = candles.length - period; i < candles.length; i++) {
    const high = candles[i].high;
    const low = candles[i].low;
    const prevClose = candles[i - 1]?.close || candles[i].open;
    
    const tr = Math.max(
      high - low,
      Math.abs(high - prevClose),
      Math.abs(low - prevClose)
    );
    atrSum += tr;
  }
  
  return atrSum / period;
}

export function calculate4HBollingerBands(candles: OHLCCandle[], period: number = 20, stdDev: number = 2): {
  upper: number;
  middle: number;
  lower: number;
  zScore: number;
} | null {
  if (candles.length < period) return null;
  
  const closes = candles.slice(-period).map(c => c.close);
  const sum = closes.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const mean = sum / period;
  
  const squaredDiffs = closes.map(c => Math.pow(c - mean, 2));
  const variance = squaredDiffs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / period;
  const std = Math.sqrt(variance);
  
  const currentPrice = candles[candles.length - 1].close;
  const zScore = std > 0 ? (currentPrice - mean) / std : 0;
  
  return {
    upper: mean + (stdDev * std),
    middle: mean,
    lower: mean - (stdDev * std),
    zScore,
  };
}

export function calculate4HKeltnerChannel(candles: OHLCCandle[], emaPeriod: number = 20, atrPeriod: number = 10, multiplier: number = 2): {
  upper: number;
  middle: number;
  lower: number;
} | null {
  if (candles.length < Math.max(emaPeriod, atrPeriod + 1)) return null;
  
  const closes = candles.slice(-emaPeriod).map(c => c.close);
  const ema = closes.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / emaPeriod;
  
  const atr = calculate4HATR(candles, atrPeriod);
  
  return {
    upper: ema + (multiplier * atr),
    middle: ema,
    lower: ema - (multiplier * atr),
  };
}

// Calculate ADX (Average Directional Index) from 4H OHLC candles
export function calculate4HADX(candles: OHLCCandle[], period: number = 14): number {
  if (candles.length < period + 1) return 25; // Default to neutral if not enough data
  
  const recentCandles = candles.slice(-period - 1);
  
  let plusDMSum = 0;
  let minusDMSum = 0;
  let trSum = 0;
  
  for (let i = 1; i < recentCandles.length; i++) {
    const current = recentCandles[i];
    const prev = recentCandles[i - 1];
    
    const highDiff = current.high - prev.high;
    const lowDiff = prev.low - current.low;
    
    // +DM and -DM
    const plusDM = highDiff > lowDiff && highDiff > 0 ? highDiff : 0;
    const minusDM = lowDiff > highDiff && lowDiff > 0 ? lowDiff : 0;
    
    // True Range
    const tr = Math.max(
      current.high - current.low,
      Math.abs(current.high - prev.close),
      Math.abs(current.low - prev.close)
    );
    
    plusDMSum += plusDM;
    minusDMSum += minusDM;
    trSum += tr;
  }
  
  if (trSum === 0) return 25;
  
  const plusDI = (plusDMSum / trSum) * 100;
  const minusDI = (minusDMSum / trSum) * 100;
  
  const diSum = plusDI + minusDI;
  if (diSum === 0) return 25;
  
  const dx = Math.abs(plusDI - minusDI) / diSum * 100;
  
  return dx; // Simplified ADX (single period DX)
}

function backtestMeanReversion(candles: OHLCCandle[], coinId: string): BacktestTrade[] {
  const trades: BacktestTrade[] = [];
  if (candles.length < 30) return trades;
  
  let position: { side: 'long' | 'short'; entryPrice: number; entryTime: number; stopLoss: number; takeProfit: number } | null = null;
  
  for (let i = 25; i < candles.length - 1; i++) {
    const histCandles = candles.slice(0, i + 1);
    const bb = calculate4HBollingerBands(histCandles, 20, 2);
    if (!bb) continue;
    
    const currentCandle = candles[i];
    const nextCandle = candles[i + 1];
    const atr = calculate4HATR(histCandles, 14);
    
    if (!position) {
      // "Falling Knife Protocol" per replit.md:
      // - Normal entry: ADX < 30 AND |Z-Score| > 1.5 (extended price in ranging market)
      // - Panic crash bypass: |Z-Score| > 3.5 ignores ADX filter entirely
      const adx = calculate4HADX(histCandles, 14);
      const absZScore = Math.abs(bb.zScore);
      const isPanic = absZScore > 3.5;
      const isRangingMarket = adx < 30;
      const isExtendedPrice = absZScore > 1.5;
      const canEnter = isPanic || (isRangingMarket && isExtendedPrice);
      
      if (canEnter) {
        if (bb.zScore > 1.5) {
          const entryPrice = currentCandle.close;
          const stopLoss = entryPrice * 1.01;
          const takeProfit = entryPrice * 0.985;
          position = { side: 'short', entryPrice, entryTime: currentCandle.timestamp, stopLoss, takeProfit };
        } else if (bb.zScore < -1.5) {
          const entryPrice = currentCandle.close;
          const stopLoss = entryPrice * 0.99;
          const takeProfit = entryPrice * 1.015;
          position = { side: 'long', entryPrice, entryTime: currentCandle.timestamp, stopLoss, takeProfit };
        }
      }
    } else {
      let exitPrice: number | null = null;
      
      if (position.side === 'long') {
        if (nextCandle.low <= position.stopLoss) {
          exitPrice = position.stopLoss;
        } else if (nextCandle.high >= position.takeProfit) {
          exitPrice = position.takeProfit;
        } else if (bb.zScore > 0) {
          exitPrice = nextCandle.close;
        }
      } else {
        if (nextCandle.high >= position.stopLoss) {
          exitPrice = position.stopLoss;
        } else if (nextCandle.low <= position.takeProfit) {
          exitPrice = position.takeProfit;
        } else if (bb.zScore < 0) {
          exitPrice = nextCandle.close;
        }
      }
      
      if (exitPrice !== null) {
        const pnlPercent = position.side === 'long' 
          ? ((exitPrice - position.entryPrice) / position.entryPrice) * 100
          : ((position.entryPrice - exitPrice) / position.entryPrice) * 100;
        
        trades.push({
          strategy: 'meanReversion',
          coinId,
          side: position.side,
          entryPrice: position.entryPrice,
          exitPrice,
          entryTime: position.entryTime,
          exitTime: nextCandle.timestamp,
          pnlPercent,
          rMultiple: pnlPercent / 1.0,
        });
        position = null;
      }
    }
  }
  
  return trades;
}

function backtestVolExpansion(candles: OHLCCandle[], coinId: string): BacktestTrade[] {
  const trades: BacktestTrade[] = [];
  if (candles.length < 30) return trades;
  
  let position: { side: 'long' | 'short'; entryPrice: number; entryTime: number; stopLoss: number; takeProfit: number } | null = null;
  
  for (let i = 25; i < candles.length - 1; i++) {
    const histCandles = candles.slice(0, i + 1);
    const keltner = calculate4HKeltnerChannel(histCandles, 20, 10, 2);
    if (!keltner) continue;
    
    const currentCandle = candles[i];
    const nextCandle = candles[i + 1];
    const atr = calculate4HATR(histCandles, 14);
    
    if (!position) {
      if (currentCandle.close > keltner.upper) {
        const entryPrice = currentCandle.close;
        const stopLoss = entryPrice * 0.99;
        const takeProfit = entryPrice * 1.015;
        position = { side: 'long', entryPrice, entryTime: currentCandle.timestamp, stopLoss, takeProfit };
      } else if (currentCandle.close < keltner.lower) {
        const entryPrice = currentCandle.close;
        const stopLoss = entryPrice * 1.01;
        const takeProfit = entryPrice * 0.985;
        position = { side: 'short', entryPrice, entryTime: currentCandle.timestamp, stopLoss, takeProfit };
      }
    } else {
      let exitPrice: number | null = null;
      
      if (position.side === 'long') {
        if (nextCandle.low <= position.stopLoss) {
          exitPrice = position.stopLoss;
        } else if (nextCandle.high >= position.takeProfit) {
          exitPrice = position.takeProfit;
        }
      } else {
        if (nextCandle.high >= position.stopLoss) {
          exitPrice = position.stopLoss;
        } else if (nextCandle.low <= position.takeProfit) {
          exitPrice = position.takeProfit;
        }
      }
      
      if (exitPrice !== null) {
        const pnlPercent = position.side === 'long' 
          ? ((exitPrice - position.entryPrice) / position.entryPrice) * 100
          : ((position.entryPrice - exitPrice) / position.entryPrice) * 100;
        
        trades.push({
          strategy: 'volExpansion',
          coinId,
          side: position.side,
          entryPrice: position.entryPrice,
          exitPrice,
          entryTime: position.entryTime,
          exitTime: nextCandle.timestamp,
          pnlPercent,
          rMultiple: pnlPercent / 1.0,
        });
        position = null;
      }
    }
  }
  
  return trades;
}

function backtestPairsTrading(btcCandles: OHLCCandle[], ethCandles: OHLCCandle[]): BacktestTrade[] {
  const trades: BacktestTrade[] = [];
  if (btcCandles.length < 50 || ethCandles.length < 50) return trades;
  
  const minLen = Math.min(btcCandles.length, ethCandles.length);
  const btc = btcCandles.slice(-minLen);
  const eth = ethCandles.slice(-minLen);
  
  let position: { entryTime: number; btcEntry: number; ethEntry: number; spreadEntry: number } | null = null;
  
  for (let i = 30; i < minLen - 1; i++) {
    const btcPrices = btc.slice(0, i + 1).map(c => c.close);
    const ethPrices = eth.slice(0, i + 1).map(c => c.close);
    
    const spreads: number[] = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < btcPrices.length; j++) {
      spreads.push(ethPrices[j] / btcPrices[j]);
    }
    
    const mean = spreads.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / spreads.length;
    const std = Math.sqrt(spreads.map(s => Math.pow(s - mean, 2)).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / spreads.length);
    const currentSpread = spreads[spreads.length - 1];
    const zScore = std > 0 ? (currentSpread - mean) / std : 0;
    
    if (!position) {
      if (Math.abs(zScore) > 2.0) {
        position = {
          entryTime: btc[i].timestamp,
          btcEntry: btc[i].close,
          ethEntry: eth[i].close,
          spreadEntry: currentSpread,
        };
      }
    } else {
      if (Math.abs(zScore) < 0.5) {
        const spreadExit = eth[i + 1].close / btc[i + 1].close;
        const pnlPercent = ((spreadExit - position.spreadEntry) / position.spreadEntry) * 100;
        
        trades.push({
          strategy: 'pairsTrading',
          coinId: 'ETH/BTC',
          side: position.spreadEntry > eth[i + 1].close / btc[i + 1].close ? 'short' : 'long',
          entryPrice: position.spreadEntry,
          exitPrice: spreadExit,
          entryTime: position.entryTime,
          exitTime: btc[i + 1].timestamp,
          pnlPercent: Math.abs(pnlPercent),
          rMultiple: Math.abs(pnlPercent) / 1.0,
        });
        position = null;
      }
    }
  }
  
  return trades;
}

interface StrategyMetrics {
  sharpe: number;
  ulcer: number;
  mike1: number;
  trades: number;
  wins: number;
}

function calculateStrategyMetrics(trades: BacktestTrade[]): StrategyMetrics {
  if (trades.length < 10) return { sharpe: 0, ulcer: 0, mike1: 0, trades: trades.length, wins: 0 };
  
  const returns = trades.map(t => t.pnlPercent / 100);
  const mean = returns.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / returns.length;
  const variance = returns.map(r => Math.pow(r - mean, 2)).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / returns.length;
  const std = Math.sqrt(variance);
  const sharpeRatio = std > 0 ? (mean * Math.sqrt(252 * 6)) / std : 0;
  
  let cumulative = 0;
  let peak = 0;
  const drawdowns: number[] = [];
  
  for (const ret of returns) {
    cumulative += ret;
    if (cumulative > peak) peak = cumulative;
    const dd = peak - cumulative;
    drawdowns.push(dd);
  }
  
  const squaredDrawdowns = drawdowns.map(d => d * d);
  const ulcerIndex = Math.sqrt(squaredDrawdowns.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / squaredDrawdowns.length);
  
  const mike1 = sharpeRatio / (ulcerIndex + 0.001);
  const wins = trades.filter(t => t.pnlPercent > 0).length;
  
  return { sharpe: sharpeRatio, ulcer: ulcerIndex, mike1, trades: trades.length, wins };
}

function calculateMike1(trades: BacktestTrade[]): number {
  return calculateStrategyMetrics(trades).mike1;
}

let warmupDetailedMetrics: {
  meanReversion: StrategyMetrics;
  volExpansion: StrategyMetrics;
  pairsTrading: StrategyMetrics;
} | null = null;

export async function runSyntheticWarmup(): Promise<StrategyMike1> {
  console.log('[Synthetic Warmup] Starting 30-day backtest for Mike1 calculation...');
  
  // Use shared source of truth for top 15 tradable cryptos
  const { getTop12TradableCryptos } = await import('./coingecko');
  const tradableCryptos = await getTop12TradableCryptos();
  const topCoins = tradableCryptos.map(c => c.id);
  
  console.log(`[Synthetic Warmup] Top ${topCoins.length} by volume: ${topCoins.join(', ')}`);
  
  // Use CoinGecko with 30-day range (Demo tier provides 4H candles for 3-30 day ranges)
  // 90 days only gives daily candles on Demo tier
  const allCandles = await getMultiple4HCandles(topCoins, 30);
  
  const meanRevTrades: BacktestTrade[] = [];
  const volExpTrades: BacktestTrade[] = [];
  const pairsTrades: BacktestTrade[] = [];
  
  for (const coinId of topCoins) {
    const candles = allCandles.get(coinId);
    if (!candles || candles.length < 30) {
      console.log(`[Synthetic Warmup] Skipping ${coinId} - insufficient data (${candles?.length || 0} candles)`);
      continue;
    }
    
    console.log(`[Synthetic Warmup] Backtesting ${coinId} (${candles.length} candles)...`);
    
    const mrTrades = backtestMeanReversion(candles, coinId);
    meanRevTrades.push(...mrTrades);
    
    const veTrades = backtestVolExpansion(candles, coinId);
    volExpTrades.push(...veTrades);
  }
  
  const btcCandles = allCandles.get('bitcoin') || [];
  const ethCandles = allCandles.get('ethereum') || [];
  if (btcCandles.length >= 50 && ethCandles.length >= 50) {
    const ptTrades = backtestPairsTrading(btcCandles, ethCandles);
    pairsTrades.push(...ptTrades);
  }
  
  const meanRevMetrics = calculateStrategyMetrics(meanRevTrades);
  const volExpMetrics = calculateStrategyMetrics(volExpTrades);
  const pairsMetrics = calculateStrategyMetrics(pairsTrades);
  
  warmupDetailedMetrics = {
    meanReversion: meanRevMetrics,
    volExpansion: volExpMetrics,
    pairsTrading: pairsMetrics,
  };
  
  warmupMike1Scores = {
    meanReversion: meanRevMetrics.mike1,
    volExpansion: volExpMetrics.mike1,
    pairsTrading: pairsMetrics.mike1,
  };
  
  warmupTrades = [...meanRevTrades, ...volExpTrades, ...pairsTrades];
  cached4HOHLCData = allCandles; // Store for live signal generation
  warmupComplete = true;
  
  console.log('[Synthetic Warmup] Complete!');
  console.log(`  Mean Reversion: ${meanRevTrades.length} trades, Mike1=${meanRevMetrics.mike1.toFixed(3)}`);
  console.log(`  Vol Expansion: ${volExpTrades.length} trades, Mike1=${volExpMetrics.mike1.toFixed(3)}`);
  console.log(`  Pairs Trading: ${pairsTrades.length} trades, Mike1=${pairsMetrics.mike1.toFixed(3)}`);
  
  const bestStrategy = Object.entries(warmupMike1Scores)
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0];
  console.log(`  Active Strategy: ${bestStrategy[0]} (Mike1=${bestStrategy[1].toFixed(3)})`);
  
  return warmupMike1Scores;
}

export function getWarmupMike1Scores(): StrategyMike1 | null {
  return warmupMike1Scores;
}

export function isWarmupComplete(): boolean {
  return warmupComplete;
}

export function getWarmupTrades(): BacktestTrade[] {
  return warmupTrades;
}

export function getWarmupDetailedMetrics() {
  return warmupDetailedMetrics;
}

export function getBestStrategy(): 'meanReversion' | 'volExpansion' | 'pairsTrading' | null {
  if (!warmupMike1Scores) return null;
  
  const entries = Object.entries(warmupMike1Scores) as [keyof StrategyMike1, number][];
  const sorted = entries.sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  
  if (sorted[0][1] <= 0) return null;
  
  return sorted[0][0];
}

export function getStrategyAllocation(): { primary: string; primaryWeight: number; secondary: string | null; secondaryWeight: number } {
  if (!warmupMike1Scores) {
    return { primary: 'volExpansion', primaryWeight: 0.7, secondary: 'meanReversion', secondaryWeight: 0.3 };
  }
  
  // DYNAMIC 70/30 ALLOCATION - Best Mike1 score gets 70%, second-best gets 30%
  // Use live Mike1 if enough trades (>= 5), otherwise use warmup scores
  const modePerf = getModePerformance();
  const modes = ['meanReversion', 'volExpansion', 'pairsTrading'] as const;
  
  const scored = modes.map(mode => {
    const perf = modePerf[mode];
    if (perf.trades >= 5) {
      const returns = perf.returns;
      const drawdowns = perf.drawdowns;
      const avgReturn = returns.length > 0 ? returns.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / returns.length : 0;
      const variance = returns.length > 1 ? returns.reduce((sum, r) => sum + Math.pow(r - avgReturn, 2), 0) / returns.length : 0;
      const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
      const liveSharpe = stdDev > 0.001 ? avgReturn / stdDev : 0;
      const sumSqDD = drawdowns.length > 0 ? drawdowns.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d * d, 0) : 0;
      const liveUlcer = drawdowns.length > 0 ? Math.sqrt(sumSqDD / drawdowns.length) : 0;
      const liveMike1 = liveUlcer > 0.001 ? liveSharpe / liveUlcer : (liveSharpe > 0 ? liveSharpe * 10 : 0);
      return { mode, score: liveMike1 };
    }
    return { mode, score: warmupMike1Scores![mode] };
  }).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
  
  const bestMode = scored[0].mode;
  const secondMode = scored[1].mode;
  
  return { 
    primary: bestMode, 
    primaryWeight: 0.7, 
    secondary: secondMode, 
    secondaryWeight: 0.3 
  };
}

// LIVE SIGNAL GENERATION USING 4H OHLC DATA
// These functions mirror the backtest logic exactly to ensure consistency

export interface Live4HSignal {
  coinId: string;
  symbol: string;
  side: 'long' | 'short';
  entryPrice: number;
  stopLoss: number;
  takeProfit: number;
  strategy: 'meanReversion' | 'volExpansion';
  reasoning: string[];
  confidence: number;
  zScore?: number;
  atr?: number;
}

export function generateLiveMeanReversionSignal(coinId: string, currentPrice: number): Live4HSignal | null {
  const candles = cached4HOHLCData.get(coinId);
  if (!candles || candles.length < 30) {
    return null;
  }
  
  const bb = calculate4HBollingerBands(candles, 20, 2);
  if (!bb) return null;
  
  const atr = calculate4HATR(candles, 14);
  const adx = calculate4HADX(candles, 14);
  const absZScore = Math.abs(bb.zScore);
  
  // "Falling Knife Protocol":
  // - Normal entry: ADX < 30 AND |Z-Score| > 1.5 (extended price in ranging market)
  // - Panic crash bypass: |Z-Score| > 3.5 ignores ADX filter entirely
  const isPanic = absZScore > 3.5;
  const isRangingMarket = adx < 30;
  const isExtendedPrice = absZScore > 1.5;
  
  // Entry gate check
  const canEnter = isPanic || (isRangingMarket && isExtendedPrice);
  
  if (!canEnter) {
    // Log why we're not entering
    if (!isExtendedPrice) {
      console.log(`[LIVE 4H GATE] ${getCryptoSymbol(coinId)}: Z-Score ${absZScore.toFixed(2)} < 1.5 (need extended price)`);
    } else if (!isRangingMarket) {
      console.log(`[LIVE 4H GATE] ${getCryptoSymbol(coinId)}: ADX ${adx.toFixed(1)} > 30 (trending), Z=${absZScore.toFixed(2)} (not panic)`);
    }
    return null;
  }
  
  if (bb.zScore < -1.5) {
    // Price below mean - expect bounce (LONG)
    const stopLoss = currentPrice * 0.985; // 1.5% stop
    const takeProfit = currentPrice * 1.015; // 1.5% take profit (1:1 R:R)
    
    console.log(`[LIVE 4H] ${getCryptoSymbol(coinId)}: Mean Rev LONG signal Z=${bb.zScore.toFixed(2)} ADX=${adx.toFixed(1)} ${isPanic ? '(PANIC CRASH)' : ''}`);
    
    return {
      coinId,
      symbol: getCryptoSymbol(coinId),
      side: 'long',
      entryPrice: currentPrice,
      stopLoss,
      takeProfit,
      strategy: 'meanReversion',
      reasoning: [
        `[4H OHLC] Mean Reversion LONG`,
        `Z-Score: ${bb.zScore.toFixed(2)} (< -1.5 threshold)`,
        `ADX: ${adx.toFixed(1)} ${isPanic ? '(PANIC BYPASS)' : isRangingMarket ? '(ranging)' : ''}`,
        isPanic ? `PANIC CRASH: |Z| > 3.5 ignores ADX` : `Extended below mean in ranging market`,
        `4H ATR: $${atr.toFixed(2)}`,
      ],
      confidence: Math.min(0.9, 0.5 + Math.abs(bb.zScore) * 0.1),
      zScore: bb.zScore,
      atr,
    };
  } else if (bb.zScore > 1.5) {
    // Price above mean - expect reversion (SHORT)
    const stopLoss = currentPrice * 1.015;
    const takeProfit = currentPrice * 0.985;
    
    console.log(`[LIVE 4H] ${getCryptoSymbol(coinId)}: Mean Rev SHORT signal Z=${bb.zScore.toFixed(2)} ADX=${adx.toFixed(1)} ${isPanic ? '(PANIC)' : ''}`);
    
    return {
      coinId,
      symbol: getCryptoSymbol(coinId),
      side: 'short',
      entryPrice: currentPrice,
      stopLoss,
      takeProfit,
      strategy: 'meanReversion',
      reasoning: [
        `[4H OHLC] Mean Reversion SHORT`,
        `Z-Score: ${bb.zScore.toFixed(2)} (> 1.5 threshold)`,
        `ADX: ${adx.toFixed(1)} ${isPanic ? '(PANIC BYPASS)' : isRangingMarket ? '(ranging)' : ''}`,
        isPanic ? `EXTREME EXTENSION: |Z| > 3.5 ignores ADX` : `Extended above mean in ranging market`,
        `4H ATR: $${atr.toFixed(2)}`,
      ],
      confidence: Math.min(0.9, 0.5 + Math.abs(bb.zScore) * 0.1),
      zScore: bb.zScore,
      atr,
    };
  }
  
  return null;
}

export function generateLiveVolExpansionSignal(coinId: string, currentPrice: number): Live4HSignal | null {
  const candles = cached4HOHLCData.get(coinId);
  if (!candles || candles.length < 30) {
    return null;
  }
  
  const keltner = calculate4HKeltnerChannel(candles, 20, 10, 2);
  if (!keltner) return null;
  
  const atr = calculate4HATR(candles, 14);
  
  // Check for Keltner breakout OR near-Keltner approach (within 0.3 ATR)
  const nearThreshold = atr * 0.3;
  const isAboveUpper = currentPrice > keltner.upper;
  const isNearUpper = !isAboveUpper && currentPrice > (keltner.upper - nearThreshold) && currentPrice > keltner.middle;
  const isBelowLower = currentPrice < keltner.lower;
  const isNearLower = !isBelowLower && currentPrice < (keltner.lower + nearThreshold) && currentPrice < keltner.middle;
  
  if (isAboveUpper || isNearUpper) {
    const stopLoss = currentPrice * 0.985;
    const takeProfit = currentPrice * 1.015;
    const signalType = isAboveUpper ? 'Keltner breakout UP' : 'Near-Keltner approach UP';
    const confidence = isAboveUpper ? 0.7 : 0.6;
    
    console.log(`[LIVE 4H] ${getCryptoSymbol(coinId)}: Vol Exp LONG signal - ${signalType}`);
    
    return {
      coinId,
      symbol: getCryptoSymbol(coinId),
      side: 'long',
      entryPrice: currentPrice,
      stopLoss,
      takeProfit,
      strategy: 'volExpansion',
      reasoning: [
        `[4H OHLC] Vol Expansion LONG`,
        isAboveUpper 
          ? `Keltner Breakout: Price $${currentPrice.toFixed(2)} > Upper $${keltner.upper.toFixed(2)}`
          : `Near-Keltner: Price $${currentPrice.toFixed(2)} approaching Upper $${keltner.upper.toFixed(2)} (within ${nearThreshold.toFixed(2)})`,
        `EMA(20): $${keltner.middle.toFixed(2)}`,
        `4H ATR: $${atr.toFixed(2)}`,
      ],
      confidence,
      atr,
    };
  } else if (isBelowLower || isNearLower) {
    const stopLoss = currentPrice * 1.015;
    const takeProfit = currentPrice * 0.985;
    const signalType = isBelowLower ? 'Keltner breakout DOWN' : 'Near-Keltner approach DOWN';
    const confidence = isBelowLower ? 0.7 : 0.6;
    
    console.log(`[LIVE 4H] ${getCryptoSymbol(coinId)}: Vol Exp SHORT signal - ${signalType}`);
    
    return {
      coinId,
      symbol: getCryptoSymbol(coinId),
      side: 'short',
      entryPrice: currentPrice,
      stopLoss,
      takeProfit,
      strategy: 'volExpansion',
      reasoning: [
        `[4H OHLC] Vol Expansion SHORT`,
        isBelowLower
          ? `Keltner Breakout: Price $${currentPrice.toFixed(2)} < Lower $${keltner.lower.toFixed(2)}`
          : `Near-Keltner: Price $${currentPrice.toFixed(2)} approaching Lower $${keltner.lower.toFixed(2)} (within ${nearThreshold.toFixed(2)})`,
        `EMA(20): $${keltner.middle.toFixed(2)}`,
        `4H ATR: $${atr.toFixed(2)}`,
      ],
      confidence,
      atr,
    };
  }
  
  return null;
}

// Get all live signals from cached 4H OHLC data
export function generateAllLiveSignals(cryptos: { id: string; current_price: number }[]): Live4HSignal[] {
  if (!warmupComplete || cached4HOHLCData.size === 0) {
    console.log('[LIVE 4H] Warmup not complete or no OHLC data cached');
    return [];
  }
  
  const signals: Live4HSignal[] = [];
  const allocation = getStrategyAllocation();
  
  for (const crypto of cryptos) {
    const coinId = crypto.id;
    const currentPrice = crypto.current_price;
    
    // Generate signals based on allocation
    if (allocation.primary === 'meanReversion' || allocation.secondary === 'meanReversion') {
      const mrSignal = generateLiveMeanReversionSignal(coinId, currentPrice);
      if (mrSignal) signals.push(mrSignal);
    }
    
    if (allocation.primary === 'volExpansion' || allocation.secondary === 'volExpansion') {
      const veSignal = generateLiveVolExpansionSignal(coinId, currentPrice);
      if (veSignal) signals.push(veSignal);
    }
  }
  
  console.log(`[LIVE 4H] Generated ${signals.length} signals from ${cryptos.length} cryptos`);
  return signals;
}

// Get cached OHLC data for a specific coin
export function getCached4HOHLC(coinId: string): OHLCCandle[] {
  return cached4HOHLCData.get(coinId) || [];
}

import { z } from "zod";

// Cryptocurrency data from CoinGecko
export const cryptoDataSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  symbol: z.string(),
  name: z.string(),
  image: z.string(),
  current_price: z.number(),
  market_cap: z.number(),
  market_cap_rank: z.number(),
  total_volume: z.number(),
  price_change_percentage_24h: z.number().nullable(),
  price_change_percentage_7d_in_currency: z.number().nullable().optional(),
  sparkline_in_7d: z.object({
    price: z.array(z.number())
  }).optional(),
  last_updated: z.string()
});

export type CryptoData = z.infer<typeof cryptoDataSchema>;

// Sentiment data from SentiCrypt
export const sentimentDataSchema = z.object({
  coin: z.string(),
  score: z.number(), // -1 to 1
  label: z.enum(["bearish", "neutral", "bullish"]),
  timestamp: z.string()
});

export type SentimentData = z.infer<typeof sentimentDataSchema>;

// Composite sentiment (calculated for non-BTC coins)
export const compositeSentimentSchema = z.object({
  coin: z.string(),
  priceScore: z.number(), // 40% weight
  volumeScore: z.number(), // 20% weight
  socialScore: z.number(), // 40% weight (BTC sentiment as proxy)
  compositeScore: z.number(), // -1 to 1
  label: z.enum(["bearish", "neutral", "bullish"]),
  timestamp: z.string()
});

export type CompositeSentiment = z.infer<typeof compositeSentimentSchema>;

// Whale transaction from Helius (Solana)
export const whaleTransactionSchema = z.object({
  signature: z.string(),
  timestamp: z.number(),
  type: z.enum(["transfer", "swap", "mint", "burn", "unknown"]),
  amount: z.number(),
  amountUsd: z.number().optional(),
  token: z.string(),
  tokenSymbol: z.string().optional(),
  from: z.string(),
  to: z.string(),
  isWhale: z.boolean()
});

export type WhaleTransaction = z.infer<typeof whaleTransactionSchema>;

// Dashboard state
export const dashboardStateSchema = z.object({
  cryptos: z.array(cryptoDataSchema),
  btcSentiment: sentimentDataSchema.nullable(),
  sentiments: z.array(compositeSentimentSchema),
  whaleTransactions: z.array(whaleTransactionSchema),
  lastUpdated: z.string(),
  isConnected: z.boolean()
});

export type DashboardState = z.infer<typeof dashboardStateSchema>;

// Signals data for WebSocket
export const signalsDataSchema = z.object({
  divergences: z.array(z.object({
    coin: z.string(),
    coinName: z.string(),
    type: z.enum(["bullish", "bearish"]),
    priceChange24h: z.number(),
    sentimentScore: z.number(),
    strength: z.number(),
    description: z.string(),
    timestamp: z.string(),
  })),
  whaleFlow: z.object({
    netFlow: z.number(),
    bullishness: z.number(),
    inflow: z.number(),
    outflow: z.number(),
    largestTransaction: whaleTransactionSchema.nullable(),
    volumeDelta: z.number().optional(),
    interpretation: z.string().optional(),
  }),
  correlations: z.array(z.object({
    coin: z.string(),
    coinName: z.string(),
    correlationWithBTC: z.number(),
    trend: z.enum(["strengthening", "weakening", "stable"]),
    strength: z.enum(["strong", "moderate", "weak"]),
  })),
});

export type SignalsData = z.infer<typeof signalsDataSchema>;

// WebSocket message types
export const wsMessageSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("crypto_update"),
    data: z.array(cryptoDataSchema)
  }),
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("sentiment_update"),
    data: sentimentDataSchema
  }),
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("whale_alert"),
    data: whaleTransactionSchema
  }),
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("signals_update"),
    data: signalsDataSchema
  }),
  z.object({
    type: z.literal("connection_status"),
    data: z.object({
      connected: z.boolean(),
      message: z.string()
    })
  })
]);

export type WSMessage = z.infer<typeof wsMessageSchema>;

// Polymarket prediction market data
export const polymarketDataSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  question: z.string(),
  description: z.string(),
  outcomes: z.array(z.object({
    name: z.string(),
    price: z.number()
  })),

export type PolymarketData = z.infer<typeof polymarketDataSchema>;

// API response wrapper
export const apiResponseSchema = <T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(dataSchema: T) =>
  z.object({
    success: z.boolean(),
    data: dataSchema.optional(),
    error: z.string().optional(),
    timestamp: z.string()
  });

// Trade record (executed order)
export const tradeRecordSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string(),
  symbol: z.string(),
  coinName: z.string(),
  side: positionSideSchema,
  orderType: orderTypeSchema,
  quantity: z.number(),
  entryPrice: z.number(),
  exitPrice: z.number().nullable(),
  stopLoss: z.number().nullable(),
  takeProfit: z.number().nullable(),
  pnl: z.number().nullable(),
  pnlPercent: z.number().nullable(),
  fees: z.number(),           // Total fees (entry + all exit fees)
  entryFees: z.number(),      // Entry fees only (for net PnL calculation)
  slippage: z.number(),
  status: z.enum(["open", "closed", "stopped_out"]),
  openedAt: z.string(),
  closedAt: z.string().nullable(),
  aiReasoning: z.string(),
  signalMode: z.enum(["volExpansion", "meanReversion", "pairsTrading", "adaptive"]).optional(),  // Strategy mode that generated this trade
  signals: z.object({
    sentiment: z.number().optional(),
    divergence: z.string().optional(),
    whaleFlow: z.string().optional(),
    correlation: z.number().optional(),
    mvrvZScore: z.number().optional(),
    capitalFlow: z.string().optional(),
  }).optional(),
});

  takeProfit: z.number().nullable(),
  takeProfitLevels: z.array(z.object({
    price: z.number(),
    quantityPercent: z.number(), // % of initial position to close at this level
    hitProbability: z.number(),  // estimated probability of hitting this level
    triggered: z.boolean(),       // whether this level has been hit
    atrMultiplier: z.number(),   // ATR multiplier used (for reference)
  })).optional(),
  unrealizedPnl: z.number(),
  unrealizedPnlPercent: z.number(),
  marginUsed: z.number(),
  leverage: z.number(),
  openedAt: z.string(),
  aiReasoning: z.string(),
  lastOrderUpdate: z.string().optional(), // ISO timestamp of last TP/SL update (max every 10 min)
});

export type Position = z.infer<typeof positionSchema>;

import { pgTable, serial, integer, text, timestamp } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import { createInsertSchema } from "drizzle-zod";
import { z } from "zod";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";

export const conversations = pgTable("conversations", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  title: text("title").notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").default(sql`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`).notNull(),
});

export const messages = pgTable("messages", {
  id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
  conversationId: integer("conversation_id").notNull().references(() => conversations.id, { onDelete: "cascade" }),
  role: text("role").notNull(),
  content: text("content").notNull(),
  createdAt: timestamp("created_at").default(sql`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP`).notNull(),
});

export const insertConversationSchema = createInsertSchema(conversations).omit({
  id: true,
  createdAt: true,
});

export const insertMessageSchema = createInsertSchema(messages).omit({
  id: true,
  createdAt: true,
});

export type Conversation = typeof conversations.$inferSelect;
export type InsertConversation = z.infer<typeof insertConversationSchema>;
export type Message = typeof messages.$inferSelect;
export type InsertMessage = z.infer<typeof insertMessageSchema>;


END OF FILE: ./shared/models/chat.ts
